### [1] [OSWAM: An Effective World Action Model with One-Step Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35850)
*Guowen Zhang,Liyi Chen,Xu Hu,Pengfei Wang,Jingfan Chen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSWAM 提出一种一步动作生成的世界动作模型，通过想象 token 初始化扩散过程并结合特权信息蒸馏，实现自动驾驶与具身控制中的实时高质量动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接策略世界动作模型虽将动作生成与显式未来视觉预测解耦以提升推理效率，但仍需数十步去噪才能达到有竞争力的动作质量，造成显著延迟；简单减少去噪步数会使非线性多步去噪轨迹退化为粗粒度更新，严重降低动作质量。

Method: 作者认为随机噪声初始化缺乏结构化动作先验，导致直接策略 WAM 需迭代去噪恢复连贯动作序列。为此构建 imagination tokens 初始化扩散过程，提供物理先验和动作条件表示；并引入带未来条件化的特权信息蒸馏，增强未来感知的动作生成，使模型仅需一步扩散即可生成高质量动作。

Result: 实验表明 OSWAM 在自动驾驶和具身控制中可取得与需数十步的直接策略 WAM 和 Joint-WAM 相当甚至更好的结果；相较直接策略 WAM 基线约 3 倍加速，并在 NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU 上支持编译推理，延迟低至 40ms。

Conclusion: 通过为扩散初始化注入结构化动作先验并结合未来条件化蒸馏，OSWAM 能在一步去噪内高效生成高质量动作，满足实时部署需求。

Abstract: Direct-policy World Action Models (WAM) have demonstrated the benefits of decoupling action generation from explicit future visual prediction for efficient inference in autonomous driving and embodied control. However, they still require tens of denoising steps to achieve competitive action quality, incurring substantial latency and hindering real-time deployment. Simply reducing the number of denoising steps will collapse the nonlinear multi-step denoising trajectory into coarse updates, substantially degrading action quality. To reduce the denoising steps while enabling real-time action generation, we present an effective world action model with one-step action generation, namely OSWAM, for autonomous driving and embodied control. We argue that it is the absence of structured action priors in random noise initialization that forces direct-policy WAMs to recover coherent action sequences through iterative denoising. Hence, we construct imagination tokens to initialize the diffusion process, providing physical priors and action-conditioned representations. We further introduce privileged information distillation with future conditioning to enhance future-aware action generation. As a result, OSWAM can efficiently and effectively generate high quality actions in just one diffusion step. Our experiments demonstrate that OSWAM achieves comparable or even better results in both autonomous driving and embodied control than direct-policy WAM and Joint-WAM that require tens of steps. It obtains approximately $3\times$ speedup over direct-policy WAM baselines and supports real-time compiled inference with latency as low as 40ms on an NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [2] [Uncertainty Evaluation to Support Safety of the Intended Functionality Analysis for Determining the Applicability of Performance Targets Across Triggering Conditions in ML-Based LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35092)
*Milin Patel,Rolf Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向ISO 21448 SOTIF的LiDAR目标检测器不确定性阈值迁移评估方法：将阈值转化为触发条件，用统计检验判断性能目标在该条件下是否适用，并报告独立序列数与被接受正确检测占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按ISO 21448进行SOTIF分析时，需覆盖所有触发条件；但在某一条件下选择的检测拒绝阈值仅对与选择数据同分布的数据提供统计保证，不能直接外推到其他触发条件。

Method: 将不确定性阈值作为待评估的触发条件；在给定错误概率下检验性能目标在该条件下是适用、不适用还是未确定；并用独立单元下界确定得出适用结论所需的独立记录序列数，以处理帧间相关性。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes上的PointPillars集成实验中，以记录序列计数时，两个数据集均不足以对任何目标限值提供足够独立单元，且所有迁移均未出现“不适用”结果；以帧计数时，对远距离、降雨及两个分别训练检测器之间的迁移，在预先设定的限值下出现“不适用”，满足迁移后目标限值的阈值会拒绝大多数正确检测，且降雨下ROC曲线下面积未下降。

Conclusion: 一个条件下达成的性能目标只有在目标触发条件下经过检验后，才能作为该条件下的证据；报告时应同时给出被接受正确检测占比和独立记录序列数。

Abstract: For safety of the intended functionality (SOTIF) analysis according to ISO 21448, a machine learning object detector for light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds can meet a performance target, a limit on the mean per-frame share of wrong detections among accepted detections, by rejecting detections above an uncertainty threshold. A threshold selected on data of one condition carries a statistical guarantee only for data distributed like its selection data, whereas SOTIF analysis covers every triggering condition. This paper presents an uncertainty evaluation in which the threshold is transferred into a triggering condition, a test decides at a stated error probability whether the target is applicable there, not applicable or not determined, and a lower bound counts the independent units an applicable outcome requires. For PointPillars ensembles on KITTI and nuScenes, counted in recorded sequences, as the dependence between frames requires, neither dataset held enough independent units for any target limit, and no transfer had a not applicable outcome. Counted in frames, the same data gave not applicable outcomes at limits fixed in advance after transfers into far distance, into rain and between two separately trained detectors, thresholds that met the target limit after a transfer rejected most correct detections, and in rain the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve did not decrease. A performance target met under one condition therefore counts as evidence under a triggering condition only after a test there, reported with its share of accepted correct detections and its number of independent recorded sequences.

</details>


### [3] [DriveUniTok: A Unified Multimodal Tokenizer for Driving World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35044)
*Jingbo Xu,Hanchao Leng,JiaHui Huang,Hanbing Li,Jingwei Zhao,Kuiyuan Yang,Chen Gao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DriveUniTok 与 DriveUniWAM，将外观、投影 LiDAR 深度和自车运动统一编码到单一可解码连续潜空间，并用于驾驶世界-动作模型进行未来预测与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型通常将外观、度量和自车运动部分独立建模，忽视它们是同一世界状态的不同投影，导致信息损失与跨模态不一致，限制下游预测和规划性能。

Method: 作者提出统一多模态 tokenizer DriveUniTok，将外观、投影 LiDAR 深度和自车轨迹编码为单一可解码连续潜变量；冻结该 tokenizer，并适配预训练扩散模型 DriveUniWAM 预测未来统一潜变量，再由 tokenizer 解码为未来外观、投影 LiDAR 深度和自车轨迹。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，统一设计相比分离流方案取得更好的未来预测与规划性能，且下游计算成本更低；经强化学习后训练后，DriveUniWAM 达到 PDMS 91.54，在被评估的 WAM 规划器中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 统一建模多模态世界状态可提升驾驶世界-动作模型的一致性与效率，并在预测、规划和计算成本之间取得优于分离式方案的表现。

Abstract: World models are emerging as a fundamental component of policy learning for real-world agents, enabling agents to predict future world states and plan actions within a learned representation of the dynamic environment. Existing approaches, however, often model appearance, metric geometry represented by projected LiDAR depth, and ego motion either partially or independently, overlooking the fact that these modalities are different projections of a shared underlying world state. This separation can lead to information loss and cross-modal inconsistency, ultimately limiting downstream performance. Motivated by this unified view of the world, we propose DriveUniTok, a unified multimodal tokenizer for driving world-action models (WAMs) that encodes these modalities in a single decodable continuous latent. We freeze DriveUniTok and adapt a pretrained diffusion model, DriveUniWAM, to predict future unified latents; the tokenizer then decodes them into future appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego trajectories. Experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that our unified design achieves better future prediction and planning performance at lower downstream computational cost than separate-stream alternatives. With reinforcement learning post-training, DriveUniWAM achieves a PDMS of 91.54, reaching state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated WAM-based planners.

</details>


### [4] [Robust Single-Parameter Point Cloud Registration using Truncated Lp Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34271)
*Seong Hun Lee,Patrick Vandewalle,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅需一个可调参数的鲁棒点云配准方法 TLP，利用不同 p 值的截断 L_p 函数进行对应关系评分、假设选择与模型精化，并通过 GPU 并行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实三维特征匹配中常存在高外点率，而多参数方法需要针对数据集调参、实用性不足；现有单参数方法在精度上仍有提升空间。

Method: TLP 使用不同 p 值的截断 L_p 函数为对应关系分配优先级分数，用于假设选择与最终模型精化；借助截断函数的鲁棒性处理高外点率，并利用 GPU 并行化高效评估假设。

Result: 可在平均不到一秒内从数千个对应关系中完成配准；在公开基准上精度高于最先进的单参数方法，并在四个评估数据集中的三个上优于两种常用多参数方法，同时保持有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: TLP 以单参数设置实现了高鲁棒性、较高精度和实用效率，在高外点点云配准中优于单参数方法并具备与多参数方法竞争的实用性。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for robust point cloud registration with only one tunable parameter. Our method, dubbed TLP, uses truncated $L_p$ functions with different values of $p$ to assign priority scores to correspondences, select the hypothesis, and refine the final model. By leveraging the robustness of truncated functions, TLP effectively handles the high outlier rates commonly encountered in real-world 3D feature matching. We also exploit GPU parallelization to evaluate hypotheses efficiently, enabling registration from thousands of correspondences in under a second on average. With only one tunable parameter, our method offers greater practicality than multi-parameter methods that require dataset-specific tuning of several parameters. Evaluations on public benchmark datasets demonstrate that TLP achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art single-parameter methods. Furthermore, it outperforms two widely used multi-parameter methods on three of the four evaluated datasets while maintaining competitive runtime.

</details>


### [5] [Segmentation of Moving Objects During Camera Motion Using the OWL Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33789)
*Daniel Raviv,Juan D. Yepes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于OWL感知函数的解析框架，利用每像素可恢复的相对平移方向\hat{t}对单相机运动下的多个独立运动刚体进行分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动分割通常依赖训练数据、深度信息或语义先验；本文希望从视觉运动线索中直接、解析地分割多个独立运动的刚体。

Method: OWL将感知looming L与感知旋转ω编码为相对3D结构的闭式表示，并由此逐图像点解析恢复相对平移单位方向\hat{t}。同一非旋转刚体上的点共享相同\hat{t}，不同刚体一般具有不同\hat{t}，因此对逐点\hat{t}估计进行聚类即可分割可见场景。作者在OWL的2D复数形式基础上，用四元数将框架推广到3D单移动相机情形。

Result: 该框架给出自然的分割准则：聚类逐点\hat{t}可将场景划分为多个独立运动刚体；方法具有并行性、相机旋转不变性，且无需训练数据、深度信息和语义先验。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅利用OWL提供的looming与旋转线索，即可解析地恢复相对平移方向并实现多刚体运动分割，为单相机运动分割提供了无学习、无深度、无语义先验的几何框架。

Abstract: We present a principled, analytical framework for segmenting multiple independently moving rigid objects during single camera motion, built directly upon the OWL perception function. OWL encodes two fundamental visual motion cues, perceived looming $L$ and perceived rotation $\omega$, into a single closed-form representation of relative 3D structure. A key consequence of this structure is that the unit direction of relative translation $\hat{t}$ is analytically recoverable per image point from $L$ and $\omega$ alone. All points belonging to a non-rotating rigid body share the same $\hat{t}$, whereas points on different rigid bodies generally yield distinct values. This furnishes a natural segmentation criterion: clustering per-point $\hat{t}$ estimates partitions the visible scene into independently moving rigid bodies. Building on the 2D complex form of OWL, we derive the framework in 3D using quaternions, for a single moving camera. The approach is parallel, camera rotation-invariant, and requires no training data, no depth information, and no semantic priors.

</details>


### [6] [C-DFF: Concept-Level Diagnostics for Mechanism Parity in Simulation-Based Validation of Autonomous Driving Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33760)
*Danial Safaei,Siddartha Khastgir,Matthew Higgins,Jeroen Ploeg,Mohsen Alirezaei,Son Tong,Ludo Gielen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出概念级机制一致性诊断方法C-DFF，用于成对真实-合成图像评估，判断自动驾驶系统是否依赖相同视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真验证中任务输出匹配并不能保证系统依赖相同视觉证据，尤其对安全关键感知功能，真实到合成的迁移验证存在缺口。

Method: 从冻结的系统-under-test中提取决定性证据，利用现成分割模型投影到共享语义概念空间，比较真实与合成图像的概念重要性向量；输出标量机制一致性分数及概念偏移诊断，识别哪些场景概念在仿真中变得更关键或不关键。

Result: 在KITTI与Virtual KITTI 2的2126个匹配对及三个冻结SUT（转向回归、YOLOP可行驶区域、YOLOP车道线）上，C-DFF具有SUT区分性，并提供可解释的概念级摘要以支持验证分诊和模拟器调试；与任务特定输出值上下文指标并用时表明输出一致本身不能确立机制一致性。

Conclusion: C-DFF可作为基于仿真的自动驾驶验证与确认流程中的实用语义诊断层。

Abstract: Simulation is widely used in autonomous-driving validation, but matching task outputs between real and synthetic images does not guarantee that a system relies on the same visual evidence. This creates a validation gap for transfer, especially in safety-critical perception functions. We present Concept-Decisive Feature Fidelity (C-DFF), a concept-level mechanism-parity diagnostic for paired real-synthetic evaluation. Starting from decisive evidence extracted from a frozen system-under-test (SUT), C-DFF projects that evidence into a shared semantic concept space using off-the-shelf segmentation and compares real and synthetic concept-importance vectors. The result is not only a scalar mechanism-parity score, but also a concept-shift diagnostic that identifies which scene concepts become more or less decisive in simulation. We evaluate C-DFF on KITTI $\leftrightarrow$ Virtual KITTI 2 with 2126 matched pairs across three frozen SUTs (steering regression, YOLOP drivable-area, YOLOP lane-line). Across these tasks, C-DFF is SUT-discriminative and yields interpretable concept-level summaries that support validation triage and simulator debugging. When reported alongside task-specific output-value context metrics, C-DFF shows that output agreement does not by itself establish mechanism parity. These results position C-DFF as a practical semantic diagnostic layer for validation and verification workflows in simulation-based AV testing.

</details>


### [7] [Gaussian Episodic Memory for Long-Term Spatiotemporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33158)
*Ling-Chia Chen,Abdelrhman Werby,Kaan Aytekin,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEMS 将持久化时空 3D Gaussian Splatting 地图与智能体推理流程结合，仅更新场景变化区域，并构建半静态环境时空问答基准 GEMS-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境持续半静态变化，现有机器人时空记忆要么受限于结构化表示，要么受限于已观测视角，难以兼顾空间细节、时间演化与开放查询。

Method: 重建一次 3DGS 地图，之后仅在场景变化区域更新并保存修改；智能体流程按需渲染新视角、迭代积累证据，回答时空问题并根据自然语言查询定位物体；同时提出包含 3 个真实和 12 个合成环境、473 个问答对的 GEMS-Bench。

Result: GEMS 在 3D 物体定位和时空问答上优于近期基线，场景更新后的渲染质量与专用场景更新方法相当，并具备存储效率优势。

Conclusion: GEMS 为半静态真实环境提供兼顾视觉细节、时空演化、新视角渲染和语言查询的持久化机器人记忆表示与推理框架。

Abstract: Real-world environments undergo continual semi-static changes, requiring a memory representation that captures both the spatial and temporal evolution of a scene. Existing spatio-temporal memories for robots typically rely either on structured representations, which are limited to the entities and attributes captured during construction, or on image-based memories, which are constrained to previously observed viewpoints. We introduce GEMS, a framework that combines a persistent spatio-temporal 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map with an agentic reasoning pipeline. The 3DGS map is reconstructed once and subsequently updated only in regions where the scene changes, storing only these modifications while preserving visual detail across time. The agentic pipeline leverages this map to render novel views on demand, iteratively accumulating evidence to answer spatio-temporal questions and ground objects from natural-language queries. To advance future research, we introduce GEMS-Bench, a spatiotemporal question-answering benchmark focused on semi-static environments. It comprises 3 real-world and 12 synthetic environments, spanning 473 question-answer pairs. We evaluate GEMS on novel-view synthesis quality across scene updates, 3D object grounding, spatiotemporal question answering, and storage efficiency. GEMS outperforms recent baselines in 3D object grounding and spatiotemporal question answering, while achieving rendering quality on par with dedicated scene-update methods. Code and data: this https URL

</details>

### [8] [Intrinsic Goals for Autonomous Agents: Hierarchical Exploration Captures Future Volitional Action in Drosophila](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34638)
*Declan Kutscher,Reece Keller,Alyn Kirsch,Felix Pei,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究结合果蝇全脑记录与神经力学数字孪生，检验内在动机驱动的分层具身智能体能否复现果蝇自发探索行为及其对未来决策的神经表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物能在无外部感觉线索或外在目标时表现出由内在动机驱动的自主性，但其计算机制尚不清楚；作者希望用人工智能体反向解析这种自主探索机制。

Method: 利用果蝇全脑记录发现自发探索决策可提前10秒以上被神经表征；构建虚拟系留球实验和神经力学数字孪生，采用分层策略从离散低层运动基元库中选择动作，并比较20种内在动机算法（好奇心、熵、胜任力等），在无外部奖励下训练智能体，评估其自发运动统计、内部表征及从运动学和神经活动解码未来转向的能力。

Result: DIAYN这一分层胜任力算法通过学习离散策略最大化行为熵和诱导感觉观测的可分离性，在行为与神经前瞻编码上最接近果蝇；其策略捕获果蝇个体探索策略差异，提示群体多样性有助于最大化状态空间覆盖；DIAYN的潜在活动也复现了生物果蝇半球间运动学编码的和差规则。

Conclusion: 分层、内在动机驱动的智能体可作为反向工程动物自主性的有力框架，并表明优化内在目标可产生与果蝇行为和神经表征定量匹配的具身人工智能体。

Abstract: Animals have a remarkable capacity for autonomy---driven by intrinsic motivation---without external sensory cues or extrinsic objectives, yet the computational mechanisms behind this ability largely remain a mystery. We combine whole-brain recordings in the fruit fly (Drosophila melanogaster), which reveal neural representations of spontaneous exploratory decisions more than 10 seconds in advance, with a neuromechanical digital twin to test whether optimizing intrinsic goals yields realistically embodied artificial agents that quantitatively match fly behavior and internal representations of future volitional action during exploration. Our hierarchical policy selects from a discrete library of low-level motor primitives, consistent with ethological theories of behavioral syllables. We train agents without external reward using 20 intrinsic motivation algorithms spanning curiosity, entropy, and competency methods. The agents are evaluated in a virtual tethered-ball assay modeled on the fly experiment, comparing spontaneous locomotor statistics and internal representations and decoding future turn direction from kinematics and neural activity on the same timescales observed in flies. DIAYN, a hierarchical competency algorithm learning discrete policies that maximize behavioral entropy and the separability of induced sensory observations, achieves the strongest joint behavioral and neural prospective coding performance relative to flies. Its learned policies closely capture individual variability in fly exploration strategies, suggesting that this diversity serves to maximize state-space coverage at the population level. DIAYN's latent activity also closely captures the sum-difference rule for interhemispheric encoding of kinematics in biological flies. These results establish hierarchical, intrinsically motivated agents as a powerful framework for reverse-engineering animal autonomy.

</details>

### [9] [Nonprehensile Robotic Catching Using Virtual Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35851)
*Aakash Khandelwal,Ranjan Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于虚拟非完整约束（VNHC）的非抓取式机器人接取方法，直接依据被接物体状态设计末端执行器运动，避免物体轨迹预测和时变机器人轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接取方法通常需要预测物体轨迹并据此生成时变的机器人轨迹；本文旨在规避这一需求，简化非抓取式接取的控制设计。

Method: 在平面情形下，利用VNHC直接根据物体被抓取前的状态设计并控制末端执行器运动，使物体在期望高度接触末端执行器；接触后采用模型预测控制（MPC）使物体与末端执行器在目标位置静止。

Result: 仿真表明可在期望高度接住物体，并使接触瞬间的相对法向速度最小；VNHC函数仅依赖期望接取高度，同一VNHC可适用于一定范围内的初始条件。

Conclusion: 该VNHC方法无需物体轨迹预测即可实现平面非抓取式接取，并借助MPC完成接触后停稳；目前结果仅通过仿真验证。

Abstract: The problem of nonprehensile robotic catching is addressed using virtual nonholonomic constraints (VNHCs); the motion of the robot end-effector is designed and controlled directly in terms of the states of the object before it is caught on the surface of the end-effector. In contrast to contemporary approaches, our approach avoids the need for object trajectory prediction, and consequent time-varying robot trajectory generation. With a focus on the planar case, we demonstrate that our VNHC approach can be used to catch an object on an end-effector at a desired height, with minimal relative normal velocity at the contact instant. Moreover, the VNHC function depends only on the desired catching height, and the same VNHC can be used for a range of initial conditions. We use model predictive control (MPC) to bring the object and end-effector to rest at a desired location after contact is made. The approach is validated in simulation.

</details>


### [10] [Trajectory Generation and Control for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34456)
*Sandesh Thapa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本项目研究四旋翼无人机的控制器设计与轨迹生成，利用微分平坦生成基于航点的最小snap轨迹，并用PD控制器进行轨迹跟踪，仿真得到平滑的位置与速度轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索四旋翼无人机的控制器设计与轨迹生成方法，验证基于微分平坦的最小snap轨迹生成及PD跟踪方案的可行性。

Method: 参考微分平坦方法进行轨迹生成；基于航点导航生成最小snap轨迹；采用简单的比例加微分（PD）控制器跟踪生成轨迹。

Result: 仿真结果展示了四旋翼无人机平滑的位置轨迹和速度轨迹。

Conclusion: 仿真表明，微分平坦结合最小snap轨迹生成与PD控制可在仿真中实现平滑轨迹生成与跟踪；摘要未给出定量跟踪误差或鲁棒性分析。

Abstract: In this project, I explored controller design and trajectory generation for a quadrotor. Differential flatness was used for trajectory generation based on one of the references. A minimum snap trajectory is generated from the given algorithm based on waypoint navigation, and a simple proportional plus derivative (PD) controller is used to track the trajectories. Simulation results are presented which generate smooth trajectories for position and velocity of the quadrotor.

</details>


### [11] [Nonlinear Control of Aerial Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34454)
*Sandesh Thapa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对配备二自由度机械臂并与环境发生物理交互的四旋翼，设计并比较了笛卡尔阻抗控制与自适应滑模控制，在有界扰动下执行悬停任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂与环境接触时存在扰动和动力学耦合，需要研究适用于物理交互任务的飞行机械臂控制方法，并比较不同控制器的性能。

Method: 建立四旋翼—二自由度机械臂完整动力学模型；分别实现笛卡尔阻抗控制器和自适应滑模控制器；在相同初始条件和期望位置下，对有界扰动中的简单悬停任务进行对比。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明两种控制器均已实现并在相同条件下完成悬停任务对比。

Conclusion: 该工作为空中机械臂物理交互提供完整动力学建模与两类控制器的对比框架，可用于评估阻抗控制与自适应滑模控制在扰动悬停任务中的适用性。

Abstract: This paper presents two controllers for a quadrotor equipped with a two-DOF robot manipulator that physically interacts with the environment. Cartesian impedance and adaptive sliding mode controllers are implemented and compared for a simple hovering task in the presence of some bounded disturbance. A full dynamic model is developed, and the two above controllers are implemented with the same initial conditions and desired position.

</details>


### [12] [Contract-Based Agentic Systems Engineering: Delegation as Quotient for Assured System Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33349)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,John S. Baras*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 当系统需求在固定邻居上分解时，交给组件的需求应由契约商（quotient）计算得到，而不是仅凭提示编写；该商是满足系统需求的最弱可靠SysML v2需求，并保留邻居对组件的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由提示构建的组件可能满足提示却破坏其所加入的系统，因为提示遗漏了邻居对其行为的假设；仅面向任务的需求会丢弃这些假设，使用mock邻居的单元测试也无法覆盖。

Method: 将含assumed与required约束的SysML v2需求视为assume/guarantee契约；在固定邻居上分解系统需求时，取契约商得到开放组件的最弱可靠需求；证明Signal Temporal Logic对商封闭；以SysML v2模型表达需求的推导、分配、满足与验证并计算商；案例中将模拟仓库机器人局部控制器（路径穿越工人路径并到达码头）交给语言模型，在商需求、仅任务需求和自然语言任务三种条件下运行实时episode，并按系统需求真值评分。

Result: 摘要给出了该案例的评分流程，但未报告具体数值；可确定的是商需求携带邻居假设，而仅任务需求丢弃这些假设，mock邻居单元测试也无法触达。

Conclusion: 组件交接需求应作为可计算的契约商生成，以保留邻居假设并保障系统级正确性；仅靠提示或任务需求不足以可靠交接组件。

Abstract: A component an agent builds from a prompt can satisfy it and still break the system it joins, because the prompt omits what its neighbours assume of it. The artifact to hand over is computed, not written. A SysML v2 requirement with assumed and required constraints is an assume/guarantee contract, so when a system requirement is decomposed over fixed neighbours, the open component's requirement is the contract quotient, the weakest sound one, and again a SysML v2 requirement. Its required constraints carry the neighbours' assumptions, which a requirement for the mission alone drops and unit tests with mocked neighbours cannot reach. Signal Temporal Logic is closed under the quotient. We build the requirements, with derivation, allocation, satisfaction and verification, as a SysML~v2 model and compute the quotient from it. A case study delegates the local controller of a simulated warehouse robot, whose route crosses a worker's path and ends at a dock, to one language model under the quotient requirement, a mission-only requirement and a natural-language task, and scores the deliveries on live episodes against the system requirement from ground truth.

</details>

### [13] [Replicate What Varies: Resource Sharing for Domain-Randomized Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33582)
*Zixu Hao,Weijun Wang,Xiangyu Li,Liang Mi,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Patchwork 是一种面向域随机化仿真的资源共享系统，通过共享模板加补丁的表示复用未变化资源，从而显著降低多实例并发仿真的内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发运行大量多样化仿真环境会消耗大量 CPU 和 GPU 内存，因为独立构建的场景会重复保存跨实例不变资源；需要一种在保持各环境随机化配置的同时共享公共资源的机制。

Method: 提出 E=T+Δ 表示：共享模板 T 保存公共资源，补丁 Δ 记录构造目标环境所需的变更。Patchwork 将随机化采样与重放分离，选择可复用模板及补丁以保持每个目标环境的预期配置；运行时分层共享编译后的模型结构和不可变资产，而可变参数与仿真状态保持实例私有。系统在 robosuite 和 MuJoCo 上实现，并在 LIBERO-plus 与 RoboCasa365 工作负载上评估。

Result: 在 32 个副本时，共享模板执行相比每环境一进程执行将 CPU RSS 降低 88–92%。受控实验表明，在支持的共享边界内，内存增长更多由模板多样性和唯一资产内容驱动，而非实例数量。

Conclusion: Patchwork 通过模板-补丁式资源共享有效降低域随机化仿真的内存成本，并将多实例内存扩展的主要因素从实例数转向模板多样性与独特资产内容。

Abstract: Simulation provides a low-cost sandbox where embodied agents can attempt tasks, observe outcomes, and learn from experience. Running many diverse simulation environments concurrently can consume substantial CPU and GPU memory because independently constructed scenes duplicate resources that remain unchanged across instances. We present Patchwork, a resource-sharing system for domain-randomized simulation, built around the representation $E=T+\Delta$: a shared template contains common resources, and a patch records the mutations needed to construct a target environment. Patchwork separates randomization sampling from replay and selects reusable templates together with patches that preserve each target environment's intended configuration. Its layered runtime shares compiled model structure and immutable assets while keeping mutable parameters and simulation state private to each instance. We implement Patchwork on robosuite and MuJoCo and evaluate it on LIBERO-plus and RoboCasa365 workloads. At 32 replicas, shared-template execution reduces CPU RSS by 88-92% relative to process-per-environment execution. Controlled experiments show how sharing shifts memory growth toward template diversity and unique asset content rather than instance count, within the supported sharing boundaries.

</details>

### [14] [RAIWAY: A Large-Wheeled Bipedal Robot with Contact-Informed Blind Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33172)
*Kyungmin Nam,Jiseong Lee,Donghyuk Choi,Jeongsoo Park,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轮式双足机器人 RAIWAY 及基于接触信息的控制方法，通过仿真接触事件蒸馏潜在表示并在部署时由本体感知在线推断，实现无接触传感的复杂地形滚动运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式机器人在不规则、不连续地形上的运动高度依赖局部轮-地接触，但真实接触难以直接测量，且不仅由地形几何决定，因此需要一种无需直接接触传感即可利用接触信息的控制框架。

Method: 在仿真中获取多接触事件并蒸馏为潜在表示；部署时控制器仅利用本体感知在线推断该接触潜在表示，无需接触传感器，从而实现接触信息引导的控制。

Result: RAIWAY 在陡坡、砾石地、凸起表面和岩石地形等多种环境中完成穿越，并在测试的户外楼梯上实现连续上行，台阶高度可达 15 cm；相比未采用接触信息机制的基线，在挑战性室内外场景中表现更优。

Conclusion: 将仿真接触事件蒸馏为潜在表示并由本体感知在线推断，可有效提升轮式双足机器人在复杂不连续地形上的滚动运动能力，并避免对直接接触传感的依赖。

Abstract: We present RAIWAY, an in-house wheeled bipedal robot with 20-inch wheels, together with a contact-informed control approach for rolling locomotion across irregular and discontinuous terrain. Such locomotion is fundamentally contact-dominated, governed by local wheel--terrain interaction that is impractical to instrument directly and depends on more than terrain geometry alone. The proposed framework exploits the contact information available in simulation, distilling multi-contact events into a latent representation. At deployment, the controller infers this representation online from proprioception, requiring no contact sensing. In real-world experiments, the proposed system traverses diverse environments including steep slopes, gravel beds, studded surfaces, and rocky terrain, and demonstrates continuous ascent on the tested outdoor staircases with riser heights up to 15~cm. On our platform, the proposed controller further outperforms baselines without the contact-informed mechanism across challenging indoor and outdoor settings. The video can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [15] [What Does World Modeling Actually Improve in Robot Action Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34593)
*Hanwen Cui,Yuanyuan Li,Qing Guo,Zhida Zhang,Chao Chen,Xi Chen,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估未来视频预测作为机器人动作学习辅助监督的作用：在FastWAM和OpenWAM中做控制消融，并在π0.5上加入轻量Transformer预测器，发现其能显著提升已见任务成功率，但分布偏移下的迁移收益不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）将未来视频预测与机器人动作学习结合，但仅看任务成功率无法说明预测监督如何影响执行行为，也无法判断其收益能否在任务和环境变化下持续。

Method: 在FastWAM和OpenWAM中分别进行未来视频预测损失消融，并固定各模型族内的架构、初始化、机器人训练数据和训练步数；结合轨迹聚类分析成功率变化；进一步将未来视频预测作为π0.5的辅助训练目标，使用仅在训练阶段参与的轻量Transformer预测器。

Result: 在已见任务的干净条件下，预测监督分别将成功率提升24.38和72.91个百分点；轨迹聚类显示多个粗粒度轨迹簇内成功率更高，且部分簇频率变化很小，表明收益不只是轨迹模式占比变化。但在分布偏移下收益不一致：未见任务上的提升因模型和任务而异，环境随机化下两个模型成功率均较低且预测监督无稳定优势。在π0.5上，辅助未来视频预测将成功率从82.94%提升到89.19%。

Conclusion: 未来视频预测可作为动作学习的有效辅助监督，尤其在熟悉任务上改善执行；但其带来的收益不等同于可靠迁移，且在所评估设置中不必依赖大型预训练视频扩散Transformer（DiT）。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-video prediction with robot action learning, yet improvements in task success alone do not reveal how predictive supervision manifests in execution behavior or whether its benefits persist under task and environmental shifts. We conduct controlled future-video prediction loss ablations within FastWAM and OpenWAM, keeping model architecture, initialization, robot training data, and training steps fixed within each model family. Under clean conditions on seen tasks, predictive supervision increases success rates by 24.38 and 72.91 percentage points, respectively. Trajectory clustering further reveals higher success rates within several coarse trajectory clusters, including cases where cluster frequencies change little, indicating that the gains are not solely reflected in changes in the prevalence of trajectory patterns. However, these benefits do not consistently persist under distribution shifts: improvements on unseen tasks vary across models and tasks, while both models exhibit low success rates under the evaluated environmental randomizations, without a consistent advantage from predictive supervision. We further introduce future-video prediction as an auxiliary training objective for $\pi_{0.5}$, where a lightweight Transformer predictor used only during training increases success from 82.94% to 89.19%. These findings support future-video prediction as effective auxiliary supervision for action learning, while distinguishing improved execution on familiar tasks from reliable transfer and showing that such gains need not rely on a large pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT) in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [16] [The German Tank Problem & Navigating by Scent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34583)
*Kordel Kade France*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将二战“德军坦克问题”的序列号估计思想迁移到机器人嗅觉导航：用化学传感器采样读数估计气味羽流峰值浓度，并据此判断是否已到达源点、继续搜索还是着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接近气味源时浓度梯度变平、湍流使信号间歇，小型机器人往往缺乏足够定位精度来绘制羽流，因此需要一个轻量且有统计依据的终止判据。

Method: 把每次传感器读数视为观测，采用无偏估计器，仅需运行最大值和样本计数；再用精确置信界将二者转化为机器人或UAV判断继续搜索或着陆的原则，并推导估计器与置信界。

Result: 展示了该方法的样本效率，并检验了即使其假设不成立时的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该估计器可作为嗅觉导航的终止准则，用于判断是否已到达气味羽流峰值/源附近并决定着陆。

Abstract: A robot searching for the source of an odor must eventually answer a deceptively hard question: have I arrived? Near the source, concentration gradients flatten, turbulence makes the signal intermittent, and a small robot often lacks the positional accuracy to map the plume. Here, we describe a lightweight remedy inspired by a World War II statistics conundrum: the German tank problem. To approximate enemy armament production, Allied analysts developed an estimator based on serial numbers from captured tanks. Analogously, we use the same procedure over sampled chemical sensor readings from a robot to create estimates for the peak concentration of an odor plume, and therefore the odor's source. Treating each sensor reading as an observation, our unbiased estimator needs only a running maximum and a sample counter. An exact confidence bound turns both numbers into a principled rule that the robot or UAV can use to determine whether to keep searching for the plume source or land. We derive the estimator and its bound, demonstrate its sample efficiency, and examine its robustness even when its assumptions fail. Finally, we summarize the estimator's role as the termination criterion for olfactory navigation.

</details>


### [17] [Dual Gradient Consensus for Adaptive Learning to Transient Out-of-Distribution Disturbances in Quadrotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34538)
*Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双梯度共识（DGC）自适应学习方法，用于四旋翼在混合域内/域外残差扰动下的轨迹跟踪，通过改进在线更新步长抑制短时域外残差对学习扰动模型的污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼轨迹跟踪在未建模扰动下常依赖在线残差自适应，但恒定步长更新对所有残差梯度一视同仁，短时域外残差会破坏已学习的扰动模型，因此需要区分可靠与不可靠更新。

Method: DGC保留随机傅里叶特征（RFF）模型、离线BPTT策略以及先验域自适应残差策略（DAPL）的最小二乘自适应，仅修改在线更新步长；它将当前残差梯度与长时、短时梯度记忆比较，分类当前自适应状态，并用置信图抑制不可靠更新，同时在持续域偏移后恢复自适应。

Result: 在数值四旋翼轨迹跟踪中，针对四种混合域内分量与短时域外事件的残差进行评测；与L1增强MPC、基于RFF的MPC、恒定步长DAPL及冻结参数基线相比，DGC在四种情况下均取得最低平均位置RMSE。

Conclusion: DGC通过梯度共识与置信图机制提升混合扰动下在线残差自适应的鲁棒性，减少短时域外事件对扰动模型学习的破坏，并在持续域偏移时保持恢复能力。

Abstract: Quadrotor trajectory tracking under unmodeled disturbances often relies on online residual adaptation, but short out-domain residuals can corrupt the learned disturbance model when constant-step updates treat every residual gradient uniformly. We propose Dual Gradient Consensus (DGC), an adaptive-learning method for mixed in-domain/out-domain disturbances that retains the random Fourier feature model, offline BPTT policy, and least-squares adaptation of a prior domain-adaptive residual policy (DAPL) while modifying the online update step size. DGC compares each residual gradient with long- and short-term gradient memories, classifies the current adaptation regime, and uses a confidence map to suppress unreliable updates while recovering adaptation after persistent domain shifts. We evaluate DGC in numerical quadrotor trajectory tracking under four residuals that mix an in-domain component with short out-domain events. Compared with $L_1$-augmented MPC, RFF-based MPC, constant-step DAPL, and a frozen-parameter baseline, DGC achieves the lowest mean position RMSE in all four cases.

</details>


### [18] [Multimodal Socially Aware Framework for Human-Robot Interaction in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34534)
*Richard Xu,Dakarai Crowder,Alexis E. Block,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合语音、视觉与触觉的多模态社交机器人框架，并在VR中与人工遥操作基线对比；自主系统表现优于中性水平但显著低于遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需协调语音与动作，并理解人类多模态输入，以实现自然、恰当的社会互动。

Method: 构建整合语音、视觉和触觉的多模态框架，用于生成符合情境的机器人语音与动作；在虚拟现实中招募18名参与者，覆盖5个社交互动场景，并与人工遥操作基线进行比较。

Result: 参与者对自主系统在自然性和社会适当性上的评分均高于中性中点，但显著低于遥操作基线；行为编码与定性反馈表明，对话时序、歧义请求澄清和空间协调是造成差距的重要因素。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自主机器人实现自然多模态社交互动的关键挑战，尤其需改进对话时序、歧义处理和空间协调能力。

Abstract: Social robots must coordinate speech and movement while interpreting multimodal human input. We present a multimodal framework that integrates speech, vision, and touch to generate contextually appropriate robot speech and movement. We evaluated the framework in virtual reality with 18 participants across five social interaction scenarios, comparing its performance against a human-teleoperated baseline. Although participants rated the autonomous system above the neutral midpoint for both naturalness and social appropriateness, the teleoperated baseline received significantly higher ratings on both measures. Behavioral coding and qualitative feedback revealed that conversational timing, clarification of ambiguous requests, and spatial coordination were important factors contributing to this performance gap. These findings highlight key challenges in developing autonomous robots capable of natural, multimodal social interaction.

</details>


### [19] [How High Can GPT-6 Astra Climb in Contact-Rich Manipulation? PhysLadder: A Hierarchical, Matched Sim-Real Benchmark for Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34527)
*Haibao Yu,Jiaqi Meng,Zijian Zhang,Bowen Jing,Lei Yang,Yue Hu,Chenchen Ge,Yiming Hou,Chenbo Xin,Dawen Ye,Haifang Qin,Jin Li,Weitao Zhou,Jianing Qiu,Jiankai Sun,Dandan Zhang,Zaiqing Nie,Ping Luo,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhysLadder 分层 Sim-Real 基准，用 16 个家庭任务在三级累积难度上评估接触丰富操作中的物理交互能力，并对 GPT-6 Astra 进行零样本真实与仿真对比测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统评估模型在接触选择、力调节与协调重构等递增交互需求下的能力边界，并检验物理与视觉匹配的仿真环境能否作为可重复、可扩展的测试平台。

Method: 构建 16 个家庭任务，分为 L1 接触选择与几何对齐、L2 持续接触中的力调节、L3 协调接触重构与力调节三级；每个任务在物理和视觉匹配的仿真与真实环境中实例化，共享任务定义和成功标准；对 GPT-6 Astra 零样本执行，每任务每环境 3 次试验。

Result: 真实环境成功率从 L1 的 80.0% 降至 L2 的 47.6%，L3 为 0.0%；仿真环境分别为 66.7%、47.6%、0.0%。16 个任务中 10 个在两环境成功次数相同，其余 6 个相差 1 次成功（共 3 次试验）。空间推理、接触选择、力调节和协调困难反复出现，常使局部物理进展无法累积为任务完成。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了 GPT-6 Astra 随交互需求增加而显现的能力边界，并表明匹配仿真可作为可重复、可扩展的测试床，用于研究限制可靠操作的持续协调物理交互。

Abstract: We introduce PhysLadder, a hierarchical Sim-Real benchmark for evaluating physical interaction capabilities in contact-rich manipulation. Its 16 household tasks span three cumulative levels: contact selection and geometric alignment (L1), force regulation within sustained contact (L2), and coordinated contact reconfiguration and force regulation (L3). Each task is instantiated in physically and visually matched simulated and real-world environments, with shared task definitions and success criteria. We evaluate GPT-6 Astra zero-shot with three trials per task in each environment. Real-world success rates decline from 80.0% at L1 to 47.6% at L2 and 0.0% at L3, with corresponding simulation rates of 66.7%, 47.6%, and 0.0%. Ten of the 16 tasks have identical success counts across environments; the remaining six differ by one success out of three trials. Across both environments, recurring difficulties in spatial reasoning, contact selection, force regulation, and coordination often prevent local physical progress from accumulating into task completion. These findings characterize Astra's observed capability boundary across increasing interaction demands and, for this model, support matched simulation as a repeatable, scalable testbed for studying the sustained, coordinated physical interactions that limit reliable manipulation.

</details>


### [20] [TRIAGE: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference Needs Both Reuse and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34519)
*Sooyoung Kwon,Carlee Joe-Wong,Kyunghan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出无需训练的视觉token调度方法TRIAGE，通过token漂移诊断在重算、复用KV缓存和剪枝之间自适应选择，以降低VLA模型在嵌入式设备上的预填充延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型用于机器人操作时需在移动 manipulator 和人形机器人等 onboard 嵌入板上实时运行，但每个控制步处理数百个视觉token导致语言骨干预填充延迟高；现有无需训练加速器对跳过token统一采用剪枝或KV复用，未区分二者对动作误差的不同影响。

Method: 作者通过token级诊断发现，跳过token应剪枝还是复用KV缓存取决于其漂移，即浅层值相对缓存计算时的变化；据此提出TRIAGE：当漂移低于按骨干校准的阈值且缓存不过旧时判定缓存有效，随后在固定重算预算下优先重算缓存失效的重要token，复用其余缓存有效token，并剪枝剩余token；剪枝-only与复用-only是该划分的两个极端，固定预算可直接控制预填充延迟。

Result: 在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上，TRIAGE平均成功率与完全重算相差不超过3个百分点；在Jetson AGX Orin上预填充延迟最多降低48%；仅重算7%视觉token时，在LIBERO-Object上比最强基线高24.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，按token漂移和缓存有效性进行细粒度重算、复用与剪枝划分，可在保持VLA操作成功率的同时显著降低嵌入式平台预填充延迟，为实时机器人部署提供有效加速方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are increasingly used for robot manipulation because they map camera images and a language instruction directly to actions. Robots that cannot be tethered to a workstation, such as mobile manipulators and humanoids, must run them on onboard embedded boards, where the prefill latency of the language backbone grows with the hundreds of visual tokens processed at every control step. To meet real-time limits, training-free accelerators recompute only a subset of the visual tokens and skip the rest, either by pruning them or by reusing their cached key-value (KV) states. The two options save nearly the same computation, yet existing methods apply only one of them to every skipped token. Through a token-level diagnostic, we show that which option keeps the action closer to full recomputation depends on the token's drift, the change in its shallow-layer value since its cache was computed. On both backbones tested, reusing tokens below a drift threshold was no worse than pruning them, whereas beyond it, reuse yields up to 4.4x the action error of pruning; the same diagnostic calibrates this threshold per backbone. Building on this finding, we propose TRIAGE, which deems a cache valid when its drift is below this threshold and it is not too old. TRIAGE recomputes a fixed budget of tokens, prioritizing important tokens with invalid caches, reuses every other token with a valid cache, and prunes the rest; prune-only and reuse-only methods are the two extremes of this partition. Because the budget fixes the number of recomputed tokens, it directly sets the prefill latency. On LIBERO and SimplerEnv, TRIAGE stays within 3 pp of full recomputation in average success rate while cutting prefill latency on a Jetson AGX Orin by up to 48%, and with only 7% of visual tokens recomputed, it beats the strongest baseline by 24.4 pp on LIBERO-Object.

</details>


### [21] [IntNet: A Communication-Driven Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34466)
*Leandro Parada,Kevin Yu,Panagiotis Angeloudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IntNet：将车辆意图传输与自适应通信调度统一到端到端多智能体强化学习框架中，以提升自动驾驶安全协同并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的多智能体强化学习面临非平稳性、部分可观测性和协同难题；现有共享编码状态观测的合作式MARL方法在安全关键场景中仍不足。

Method: IntNet采用无模型端到端架构，核心是观测预测模块，使智能体预测后续策略输出并通过车联网共享；使用图注意力网络处理消息，并用调度器动态优化通信图以提升带宽效率，联合优化意图传输与通信调度。

Result: 在包含自动驾驶与人工驾驶车辆的复杂城市环境中，与先进通信式MARL方法相比，IntNet实现更好的协同、更低碰撞率，并将通信开销最多降低60%；实验评估了学习效率、可扩展性及信息共享与带宽使用的平衡。

Conclusion: IntNet表明，将意图预测共享与自适应通信调度统一学习，可在安全关键自动驾驶协同中兼顾安全性、协同效果和通信效率。

Abstract: Achieving safety in autonomous driving through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is a critical yet challenging task due to non-stationarity, partial observability, and the need for effective coordination among agents. Although earlier cooperative MARL methods have aimed to improve coordination by sharing encoded state observations, we find these strategies are insufficient in safety-critical scenarios. To address this gap, we present IntNet, a novel MARL framework that enhances safety by incorporating the transmission of vehicle intent and adaptive communication scheduling into a unified end-to-end learning paradigm, jointly optimising all components for safe coordination. Central to our approach is an observation prediction module that enables agents to forecast subsequent policy outputs, which they can then share across a vehicle-to-vehicle network. Our model-free architecture employs a Graph Attention Network to process incoming messages and a scheduler component to dynamically optimise the communication graph for bandwidth efficiency. We compare IntNet against state-of-the-art communicative MARL methods in complex urban environments with both autonomous and human-operated vehicles, achieving improved coordination, lower collision rates, and reducing communication efforts by up to 60\%. Through extensive experiments, we assess the framework's learning efficiency, scalability, and the balance between information sharing and bandwidth usage for collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [22] [Scaling Dexterous Manipulation via Action Representation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34453)
*Yanjinghao Xu,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Jianjia Mo,Zhenghao Yin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ARPG，将动作表征学习与动作生成显式分离：先在大规模人类操作数据上预训练动作表征编码器，冻结后训练130M参数的生成骨干在表征空间学习任务条件动作分布，从而提升复杂双臂灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据稀缺，现有方法需用有限演示同时学习动作表征和任务条件动作分布，导致动作表征学习不足，并限制下游生成模型产生复杂灵巧动作的能力。

Method: ARPG首先在大规模人类操作数据上预训练动作表征编码器；随后冻结该编码器，并训练紧凑的生成骨干网络（130M参数），在编码器表征空间中学习任务条件动作分布。

Result: ARPG在DexJoCo基准上取得最先进性能，并将双臂灵巧操作平均成功率从π_{0.5}的10.1%提升至44.7%；缩放实验表明，更广泛的人类操作行为覆盖可有效提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 动作表征预训练为提升复杂灵巧操作能力提供了独立的缩放维度。

Abstract: Large-scale vision, language, and video pretraining has substantially improved the perception and generalization capabilities of robotic manipulation models, but action generation for complex dexterous manipulation remains constrained by the scarcity of robot demonstrations. Limited robot demonstrations must be used to learn both action representations and task-conditioned action distributions, making it difficult to adequately learn action representations and, in turn, limiting the ability of downstream generative models to generate complex dexterous actions. To address this limitation, we propose ARPG (Action Representation Pretraining and Generation), which explicitly separates action representation learning from action generation: we first pretrain an action representation encoder on large-scale human manipulation data. We then freeze the encoder and train a compact generative backbone (130M parameters) to learn task-conditioned action distributions in the encoder's representation space. Experimental results show that ARPG achieves state-of-the-art performance on the DexJoCo benchmark and improves the average success rate on bimanual dexterous manipulation from 10.1% for $\pi_{0.5}$ to 44.7%. Scaling experiments further show that broader coverage of human manipulation behaviors effectively improves downstream performance. These results indicate that action representation pretraining provides an independent scaling dimension for improving complex dexterous manipulation capabilities.

</details>


### [23] [AuRA: From Atoms to Bits through Autonomous Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34452)
*Xinchen Yu,Yoonkyo Jung,Haoran Chen,Zikui Cai,Aayush Talreja,Seungjae Lee,Hyunjin Kim,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AuRA 自动化机器人交互与数据准备，用于数字孪生重建，在显著减少人工与采集时间的同时保持可比的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息数字孪生通常依赖人工操作和手动标注交互数据，限制了机器人学习实验的规模化。

Method: AuRA 结合执行感知的抓取选择、由机器人状态推导的夹爪运动测量，以及配准的预交互几何与外观先验，实现自主数据采集，避免依赖视觉夹爪跟踪，并恢复被机器人遮挡的物体信息。

Result: 在13个可变形物体上，AuRA 将每个成功重建资产的主动人工劳动和墙钟采集时间分别降低88.5%和55.4%；相对人工采集的 PhysTwin 基线，在已知夹爪运动下的未来预测9项指标中7项提升，单向 L1 Chamfer 距离降低28.1%，在评估的未见交互下平均轮廓 IoU 和 Chamfer 距离相近。

Conclusion: 自主机器人交互能够支持更高效的数字孪生获取，并达到可比的重建质量。

Abstract: Simulation enables robot-learning experiments at scale, but acquiring physics-informed digital twins often requires human manipulation and manual annotation of recorded interactions. To reduce this reliance on human effort, we present AuRA, a system that automates robot interaction and data preparation for digital-twin reconstruction. AuRA combines execution-aware grasp selection, gripper-motion measurements derived from robot states, and registered pre-interaction geometry and appearance priors. These choices enable autonomous collection, avoid reliance on visual gripper tracking, and recover object information obscured by the robot. Across 13 deformable objects, AuRA reduces active human labor and wall-clock collection time per successfully reconstructed asset by 88.5% and 55.4%, respectively. Relative to the human-collected PhysTwin baseline, AuRA improves seven of nine metrics for future prediction under known gripper motion, including a 28.1% reduction in one-way $L_1$ Chamfer distance. It also achieves similar mean silhouette IoU and Chamfer distance under the evaluated unseen interactions. These results highlight the potential of autonomous robot interaction to support more efficient digital-twin acquisition with comparable reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [24] [On Distributed Sensing and Adversarial Localization of Narrowband Acoustic Sources for Offshore Data Center Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34393)
*Adnan Abdullah,David Blow,Harsha Gundala Sureshbabu,Dongyoon Joung,Sara Rampazzi,Md Jahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告提出一种面向海上数据中心威胁的窄带对抗声源被动声学监视框架：利用移动接收器与选定固定接收器临时构成锚点对以约束可行解空间，并通过“锚约束初始定位+粒子滤波联合跟踪位置与速度”的两阶段流程，实现稀疏传感器和窄带发射条件下的声源定位与跟踪；经蒙特卡洛仿真、ROS-Gazebo机器人演示和开放水域实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声源定位方法通常依赖宽带信号和紧凑接收阵列，而目标场景为窄带发射与稀疏传感器，导致TDOA测量出现混叠并形成模糊解空间，常规定位方法失效；因此需要保护海上大型基础设施（如海上数据中心）免受对抗性声源威胁。

Method: 提出由移动接收器与选定的固定接收器临时形成锚点对，以约束可行解空间；两阶段流水线首先利用锚点约束估计声源初始位置，随后初始化粒子滤波器，结合一个实时锚点测量与质量自适应的固定接收器对测量，联合跟踪声源位置和速度。评估采用蒙特卡洛声学仿真、ROS-Gazebo机器人演示和开放水域现场实验。

Result: 仿真中定位成功率达到91%；在接收器位置不确定和传感噪声条件下，10次现场试验中有9次跟踪误差低于2米。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了被动声学监视在保护大规模基础设施免受声学威胁方面的可行性。

Abstract: This report investigates an acoustic surveillance framework for localizing and tracking narrowband adversarial acoustic sources that threaten offshore data centers. Unlike conventional sound source localization (SSL) methods that rely on broadband signals and compact receiver arrays, the considered scenario involves narrowband emissions and sparse sensors. In such scenarios, the observed time-difference-of-arrival (TDOA) measurements are aliased and produce an ambiguous solution space, rendering conventional localization methods ineffective. To address this challenge, we integrate a mobile receiver that temporarily forms anchor pairs with selected fixed receivers, constraining the feasible solution space. The proposed two-stage pipeline first estimates the initial source position using anchor constraints, then initializes a particle filter that jointly tracks source position and velocity using one live anchor measurement and quality-adaptive fixed-pair measurements. We evaluate the proposed method through Monte Carlo acoustic simulations, ROS-Gazebo robotic demonstrations, and open-water field experiments. Our framework achieves a localization success rate of 91% in simulation, while 9 of 10 field trials show tracking error below 2m under receiver-position uncertainty and noisy sensing conditions. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of passive acoustic surveillance for protecting large-scale infrastructure against acoustic threats.

</details>


### [25] [ThinkLocoManip: A Whole-Body Control Agentic Harness for Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34370)
*Nuo Chen,Shizeng Zhang,Yujie Yang,Jinghong Wang,Zhenyang Qu,Xiao-Ming Wu,Yunfei Guo,Zhongwen Lu,Haojie Liu,Chunpeng Lu,Qiao Sun,Xueyi Zhang,Jiangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ThinkLocoManip——面向腿式移动操作（loco-manipulation）的智能体化执行框架，通过交错推理与行动、工具编排和关键帧思考机制，提升大规模环境中长时序任务的泛化与错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接动作生成或“先规划后执行”范式在长时序任务中面临泛化不足和错误恢复能力弱的问题，难以支撑复杂环境中的持续执行与策略自适应。

Method: 采用交错式推理-行动范式，使智能体持续推理状态、执行动作、观察结果并调整策略；构建面向感知、导航、操作和移动的工具集，并将移动（locomotion）显式作为工具域；设计兼顾单域工具使用与跨域协调的技能；引入关键帧思考机制以保留关键执行上下文并支持高效错误恢复。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，ThinkLocoManip 在具有挑战性的长时序腿式移动操作任务上显著优于直接动作生成和“先规划后执行”的代表方法，成功率达 77%，超过最强 plan-then-act 基线（37.1%）的两倍；消融实验验证了交错推理与行动、工具编排及协调技能执行的有效性。

Conclusion: ThinkLocoManip 通过智能体化工具编排与交错推理-行动机制，有效提升腿式移动操作在复杂长时序任务中的执行成功率和错误恢复能力，说明将移动纳入工具域并协调多域技能是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose ThinkLocoManip, an agentic harness for legged loco-manipulation designed to tackle complex, long-horizon tasks in large-scale environments. Existing approaches typically follow either direct action generation or a plan-then-act paradigm, both of which can struggle with the generalization and error-recovery capabilities required for extended task execution. In contrast, ThinkLocoManip adopts an interleaved reasoning-and-acting paradigm, allowing the agent to continuously reason about its current state, execute actions, observe their outcomes, and adapt its strategy accordingly. Building on this paradigm, our agentic harness orchestrates a suite of specialized tools for perception, navigation, manipulation, and locomotion, enabling flexible composition of heterogeneous capabilities. Unlike existing robotic agentic harnesses, ThinkLocoManip explicitly incorporates locomotion as a tool domain for agent reasoning and execution. We further design a set of skills that explicitly account for both tool usage within each functional domain and coordination across domains, supporting effective execution of complex behaviors over long horizons. To tackle task failure during long-horizon interactions, we develop keyframe thinking mechanism to preserve critical execution context and support efficient error recovery. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that ThinkLocoManip substantially outperforms representative methods based on both direct action generation and plan-then-act paradigms across challenging long-horizon legged loco-manipulation tasks. Notably, ThinkLocoManip achieves a success rate of 77%, more than twice that of the strongest plan-then-act baseline (37.1%). Our experiments further validate the effectiveness of interleaved reasoning and acting, tool orchestration, and coordinated skill execution.

</details>


### [26] [RynnVLA-Latent: Scaling Robotic Manipulation Foundation Models with 3D-Grounded Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34356)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Zhian Su,Bohan Hou,Yinzhou Tang,Fengyi Wang,Kehan Li,Yuming Jiang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Zhenyu Zeng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnVLA-Latent 利用异构交互视频学习连续、3D 接地的潜在动作，并将其作为可扩展监督预训练机器人策略，从而减少对原生动作标注和统一控制空间的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型扩展受限于稀缺的动作标注演示，以及不同机器人本体控制空间不兼容，难以直接利用大规模异构视频。

Method: 提出 RynnLAM，从视觉变化中学习无需原生机器人控制的连续 3D 接地潜在动作；结合残差未来特征预测、相机补偿 3D 流监督和相机运动解耦，使表示捕捉控制相关运动而非仅外观变化。在 30.1K 小时人类、机器人和仿真视频上生成 745.52M 潜在动作描述符。RynnVLA-Latent 通过流匹配从视觉观测和语言预测潜在动作块，再将预训练骨干和动作专家适配到具体本体的机器人动作。

Result: 4B 策略在 LIBERO 达 98.8% 成功率，在无基准特定适配的 LIBERO-Plus 达 84.7%，在 VLABench 五个赛道平均 51.9%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 双臂基准平均 88.6%；在六项真实世界操作任务（灵巧手和移动夹爪平台）上取得对比方法中最高观测成功率。

Conclusion: 表示消融和数据扩展结果表明，潜在动作监督可突破原生动作标注数据限制，为扩展机器人策略学习提供有效路径。

Abstract: Scaling vision-language-action (VLA) models is constrained by scarce action-labeled demonstrations and incompatible control spaces across robot embodiments. We present RynnVLA-Latent, a framework that turns heterogeneous interaction videos into scalable supervision for robot policy pretraining. At its core, RynnLAM learns continuous, 3D-grounded latent actions from visual transitions without requiring native robot controls. By combining residual future-feature prediction, camera-compensated 3D-flow supervision, and camera-motion decoupling, RynnLAM encourages representations that capture control-relevant motion rather than appearance changes alone. We apply RynnLAM to 30.1K hours of human, robot, and simulation videos, producing 745.52M latent-action descriptors. RynnVLA-Latent then learns to predict latent-action chunks from visual observations and language through flow matching, before adapting its pretrained backbone and action expert to embodiment-specific robot actions. The resulting 4B policy achieves 98.8% success on LIBERO, 84.7% on LIBERO-Plus without benchmark-specific adaptation, 51.9% average success across five VLABench tracks, and 88.6% average success on the RoboTwin 2.0 bimanual benchmark. It also achieves the highest observed success rates among the compared methods on six real-world manipulation tasks across dexterous-hand and mobile-gripper platforms. Together with representation ablations and data-scaling results, these findings demonstrate the potential of latent-action supervision for scaling robot policy learning beyond native action-labeled data.

</details>


### [27] [ScheNav: Schematic-Guided Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34286)
*Guichen Huang,Chenchen Jing,Che Sun,Yuwei Wu,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出基于示意图引导的目标物体导航框架：构建Furnished-RPLAN数据生成流程，并设计ScheNav闭环导航方法，通过SchemaField将粗略示意图与累积观测融合为空间价值场，从而提升导航成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标物体导航要求智能体在复杂环境中定位并到达目标，而人类常利用低成本但信息丰富的粗略草图或方向标注等示意图；作者希望让具身智能体也能有效利用此类示意引导。

Method: 作者开发了两部分工作流：一是将2D平面图转化为导航训练环境的数据生成管线，生成Furnished-RPLAN数据集，包含超过33k个带家具布局、配对导航回合及可控噪声示意图；二是ScheNav闭环导航框架，利用学习到的空间模块SchemaField融合示意图引导与累积观测，预测空间价值场，并随探索更新，结合轨迹训练和跨帧一致性目标保证不同视角下引导一致。

Result: 实验表明，ScheNav能从模拟平面图中学习使用示意图引导，并迁移到未见过的具身环境；在示意图、目标椭圆和指向方向等引导形式下，导航成功率和路径效率均有提升，并对测试的示意图扰动和缺失具有一定容忍性。

Conclusion: 粗略示意图引导是一种切实可行的方法，可改善具身目标物体导航性能。

Abstract: Object goal navigation requires an embodied agent to locate and reach target objects in complex environments. Humans often rely on low-cost yet informative schematic maps, such as rough sketches or directional annotations. We develop a workflow for schematic-guided embodied navigation with two components: a training-data generation pipeline and ScheNav, a closed-loop navigation framework. Our lightweight data-generation pipeline turns 2D floorplans into navigation training environments, yielding Furnished-RPLAN, a dataset of over 33k furnished layouts with paired navigation episodes and noise-controllable schematic maps. ScheNav turns rough schematic guidance into exploration and stopping decisions by using SchemaField, a learned spatial module that fuses the guidance with accumulated observations and predicts spatial value fields. The framework updates these fields as exploration proceeds, with trajectory-based training and a cross-frame consistency objective supporting coherent guidance across changing viewpoints. ScheNav learns to use schematic guidance entirely from simulated floorplans and transfers this ability to unseen embodied environments. Experiments show improved navigation success and path efficiency across schematic maps, goal ellipses, and pointed directions, together with tolerance to the tested schematic perturbations and omissions. Together, these results demonstrate that rough schematic guidance offers a practical way to improve embodied navigation.

</details>


### [28] [AdaPolicy: Action-Driven Anticipatory Robot Policies via Next-Chunk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34280)
*Fan Yang,Yuting Su,Chen Zhao,Fugui Fan,Peiguang Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaPolicy 提出一种动作驱动的预见性策略框架，通过级联下一动作块预测从动作信号中激发未来感知表示，无需未来观测监督，且推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略的预见能力多依赖未来观测在像素或潜空间中的显式监督，带来额外架构与计算复杂度；本文探究能否直接从动作信号中获得预见性。

Method: AdaPolicy 使用 Online Action Head 同时输出当前动作块和演化潜状态，再由级联的 Unrolled Action Head 基于该状态预测下一动作块，并将损失经演化状态回传以注入隐式预见；该展开仅用于训练，不需编码未来观测，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在 MetaWorld、RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO 及真实世界部署中取得一致提升，在耳机装配、穿鞋带等毫米级精度任务上最高提升 14 个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作驱动的级联下一块预测可轻量地为机器人策略赋予预见能力，在不增加推理成本的情况下提升多样任务的控制性能。

Abstract: Endowing robot policies with anticipatory capabilities has proven effective in improving control performance. However, existing methods predominantly rely on explicit supervision derived from future observations, in either pixel or latent space, at the cost of additional architectural and computational complexity. Departing from explicit future supervision, we ask whether such foresight can be elicited directly from the action signal. To this end, we propose \textbf{AdaPolicy}, an \textbf{A}ction-\textbf{d}riven \textbf{a}nticipatory policy framework that shapes future-aware representations via cascaded next-chunk prediction. Specifically, an Online Action Head jointly produces the current action chunk and an evolved latent state. Building on this state, a cascaded Unrolled Action Head forecasts the next chunk, and the resulting loss flows back through the evolved state to instill implicit foresight into the online policy. Importantly, this training-only unroll requires no future-observation encoding, keeping AdaPolicy lightweight to train with zero inference-time overhead. Experiments on MetaWorld, RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-world deployments demonstrate consistent gains across diverse settings, including up to $14$ percentage points on millimeter-precision tasks such as earphone assembly and shoelace threading. More details and visualizations are available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [29] [HuGe: Scaling Humanoid Generalist Vision-Language-Action Models with Egocentric Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34186)
*Yang Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Hengtao Li,Yuheng Huang,Zijian Yang,Ji Dai,Maoxin Wang,Yi Gao,Qishen Wang,Baohua Yin,Peiyang Li,Shuanghao Bai,Mingyang Sun,Pengxiang Ding,Jianqin Yin,Lixin Yang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HuGe 是一类人形通用 VLA 模型，利用超过 2000 小时的第一人称人类与机器人数据预训练，通过预测预训练全身控制器的潜在运动 token 和手部控制信号，实现从人类示范到人形机器人策略的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLA 扩展到人形机器人需要协调的全身控制和多样化示范，而真实世界数据采集成本高；第一人称人类示范可补充任务经验，但视觉具身差异和缺失机器人本体感知阻碍迁移。

Method: HuGe 采用三种代表性 VLA 架构，预测预训练全身控制器的潜在运动 token 及手部控制信号；构建三阶段训练流程：第一人称预训练、人形适配和下游微调；预训练时通过仿真回放构造伪本体感知状态，微调时通过重渲染修改第一人称观测中的手臂外观以促进跨具身迁移。

Result: HuGe 在仿真基准 HumanoidArena 上取得最先进性能；在 Unitree G1 真实实验中，用视觉适配后的第一人称人类示范增强机器人示范，显著提升 pick-and-drop 任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结合第一人称人类数据与视觉/本体感知适配，可有效缓解跨具身迁移难题，为人形机器人通用 VLA 的可扩展训练与迁移提供可行路径。

Abstract: Extending Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) to humanoid robots requires coordinated whole-body control and diverse demonstrations that are costly to collect in real-world scenarios. Although egocentric human demonstrations provide a complementary source of task experience, differences in visual embodiment and missing robot proprioception complicate transfer to humanoid policies. To address these challenges, we introduce HuGe, a family of Humanoid Generalist VLAs pre-trained on over 2,000 hours of egocentric human and robot data. Specifically, HuGe employs three existing representative VLA architectures to predict latent motion tokens for a pretrained whole-body controller, together with hand-control signals. Based on this, we introduce a three-stage training pipeline that combines egocentric pre-training, humanoid adaptation, and downstream fine-tuning. To facilitate transfer across embodiments, we construct pseudo-proprioceptive states through simulation playback during pre-training and modify arm appearance in egocentric observations through re-rendering during downstream fine-tuning. With this pipeline, HuGe achieves the state-of-the-art on simulation benchmark HumanoidArena. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 indicate that augmenting robot demonstrations with visually adapted egocentric demonstrations significantly increases success on a pick-and-drop task.

</details>


### [30] [Learning Force-Reactive Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35862)
*Sihang Li,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Charlie Cheng,Ruihan Yang,Chen Feng,Guanya Shi,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLORE 是一个学习力反应式人形机器人移动操作（loco-manipulation）的框架，通过无机器人演示采集与力反应扩散策略，实现双手及全身协调的接触力控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日常任务不仅需要控制运动，还需要控制接触力；在人形机器人上，接触力往往需要跨双手和全身协调，无法仅由运动学轨迹指定。

Method: 使用两个带力传感的 UMI 风格手持夹爪采集手部运动与接触力旋量，并用可穿戴追踪器采集身体运动，从而获得无机器人演示；力反应扩散策略以视觉、本体感知和测量力旋量为输入，预测动作及每只手力指令；带力跟踪的混合柔顺全身控制器利用真实力/力矩传感器测得力误差的积分，在保持平衡的同时让每只手沿指令方向精确跟踪力、在其他方向保持柔顺。

Result: 仿真中控制器能跟踪不同幅值和方向的手部力指令，且全身运动精度损失很小；在机器人上的三个接触丰富任务中，力反应策略成功率为 80%，而在相同演示和相同控制器下，仅位置策略成功率为 26.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将力反应策略与全身柔顺力跟踪控制结合，可显著提升人形机器人在接触丰富移动操作任务中的成功率。

Abstract: Many everyday tasks require controlling contact force as well as motion. On a humanoid, this force must often be coordinated across both hands and the whole body, and cannot be specified by kinematic trajectories alone. Therefore, we present FLORE, a framework for learning force-reactive humanoid loco-manipulation. We collect robot-free demonstrations using two force-sensing UMI-style handheld grippers for hand motion and contact wrenches, and wearable trackers for body motion. A force-reactive diffusion policy takes vision, proprioception, and measured wrenches as input, then predicts actions and per-hand force commands. A hybrid compliance whole-body controller with force tracking executes these commands while maintaining balance: an integral of the force error measured by real force/torque (F/T) sensors lets each hand track force accurately along the commanded direction and remain compliant along the others. In simulation, the controller tracks per-hand force commands of varied magnitude and direction, with little loss in whole-body motion accuracy. Across three contact-rich tasks on the robot, the force-reactive policy succeeds in 80% of rollouts, compared with 26.7% for a position-only policy trained on the same demonstrations and executed by the same controller. For more details, please visit our website: this https URL .

</details>


### [31] [Differentiable Contact Dynamics for Legged Locomotion: Policy Learning and Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35852)
*Gijeong Kim,Seungbin Oh,Dongyun Kang,Hae-Won Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于摩擦接触的平滑解析梯度，仅在后向传播中替换接触灵敏度，保持前向硬接触求解不变，并用于腿足机器人策略学习与接触隐式轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确接触灵敏度虽有闭式表达，但仅描述当前接触模式内的变化，无法为相邻接触模式提供一阶信息，导致梯度在接触模式切换时不可用。

Method: 对de Saxcé修正的Coulomb摩擦二阶锥互补问题采用中心路径松弛，平滑分离、夹紧和滑动模式的条件；在由硬接触解初始化的Newton迭代所得中心路径点上计算松弛灵敏度，并仅用于反向传播。

Result: 该灵敏度在接触模式转换中保持连续，并随松弛消失收敛到精确灵敏度；仅用10万样本、桌面PC少于2分钟训练即可得到无需微调迁移到硬件的策略，trotting策略可抵抗推挤并穿越碎石和湿滑地面；轨迹优化中可到达精确梯度和Signorini松弛梯度遗漏的接触丰富解。

Conclusion: 所提出的平滑解析接触梯度在保持前向可行性的同时改善了跨接触模式的梯度信息，可有效支撑腿足机器人策略学习和接触隐式轨迹优化。

Abstract: This paper proposes a smooth analytic gradient for frictional contact and applies it to policy learning and contact-implicit trajectory optimization for legged locomotion. The exact contact sensitivity is available in closed form but describes changes within the current contact mode, providing no first-order information toward neighboring contact modes. The proposed gradient replaces this sensitivity only in the backward pass, leaving the hard-contact forward solve unchanged and the realized rollout feasible. A central-path relaxation of the de Saxcé-corrected second-order-cone complementarity problem for Coulomb friction smooths the conditions defining the separating, clamping, and sliding modes. The relaxed sensitivity is evaluated at a point on the central path obtained by a Newton iteration initialized from the hard-contact solution. The sensitivity remains continuous through contact-mode transitions and converges to the exact sensitivity as the relaxation vanishes. Using 100,000 samples and less than 2 min of training on a desktop PC, policy learning with the proposed gradient produces a policy that transfers to hardware without fine-tuning. On hardware, the robot under a trotting policy withstands pushes and traverses debris and slippery ground. In trajectory optimization, the proposed gradient reaches contact-rich solutions missed by exact and Signorini-relaxed gradients. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [32] [Profiling a Frontier Agent with EBench: From Direct Control to Harness with Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35832)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Guanbo Jiao,Xing Gao,Ning Gao,Yukai Wang,Jiantong Chen,Zihou Zhu,Xinyu Li,Ningyu Yan,Shuai Wang,Jianming Ma,Haoyu Guo,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告在EBench上评估前沿多模态大模型GPT-6-Astra作为机器人控制智能体的能力，并与后训练的VLA和WAM策略在相同视觉与本体感知协议下比较；重点分析直接控制和“智能体框架+策略调用”两种配置的互补性与性能边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿MLLM已能在无机器人专项训练下控制机器人，但其与后训练VLA/WAM策略在不同操作需求下的能力差异、交互方式及适用边界尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 将GPT-6-Astra置于智能体框架中，通过统一观测协议控制机器人；设置两种配置：一是直接生成路点命令的直接控制，二是可调用后训练VLA或WAM策略执行动作的框架加策略模式，并在EBench上比较整体性能、泛化维度、任务类型与token成本。

Result: 直接控制整体表现可与后训练策略竞争，在短时程任务上领先且跨泛化维度性能波动最小，但在长时程、桌面操作和高精度接触丰富任务上落后。框架加策略能结合两者优势，将整体成功率提升至最强对比后训练策略水平并显著降低token成本；但仍低于各组件逐任务上界，原因包括策略调用次优、固定指令执行缺乏子目标、以及智能体恢复控制时造成抓取中断。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了前沿MLLM智能体的能力边界，表明MLLM直接控制与后训练VLA/WAM策略具有互补性，并为未来智能体框架设计在策略调用、子目标分解和控制切换等方面提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated. In this report, we evaluate GPT-6-Astra on EBench as the core of an agent that controls a robot through an agent harness under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol as post-trained policies. We study two agent configurations: direct control, in which the agent generates waypoint commands directly, and harness with policy, in which it can also invoke a post-trained VLA or WAM policy for action execution. Under direct control, the agent achieves competitive overall performance relative to post-trained policies. It leads on short-horizon tasks and exhibits the least performance variation across generalization dimensions, whereas it underperforms post-trained policies on long-horizon tasks, tabletop manipulation, and high-precision contact-rich tasks. In the harness with policy, invoking a post-trained VLA or WAM combines the complementary strengths of both components, raising overall task success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost. However, performance remains below the per-task upper bound of the standalone components due to suboptimal policy invocation, fixed-instruction execution without subgoals, and grasp disruption when the agent resumes control. Overall, our study characterizes the capability boundary of a frontier MLLM agent and suggests directions for improving future agent harness design. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [33] [Pretraining Knows What, Simulation Teaches How: Compositional Generalization in a Sim-Trained Robot Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35817)
*Benjamin Pikus,Daniel Saunders,Mircea Mironenco,Salman Mohammadi,Mathew Mathew,Sirou Zhu,Divya Makkar,Xuhua Huang,Qinbo Li,Supreeth Narasimhaswamy,Siyuan Cen,Thomas Neff,Dejia Xu,Shyamal Buch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAP 提出一种无需真实机器人数据的机器人策略配方：用现成小型视觉语言模型（27B）通过工具调用控制机器人，先利用预训练模型的世界理解与推理能力，再在简单仿真任务上做监督微调和 GRPO，学习机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需要同时理解场景中的任务意图并生成正确的机器人动作。现有 VLA 方法通常依赖大量真实机器人数据，成本高且泛化与效率受限，因此作者希望用小型现成 VLM 和简单工具接口实现高效、可泛化的机器人控制。

Method: ZAP 使用预训练 27B 参数模型作为基座，通过简单工具 harness 让模型调用机器人控制接口；先用预训练能力获得世界理解与推理，再通过监督微调和 GRPO 在简单仿真任务上训练动作决策。训练过程中不使用任何真实世界机器人数据。

Result: 在真实世界任务上，ZAP 达到 96.7% 成功率，在该 harness 下与 GPT-6 Astra 持平，优于 MolmoAct2 VLA 的 33.3%，同时运行速度快 3 倍、成本低 17 倍。ZAP 还能泛化到训练中未出现的新物体、目标、策略和语言指令，例如将试管放入试管架并可选择不同孔位、双臂空中交接试管、在井字棋盘上画 X 以及下棋。此外，ZAP 可迁移到未见过的本体：在单臂 Franka 上于 RoboLab-120 达到 55.9%，领先最强 VLA/WAM 基线 4.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: ZAP 表明，小型现成 VLM 结合工具 harness、仿真监督微调和 GRPO，可以在不使用真实机器人数据的情况下获得高成功率、低成本和强泛化能力，并能迁移到新的机器人本体，为高效通用的机器人策略学习提供了可行路线。

Abstract: A robot policy needs to understand what a task is asking for in a scene, and how to move the robot to accomplish that task. To achieve these two skills, we introduce Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP), a recipe that teaches a small off-the-shelf vision-language model to control a robot via a simple tool harness. We use a pretrained 27B parameter model to bootstrap world understanding and reasoning, followed by supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on simple simulated tasks to teach it how to move the robot. Without any real-world robot data during training, ZAP reaches 96.7% success on real-world tasks, matching GPT-6 Astra in our harness and outperforming the MolmoAct2 VLA (33.3%), while running 3x faster and 17x cheaper than GPT-6 Astra. We demonstrate that ZAP also generalizes to completely new objects, goals, strategies, and language by seating a test tube in a rack (and choosing a different hole in the rack when prompted), handing a test tube between arms mid-air, drawing an X on a tic-tac-toe board, and playing chess. These goals and types of manipulation don't appear in our training data. Furthermore, ZAP also transfers to an unseen embodiment. On a single-arm Franka, ZAP reaches 55.9% on RoboLab-120, 4.9 percentage points ahead of the leading VLA/WAM baseline.

</details>


### [34] [SPECOLA: A Tool for Specification Driven Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35730)
*Immanuel Ampomah Mensah,Hadas Kress-Gazit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Specola 是一个基于信号时序逻辑（STL）的机器人仿真行为评估工具，用规范化监控补充单一成功率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真基准通常只报告成功率，无法反映成功裕度、对目标参数变化的敏感性、违反的约束以及成功是否持续，因此需要更细粒度的形式化评估。

Method: Specola 允许用户在仿真器信号上编写 STL 规范，并在策略执行时自动创建和管理监控器；提供 Python API 和命令行接口以集成到现有策略执行循环，并提供适配器接口连接新仿真器。

Result: 已提供九个仿真环境和基准的适配器，包括 LIBERO、RoboCasa、Isaac Lab 和 RoboDojo，并通过案例研究展示了 Specola 支持的细粒度评估能力。

Conclusion: Specola 通过将 STL 监控引入机器人仿真评估，能够超越成功率提供更细致、可形式化的机器人行为分析。

Abstract: Simulation benchmarks are a useful way to measure progress in robotics; typically they provide success rate as the dominant measure of performance. However, success rate reports only whether a rollout completed the task, hiding by what margin it succeeded, how sensitive the success is to goal parameter changes, requirements it may have violated, and whether success persisted. Signal Temporal Logic (STL) provides a formal language for capturing specifications; it can be used to define requirements, and measure how close a robot behavior is to satisfying or violating them. Using STL monitors in a simulator, however, requires exposing simulator state and mapping it to specification-relevant signals. We present Specola, a tool for evaluating robot behaviors in simulation with respect to STL specifications. Specola provides an interface for writing specifications over simulator signals, and creates and manages the relevant monitors while a policy executes. We provide a Python API and command-line interface for integrating monitoring into existing policy execution loops. We provide an adapter interface for connecting new simulators, and provide adapters for nine simulation environments and benchmarks, including LIBERO, RoboCasa, Isaac Lab, and RoboDojo. We provide case studies that illustrate the nuanced evaluation that Specola enables. Code and adapters are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [TrySee: Modular Perception-Enhanced VLM Grounding for Closed-Loop Robot Task Planning in Specialized Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35687)
*Elias Hofele,Thomas Trapp,David Timmermann,Tristan Schnell,Georg Heppner,Arne Roennau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrySee 是一个模块化闭环机器人任务规划流水线，将通用 VLM 推理与领域特定感知和成熟机器人组件结合，无需 VLM 微调或端到端演示，并在 5 个差异较大的领域及真实拆解任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在高层推理和任务规划上表现突出，但在专业领域中图像解释与接地能力不足；将 VLM 规划集成到领域特定训练数据有限的机器人系统仍具挑战。

Method: 提出 TrySee：采用本质反应式的循环规划流水线，加入领域特定模块化视觉预处理以提供在线接地，并通过动态预处理选择机制提升可扩展性；系统依赖可替换感知模块和运动规划，无需 VLM 微调或端到端示范。

Result: 在涵盖 5 个差异显著领域的规划任务上评估，包括真实机器人拆解任务；棕地环境现场测试表明该方法能将 VLM 规划实际集成到领域特定训练数据有限的专业机器人系统中。

Conclusion: TrySee 通过模块化闭环规划与可替换感知/运动组件，使通用 VLM 规划可适应专业机器人任务，并在有限领域数据条件下具备实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in high-level reasoning and task planning for robots. However, specialized domains remain a challenge for VLMs' image interpretation and grounding abilities. We introduce TrySee, a modular closed-loop robot task planning pipeline integrating general-purpose VLM reasoning with domain-specific perception and established robotics components. The system consists of an inherently reactive cyclic planning pipeline, augmented with domain-specific modular vision preprocessing, to provide online grounding. Finally, a dynamic preprocessing selection mechanism contributes to the scalability of the pipeline. The system requires neither VLM fine-tuning nor end-to-end demonstrations and adapts through replaceable perception modules and motion planning. The approach is evaluated on planning tasks across 5 widely different domains, including a real-world robot disassembly task. The field tests in a brownfield environment demonstrate the approach's practicality in integrating VLM planning into specialized robotic systems with limited domain-specific training data.

</details>


### [36] [FISRF: Flexible Instructional Scaffolding Reflection Framework for Robotics Task Planning with Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35686)
*Qingmiao Liang,Jiatao Zhang,Runxin Zou,Yufan Song,Wei Song,Yuhua Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出FISRF框架，将LLM任务规划中的反思纠错建模为智能体—导师架构，利用最近发展区（ZPD）动态选择反思策略与导师指导，以减少错误经验累积并提升纠错效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的反思纠错方法主要依赖自主探索，在新环境早期交互中容易积累错误经验，影响后续任务规划与纠错效果。

Method: 提出灵活教学脚手架反思框架FISRF：估计机器人在任务规划中的最近发展区（ZPD），并据此自适应选择反思策略和导师指导，促进有效纠错经验的积累。

Result: 在ALFWorld和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，FISRF将平均纠错轮数降低37%，冗余推理降低25.1%，整体任务成功率提升8%。

Conclusion: FISRF通过引入ZPD估计与自适应教学脚手架，有效缓解自主探索中的错误经验累积问题，并提升LLM任务规划中的纠错效率与任务成功率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely adopted for reflective error correction in task planning; however, existing approaches primarily rely on autonomous exploration, rendering them prone to accumulating erroneous experiences during early interactions with new environments. To address this limitation, we propose the Flexible Instructional Scaffolding Reflection Framework (FISRF), an agent--mentor architecture that estimates the robot's Zone of Proximal Development (ZPD) in task planning and adaptively selects reflection strategies and mentor guidance, thereby facilitating the accumulation of effective error-correction experience. Experiments in ALFWorld and on real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that FISRF reduces the average number of error-correction rounds by 37% and redundant reasoning by 25.1%, while improving the overall task success rate by 8%. Project webpage : this https URL

</details>


### [37] [Continuous-Time Safe Planning and Control for Agile Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35670)
*Gage MacLin,Mason Peterson,Camilla Tabasso,Venanzio Cichella,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时四足运动规划框架：保留完整驱动的SE(2)模型及其各向异性、航向耦合速度约束，用复合Bernstein多项式参数化轨迹，并借助凸包性质在整个规划时域内认证避障与输入约束，在线闭环输出机体速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数实时规划器依赖凸简化与简化运动学模型，虽易求解却丢弃四足可执行的复杂运动，并且只在离散点认证安全，无法排除采样点间与移动障碍发生碰撞的可能。因此需要同时保证动态可行性与可证明碰撞避免的实时规划方法。

Method: 以复合Bernstein多项式参数化轨迹，保留四足全驱动SE(2)模型及各向异性、航向耦合速度界并接受由此产生的非凸性；利用Bernstein多项式凸包性质，在每个规划时域整体上认证碰撞避免和输入界；以在线重规划速率闭环求解优化，向运动控制器发送机体速度；通过仿真消融、外部基线对比和硬件实验验证。

Result: 仿真中方法优于消融与外部基线；硬件实验中四足机器人在未知移动障碍间导航，并恢复了简化模型通常会排除的横向运动与航向耦合运动。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保留完整四足SE(2)运动约束的同时实时生成连续安全轨迹，兼顾动态可行性与可证明安全性，并具备实机部署可行性。

Abstract: Agile quadruped navigation in dynamic environments demands trajectories that are both dynamically feasible and provably collision-free. Most real-time planners achieve tractability through a convex reduction that requires simplified kinematic models, discarding motion that the robot can readily execute, and certify safety only at discretization points, leaving open possible inter-sample collisions with moving obstacles. We present a real-time motion planning framework that retains the fully-actuated $SE(2)$ model of a quadruped, with its anisotropic, heading-coupled velocity bounds, and accepts the resulting nonconvexity. By parameterizing trajectories as composite Bernstein polynomials, we exploit the convex hull property to certify collision avoidance and the input bounds over the entirety of each planning horizon. Solved at online replanning rates, the optimization runs in closed loop, commanding body velocities to the locomotion controller. We validate the approach in simulation against ablations and external baselines, and in hardware experiments on a quadruped navigating among unknown moving obstacles, recovering both lateral and heading-coupled motion that simplified models exclude.

</details>


### [38] [Environment Edits as a Corrective Signal for Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35659)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出环境编辑作为机器人修正的补充反馈，并开发FERRET从机器人与环境编辑轨迹中学习用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次机器人修正会同时改变多个特征，难以消歧；且关于物体属性及物体间关系的偏好无法仅通过移动机器人表达。

Method: 在机器人运动保持不变的条件下让用户编辑场景，使编辑唯一可归因于被移动物体；FERRET同时从机器人修正和环境编辑中学习偏好。

Result: 环境编辑能够消歧特征，并学习到仅靠机器人修正无法学到的偏好。

Conclusion: 环境编辑是与机器人修正互补的有效反馈形式，可提升偏好学习能力。

Abstract: Robot corrections allow people to adjust a robot's behavior, but a single correction changes many features. Also, preferences about object attributes and relationships between objects cannot be expressed by moving the robot. We propose environment edits as a complementary form of feedback. The robot's motion is held constant as the human edits the scene, so the only explanation for the edit is the moved object. We introduce Feature Learning from Environmental and Robot-Relational Edit Traces (FERRET), which learns preferences from both robot and environment corrections. We show that environment edits are able to disambiguate features and learn preferences that robot corrections alone cannot.
