</details>


### [39] [VPR-Space: A Benchmark for Visual Place Recognition in Space Analog Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35642)
*Richard Fechner,Peer Neubert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 VPR-Space，一个面向月球/火星等地外表面视觉地点识别（VPR）的新基准，包含 16 个基于太空模拟域的数据集、面向下游 SLAM 价值的新真值生成方法，并评估了 9 种先进 VPR 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPR 是视觉 SLAM 中回环检测的重要手段，现有 VPR 与 SLAM 方法在城市和特征丰富自然环境中表现良好，但在地外表面这类广阔且特征贫乏的环境中面临严重挑战，因此需要专门的基准来评估地外表面 VPR 能力。

Method: 作者构建了 16 个基于 Space Analog Domains 的 VPR database-query 数据集，覆盖环境、视角变化和光照条件等挑战；针对 VPR 病态性问题，提出不采用固定位置与视角阈值定义同地点，而是依据匹配是否对下游 SLAM 有价值来生成真值，并利用多种先进 Relative Pose Estimators 的集成性能生成 VPR GT 矩阵，要求 VPR 方法识别出后续 RPE 可提供有价值位姿估计的图像对。

Result: 作者评估了 9 种先进 VPR 方法，结果表明现有方法仍有相当大的改进空间，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Conclusion: VPR-Space 为地外表面 VPR 提供了任务导向的评测基准和新真值定义方式，揭示了现有 VPR 方法在特征贫乏地外环境中的不足，并发布了数据集预发布版本以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is an important means for loop closure detection in visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). While state-of-the-art VPR and SLAM approaches excel in urban and feature-rich natural environments, the vast and feature-poor environments of extraterrestrial surfaces like on the Moon or Mars pose severe challenges for existing approaches. This paper presents a novel benchmark, VPR-Space, to evaluate VPR capabilities on extraterrestrial surfaces. There are three contributions: (1) We collected sixteen VPR database-query datasets based on existing data from Space Analog Domains (SAD), terrestrial environments that resemble properties of the Moon or Mars. The collection includes a range of challenges regarding environment, viewpoint change, and illumination conditions. (2) To address the ill-posed nature of VPR, we propose a novel approach to generate the ground-truth information in VPR benchmarks. Instead of defining that a query and datbase image show the same place if and only if their camera poses are within fixed position and viewing direction thresholds, we propose to make the decision dependent on whether the match would be valuable in the downstream SLAM task. To avoid the overhead and restrictions of evaluating VPR in-the-loop within SLAM systems, we provide VPR ground-truth (GT) matrices based on the performance of an ensemble of state-of-the-art Relative Pose Estimators (RPE). Under this GT we require a VPR method to identify exactly those image pairs for which a subsequently applied RPE is expected to provide a valuable pose estimate in a SLAM context. (3) We evaluated nine state-of-the-art VPR approaches. The results show considerable room for improvement and indicate directions for future research. We provide a pre-release version of the dataset on github: $\texttt{ this https URL }$

</details>


### [40] [Revisiting Direct Control: A Field Evaluation of Sensor Fusion Efficacy in Teleoperating Full-Scale Autonomous Vehicles via Bird's Eye View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35630)
*Stanisław Kuczma,Sławomir K. Tadeja,Michał R. Nowicki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在真实车尺寸车辆现场实验中比较三种远程驾驶可视化界面，发现LiDAR与相机融合的鸟瞰图（BEV）在主动控制场景下显著提升可用性、降低认知负荷和任务时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统失效时，远程操作是重要安全兜底机制；但现有研究多关注高层监督控制或仿真实验，难以反映真实全尺寸车辆驾驶的物理动态与感官需求。

Method: 采用真实车尺寸车辆开展现场实验，N=18名参与者，比较三种空间数据表示界面：(i) 标准相机画面基线；(ii) 原始LiDAR点云BEV；(iii) LiDAR与相机数据流融合BEV，并结合客观性能与主观评估。

Result: 与被动观察研究相反，主动控制下融合BEV显著优于LiDAR BEV的感知可用性（p≤0.0001）和认知任务负荷（p=0.01，NASA-TLX=34.58/100），也显著优于仅相机视图（p=0.043）；融合界面还显著缩短任务完成时间（p=0.0461），SUS可用性得分达77.9/100，优于仅相机和仅LiDAR视图。

Conclusion: BEV传感器融合在最大化安全裕度同时最小化操作员心理需求，挑战现有远程操作惯例，并为稳健远程驾驶系统设计提供实证支持。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, robot teleoperation serves as a critical safety fallback mechanism, requiring operators to assume direct control in the event of system failure. While recent research has pivoted toward high-level supervisory control or simulation-based studies, these approaches often fail to capture the physical dynamics and sensory demands of driving a full-scale vehicle. To address this gap, we evaluated the impact of spatial data representation on operator performance using a real car-sized vehicle in a field experiment with $N = 18$ participants. We compared three visualization interfaces: (i) a standard camera feed baseline, with two bird's-eye views (BEV) from (ii) a raw LiDAR point cloud, and (iii) a sensor fusion of LiDAR and camera data streams. Contrary to prior passive observation studies suggesting that sensor-fusion BEV may hinder awareness, our active-control results demonstrate that such representation significantly outperforms the standard LiDAR BEV in perceived usability ($p \leq 0.0001$) and cognitive task load ($p = 0.01$, NASA-TLX score: 34.58/100) while also being significantly better than the Camera-only view ($p = 0.043$). The fused interface also led to statistically significant reductions in task completion time ($p = 0.0461$) and superior usability (SUS: 77.9/100) compared to those of the Camera-only and LiDAR-only views. By synthesizing objective performance data with comprehensive subjective assessments, we demonstrate that BEV fusion maximizes safety margins while minimizing the operator's mental demand. These findings challenge existing teleoperation conventions and provide an empirically validated approach for designing robust remote piloting systems.

</details>


### [41] [From Language to Drives: A Multimodal Framework for Aligning Autonomous Robots with Human Purposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35629)
*Diana Cerdas-Vargas,Alejandro Romero,Richard J. Duro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用大语言模型和多模态模型作为文化赋能器，将自然语言表达的人类目的转化为机器人的任务与驱动力，从而实现开放式学习自主机器人与人类意图的多模态交互对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式学习自主机器人能够通过与环境交互自行生成技能和目标，但缺乏确保这些技能和目标与人类用户保持相关且一致的机制，因此需要引入LLM/VLM中的通用与文化知识来实现人机目的对齐。

Method: 提出一个多模态交互对齐流程，将自然语言表达的人类目的转化为机器人架构中的动机组件，即机器人任务（missions）和驱动力（drives）。系统分为语义对齐、任务生成和驱动力建模三层，并形成双向交互循环：人类输入定义高层目的，机器人借助LLM/VLM传递的多模态信息扩展为可执行任务，反馈用于维持与人类意图的一致性。

Result: 在仿真和实体机器人上的实验表明，该流程能够生成有效任务和行为，并在与自主机器人的交互场景中支持有效的人机对齐。

Conclusion: 以LLM/VLM作为文化赋能器，并通过多层架构和双向反馈实现从人类目的到机器人动机组件的转化，是促进开放式学习自主机器人行为与人类目的对齐的有效途径。

Abstract: This work explores a multimodal approach to the alignment of the behavior of open-ended learning autonomous robots with human purposes through the use of large language models as cultural enablers. While autonomous robots can generate their own skills and goals through interaction with the environment, they often lack mechanisms to ensure that these remain relevant and aligned to those of human users. The proposed approach makes use of the general and cultural knowledge contained in LLMs and VLMs to establish a multimodal interactive alignment process in which human purposes, expressed in natural language, are transformed into motivational components of a robot architecture in the form of robot missions and drives that guide the behavior of the robot. The system is structured into three layers for semantic alignment, mission generation, and drive modeling. This design enables a bidirectional interaction loop: human inputs define high-level purposes, robots expand them into executable missions making use of multimodal information relayed by LLMs and VLMs, and feedback maintains alignment with human intent. Experiments in simulation and with a physical robot demonstrate that the process produces valid missions and behaviors, supporting effective human-agent alignment in interactive scenarios with autonomous robots.

</details>


### [42] [Robot-Aligned 3D Gaussian Splatting for Precise Reaching with Wrist-Camera VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35626)
*Zuoxu Wang,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的数据生成流程，为腕部相机VLA策略生成机器人对齐的视觉-动作监督，并配合指令条件化的接近验证器，实现电子废弃物拆解中的精准接触前接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略学习受限于机器人数据采集，电子废弃物拆解场景尤为严重：零件密集堆叠、外观相似，且薄壁、反光、机械结构复杂，难以获得精确的物体级网格与接触模型，完整数字孪生仿真不可行，因此本文聚焦接触前的精准接近任务。

Method: 利用3DGS重建真实工作空间，并从机器人对齐位姿渲染多样化的腕部相机观测；将这些观测与平滑、结构化的接近轨迹配对，为VLA微调提供机器人对齐的视觉-动作监督；同时在VLA策略上增加指令条件化的接近验证器(reach verifier)，估计目标接近度并在物理交互前给出可靠终止。

Result: 在主板拆解实验中，仅依赖腕部相机观测（无需外部侧视相机）即可实现精准的VLA接近；使用π0.5时，1,500条生成轨迹在三个目标上取得80.0%的精准接近成功率，而150条遥操作演示为58.7%，且主动人工采集时间不足10分钟。

Conclusion: 3DGS-based合成数据生成能以极低人工成本替代大量遥操作演示，结合接近验证器可支撑腕部相机VLA在复杂电子废弃物场景中的精准接近，为难以构建数字孪生的场景提供了可扩展的数据获取途径。

Abstract: Efficient robotic data collection remains a major bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) policy learning, especially for tasks requiring accurate end-effector positioning and camera-action alignment. This challenge is particularly severe in electronic-waste disassembly, where robots operate in cluttered assemblies of densely packed, visually similar components. Full digital-twin simulation is difficult because many parts are thin, reflective, or mechanically complex, making accurate object-level meshes and contact models difficult to obtain. In this work, we focus on pre-contact reaching and propose a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based data generation pipeline for wrist-camera VLA adaptation. Our method reconstructs the real workspace with 3DGS and renders diverse wrist-camera observations from robot-aligned poses. These observations are paired with smooth, structured reaching trajectories to provide robot-aligned visual-action supervision for VLA fine-tuning. We further augment the VLA policy with an instruction-conditioned reach verifier that estimates target proximity and provides reliable termination before physical interaction. Experiments in motherboard disassembly demonstrate precise VLA-based reaching using wrist-camera observations alone, without an external side-view camera. With $\pi$0.5, 1,500 generated trajectories achieve 80.0\% precise-reaching success across three targets, compared with 58.7\% using 150 teleoperated demonstrations, while requiring less than 10 minutes of active human capture. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [43] [Context-Aware Generation and Modulation of Expressive Motion Behavior using Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35615)
*Till Hielscher,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从多模态上下文自动生成并调制复杂、富有表现力的全身手势序列的方法，结合动画原理、动态运动基元与多模态基础模型，并提升部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富有表现力的机器人动作能提升人机交互中的参与度、喜爱度和任务表现，但自动生成且上下文自适应的复杂全身动作仍具挑战。

Method: 用动态运动基元统一实现动画原理，借助多模态基础模型从文本、音频、视频上下文生成手势序列及参数化动作变体；方法可解释、可组合并跨机器人形态；系统集成到连续控制框架，并通过知识蒸馏学习更小模型以降低token消耗和延迟。

Result: 用户研究表明，相比非调制或API级条件，参与者认为该系统生成的动作与交互上下文更一致；并展示了系统能为不同运动学结构的机器人生成富有表现力的动作。

Conclusion: 该方法在灵活性、通用性和部署效率方面推进了表情化机器人动作生成，并验证了上下文对齐性与跨形态适用性。

Abstract: Expressive robot motion positively impacts human-robot interaction by improving user engagement, likability, or task performance. We present a novel approach to automatically generate and modulate complex, expressive full-body gesture sequences from a multimodal (text, audio, video) context description. Based on a unified mathematical implementation of the Principles of Animation using Dynamic Movement Primitives, we use multimodal foundation models to generate such sequences along with parametric motion variations that are highly context-aware. This method extends the state of the art in terms of flexibility and generality by being interpretable, composable, and working across different robot morphologies. Moreover, we integrate the system into a continuous control framework and leverage knowledge distillation to learn a much smaller model, significantly improving token efficiency and system latency. Results from a user study with a human-like platform indicate that participants judged our system's motions to be better aligned with the interaction context than motions produced under a non-modulated or API-level condition. We also demonstrate how the system generates expressive motion for robots with different kinematics, showcasing its versatility. Paper webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [RoboDoctor: Training-Free Recursive Self-Improvement for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35604)
*Heyang Yu,Haomin Li,Sihang Li,Jixin Yan,Shiyun Peng,Wanqi Zhong,Tianyu Xin,Yuan Yao,Xiaoyu Tian,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDoctor 是一个免训练框架，将通用机器人策略与语言模型智能体结合，在固定模型权重下通过执行反馈实现诊断、接地动作与持久经验复用，从而完成执行时适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略和语言模型智能体能力互补，但任务完成还要求根据物理交互后果调整其使用方式；论文将执行视为适应发生的关键环节，利用反馈指导当前动作并为后续决策修正提供依据。

Method: 提出 RoboDoctor，连接诊断、接地动作和持久经验，围绕三个修正目标组织：harness 在策略与智能体间分配控制权，intervention 决定何时辅助并保留有用策略行为，operation 依据观测效果改进物理方法；链接经验记录保留决策原因、尝试方法、结果及复用条件。

Result: 在包含 54 个 RoboDojo-Sim 任务条目的精选档案上，RoboDoctor 取得五维平均分 57.10，原生成功率为 49.44%；记录交互展示了策略变化、选择性纠正和抓取修正。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了所评估系统中的执行时适应能力，但仍需受控实验来分离跨回合经验本身的贡献。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies and language-model agents provide complementary capabilities, but completing a task also requires adapting their use to the consequences of physical interaction. We study execution as a locus of adaptation: feedback guides the current action and supplies evidence for revising subsequent decisions under fixed model weights. We introduce RoboDoctor, a training-free framework that connects diagnosis, grounded action, and persistent experience across three revision targets. The harness allocates control between policy and agent; intervention determines when to assist while preserving useful policy behavior; and operation refines physical methods through observed effects. Linked experience records retain the reason for a decision, the attempted method, its outcome, and conditions for reuse. On a curated archive of 54 RoboDojo-Sim task entries, RoboDoctor achieves a five-dimension mean score of 57.10, with a native success rate of 49.44%. Recorded interactions illustrate strategy changes, selective correction, and grasp revision. These findings characterize execution-time adaptation in the evaluated system; controlled trials remain necessary to isolate the contribution of cross-episode experience.

</details>


### [45] [Distributed Predictive Flocking for a Swarm of Miniature Vibration-Driven Robots in a Confined Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35582)
*Prajol Shrestha,Siddhant Mohapatra,Filip Novkoski,Julian Oelhaf,Nicolas Vandewalle,Ana-Sunčana Smith,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式预测集群控制器：每个机器人实时求解自身NMPC并仅与局部邻居交换预测轨迹，在0.64平方米受限场地中比较六种避碰安全约束；前瞻离散时间CBF整体最优，并在最多至几何堆积极限的集群规模下保持低碰撞、高局部对齐与良好协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群机器人需要在无领导者、无中心规划器、无全局地图的受限场地中协调大量微型机器人；随着集群密度增加，避碰成为限制因素，而可安全共存的机器人数量取决于机器人间安全约束的建模方式。

Method: 设计分布式预测集群控制器，每个机器人求解自己的非线性模型预测控制问题，优化集群凝聚、对齐、机器人间与障碍物安全以及任务目标，并仅与局部邻居交换预测轨迹。在该通用实时控制架构内，于开源振动驱动bristlebot数字孪生和0.64平方米场地中比较六种屏障函数与控制屏障函数安全公式；将最佳方案部署于趋光、趋光绕行和无刺激凝聚任务，集群规模从10个机器人直至几何堆积极限。

Result: 前瞻离散时间CBF整体表现最佳，在几乎所有测试集群规模下碰撞最少、机器人间距余量最大。部署后，小规模集群碰撞较低，接近堆积极限时碰撞陡增；协调与凝聚性能平缓下降而非崩溃；各规模下局部对齐均较高，且集群未以安全换秩序，在受限场地中同时保持安全与秩序。

Conclusion: 分布式NMPC结合前瞻离散时间CBF可在受限场地中实现密集集群的安全有序共存；安全约束公式显著影响可共存规模与碰撞表现，且安全性与群体秩序可以同时维持。

Abstract: Coordinating many miniature robots in a confined arena, with no leader, central planner, or global map, is a core challenge for swarm robotics. As the swarm grows denser, collision avoidance becomes the limiting factor, and the number of safely coexisting robots depends on the formulation of the inter-robot safety constraint. We present a distributed predictive flocking controller in which each robot solves its own nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) problem, optimizing swarm cohesion and alignment costs, inter-robot and obstacle safety, and a task objective, while exchanging only predicted trajectories with local neighbors. Within this generalized controller architecture, solved in real time, we compare six barrier and control-barrier-function (CBF) safety formulations on a digital twin of an open-source vibration-driven bristlebot in a $0.64\,\mathrm{m^2}$ arena. A look-ahead discrete-time CBF performs best overall, with the fewest collisions and the widest inter-robot margins across nearly every swarm size tested. We deploy it on phototaxis, phototactic orbiting, and cohesion without a stimulus tasks, using swarm sizes starting from ten robots and up to the geometric packing limit. The swarm holds well: collisions stay low in small swarms, rising steeply as the packing limit approaches, while coordination and cohesion degrade gently rather than collapsing. The swarm is found not to trade safety for order, keeping both across the confined arena, with local alignment high at every swarm size.

</details>


### [46] [Preemptive Instruction Following with Resumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35576)
*Zerun Liu,Lechao Cheng,Yimin Liu,Shengeng Tang,Yaxiong Wang,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出PIFR基准，用于评估长时程具身导航中运行时任务抢占与恢复能力，并给出一个轻量候选排序基线以改善中断后的目标重定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身导航常被更高优先级指令打断；智能体需完成当前原子任务、执行插入任务，并在环境状态、视角和候选导航点变化后恢复原任务序列。现有评估缺乏对此类运行时抢占与恢复的系统刻画。

Method: 构建PIFR基准，包含单次、重复和嵌套中断设置；设计针对插入任务完成、原任务恢复和完整序列完成的分阶段指标；并提出轻量候选排序基线，用于中断后目标重定位。

Result: 评估显示恢复性能随中断条件变化，而聚合成功率会掩盖不同中断处理与恢复能力之间的差异；所提候选排序基线可改善中断后的目标重定位。

Conclusion: PIFR与基线共同提供了研究长时程具身导航中中断处理与任务恢复的系统框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied navigation may be interrupted by a higher-priority instruction before the current task sequence is complete. The agent must finish its current atomic task, execute the inserted task, and resume the remaining sequence from the updated environment state, where its viewpoint and available navigation candidates may have changed. To evaluate this capability, we introduce PIFR benchmark, a novel task for runtime task preemption and resumption in embodied navigation. PIFR includes single, repeated, and nested interruption settings,with stage-specific metrics for inserted-task completion, resumption of the original task, and completion of the full sequence. Evaluations show that recovery performance varies across interruption conditions, while aggregate success can obscure differences in interruption handling and resumption. We further propose a lightweight candidate-ranking baseline to improve target re-grounding after interruption. Together, PIFR and the baseline provide a systematic framework for studying interruption handling and task recovery in long-horizon embodied navigation.

</details>


### [47] [Enhancing Deep Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Motion Planning via Task Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35494)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,Jiayu Xu,Juan David Hernández,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向深度强化学习的任务分解框架，将迁移学习与奖励重设计结合，以提升障碍环境下机器人操作策略的学习效率、成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真中训练机器人操作策略具有安全性和成本优势，但涉及障碍的任务在深度强化学习下表现较差且收敛缓慢，因此需要更有效的训练框架。

Method: 提出任务分解框架：迁移学习仅跨结构相关任务迁移第一层critic参数，以保留通用状态-动作表示并避免迁移奖励/障碍特定的深层价值几何；奖励重设计将任务分解为分阶段子目标，提供更密集的优化信号。在PyBullet Panda平台验证，并将最佳策略部署到真实Franka Emika FR3机器人进行定性可行性展示。

Result: 在障碍设置中取得稳定提升：Push with obstacle达到0.83±0.05，比最强基线TQC的0.77±0.06高约7.8%；Slide with obstacle达到0.95±0.02，比SAC的0.84±0.06高约11%。前向迁移最多减少70%的障碍任务收敛时间；奖励重设计将稀疏奖励Pick-and-Place训练从约0.75M步缩短到0.20M步，减少约73%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该任务分解框架结合选择性critic迁移与奖励重设计，能显著提高障碍环境下机器人操作任务的最终性能和训练效率，并具备向真实机器人部署的可行性。

Abstract: Training robotic manipulation policies in simulation is widely adopted due to safety and cost efficiency; however, tasks involving obstacles remain under-performing and often converge slowly under Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL). To address this challenge, we propose a task decomposition framework for DRL that integrates Transfer Learning (TL) with Reward Redesign (RR) to improve learning efficiency and robustness in obstacle environments. The TL strategy selectively transfers only the first-layer critic parameters across structurally related tasks, since that layer captures general state--action representations while deeper layers encode reward- and obstacle-specific value geometry; RR decomposes tasks into staged sub-objectives to provide denser optimization signals. Experiments on the PyBullet Panda platform show consistent gains in obstacle settings. On Push with obstacle, our method achieves $0.83 \pm 0.05$, outperforming the strongest baseline (Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC), $0.77 \pm 0.06$) by approximately $7.8\%$. On Slide with obstacle, performance reaches $0.95 \pm 0.02$, exceeding Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) ($0.84 \pm 0.06$) by about $11\%$ success rates. In addition to higher final performance, forward transfer reduces convergence time by up to $70\%$ in obstacle tasks, and RR shortens sparse-reward Pick-and-Place training from roughly $0.75$M to $0.20$M steps ($\approx73\%$ reduction). The best policy is deployed on a real Franka Emika FR3 robot as a qualitative feasibility demonstration.

</details>


### [48] [DEX-PHD: A Dexterous Portable Handheld Device for Multimodal Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35462)
*Jooyoung Hong,Noboru Myers,Obin Kwon,Yixian Yang,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEX-PHD 是一种便携式手持设备，通过集成灵巧手、逐指连续控制和多模态记录，实现便携且准确的多模态灵巧操作数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作数据采集方法存在权衡：基于运动链的遥操作可准确获取机器人状态，而基于视觉的方法便携但精度/状态获取有限。

Method: DEX-PHD 将灵巧机器人手、逐指连续控制和多模态日志集成于无缆手持设备；操作者通过关节级映射直接控制机器人手，系统同步记录本体感知信息、指尖接触数据和腕部相机视频。

Result: 在需要多指协调的接触丰富任务上验证，并将采集的示范通过开环回放部署到实体人形机器人；结果表明开环回放保留了手指运动的主要时间模式和任务相关接触。

Conclusion: DEX-PHD 为便携式、多模态灵巧操作数据采集提供了实用平台。

Abstract: Existing manipulation data collection methods typically offer either accurate robot-state acquisition through kinematic-chain-based teleoperation or convenient, portable operation through vision-based approaches. We present DEX-PHD, a portable handheld device that combines these advantages for dexterous manipulation by integrating a dexterous robot hand, continuous per-finger control, and multimodal logging. The system records proprioceptive information, fingertip contact data, and wrist-camera video, while the operator directly controls the robot hand through joint-level mapping. Its untethered design enables data collection across diverse environments. We demonstrate DEX-PHD on contact-rich tasks requiring coordinated multi-finger manipulation and deploy the collected demonstrations through open-loop replay on a physical humanoid robot. The results show that open-loop replay preserves the main temporal patterns of finger motion and task-relevant contact. Therefore, DEX-PHD provides a practical platform for portable, multimodal dexterous manipulation data collection.

</details>


### [49] [RoKON: Efficient and Adaptive Data-Enabled Predictive Control for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35458)
*Akshun Sharma,Zhongqi Wei,Buqing Ou,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文面向实时机器人任务扩展数据驱动预测控制（Data-enabled Predictive Control），用高斯核非线性预测器、阻尼高斯-牛顿优化和混合缓冲区管理，在未知摩擦变化的Franka机械臂推块任务中验证跟踪性能与计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动预测控制无需显式学习动力学模型即可构造透明的优化控制问题，但要在实时机器人中应用仍受非线性预测、计算效率和动力学变化适应性的限制。

Method: 采用基于高斯核的非线性预测器表征输入输出数据关系；提出阻尼高斯-牛顿方法，利用控制问题的最小二乘结构实现实时求解；设计混合缓冲区管理策略，在保留相关离线数据的同时引入有信息量的在线数据，使预测器适应动力学变化。

Result: 在MuJoCo中使用Franka Research 3机械臂推动表面上的块，且事先不知道变化的摩擦值；实验展示了所提方法的跟踪性能和计算适用性。

Conclusion: 所提扩展使数据驱动预测控制更适用于实时机器人任务，并能在无需显式动力学模型的情况下适应变化的动力学条件。

Abstract: Data-enabled predictive control has recently emerged as a promising control paradigm, enabling a transparent optimal-control formulation without the need to learn an explicit dynamics model. This paper presents a number of extensions paving the path towards its use in real-time robotics tasks. Following recent work, we utilize a nonlinear predictor based on Gaussian kernels to represent the relationship between input and output data. We propose a damped Gauss-Newton method that enables real-time control by efficiently exploiting the least-squares structure of the control problem. In addition, we introduce a hybrid buffer management strategy that incorporates informative online data while retaining relevant offline data, allowing the predictor to account for changes in the dynamics. We demonstrate the tracking performance and computational suitability of the proposed method in MuJoCo using a Franka Research 3 arm pushing a block across surface, without prior knowledge of its changing friction values.

</details>


### [50] [Model-Aware Generation of Safety-Critical Scenes for Robust End-to-End Driving via Distilled Planner Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35448)
*Khaled M. Seyam,Julian Wiederer,Samar Fathalah,Markus Braun,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对端到端驾驶模型在安全关键场景中频繁碰撞的问题，指出瓶颈主要在规划而非感知，并提出一种面向特定规划器的安全关键训练场景生成与微调框架，以显著降低闭环碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶模型在标准基准上表现良好，但在安全关键交互中碰撞频繁。对NeuroNCAP的失败模式分析显示，DiffusionDrive有95.9%的碰撞属于规划失败而非检测失败。作者将其归因于真实驾驶日志中安全关键交互稀少导致的数据不平衡，规划器很少接受此类训练。

Method: 提出模型感知框架：针对特定规划器生成安全关键训练场景。通过从其规划头蒸馏出的可微代理，在BEV层面攻击每个规划器，避免将昂贵视频渲染器放入攻击循环。每个规划器提取50个场景，每个场景包含一次攻击及一条安全逃逸轨迹作为微调标签。采用全自动质量-多样性搜索生成攻击与逃逸，仅渲染这些场景用于微调，从而显著降低计算成本。

Result: 在两个规划器上进行微调后，NeuroNCAP闭环碰撞率约减半：SparseDrive从28.0%降至12.2%，DiffusionDrive从37.5%降至19.2%，同时基本保持正常驾驶性能。跨规划器迁移实验表明收益来自针对特定规划器：各规划器在针对自身生成的场景上提升最大；DiffusionDrive在SparseDrive场景上微调后，仅保留其18.3个百分点碰撞率下降中的6.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 安全关键交互的数据稀缺是端到端规划的主要瓶颈之一；通过模型感知、自动生成并微调于针对特定规划器的安全关键场景，可在低计算成本下显著提升闭环安全性，且该方法具有规划器特异性。

Abstract: End-to-end driving models perform well on standard benchmarks but collide frequently in safety-critical scenarios. We present a failure-mode analysis on NeuroNCAP which shows that 95.9% of DiffusionDrive's collisions are planning failures despite reliable detection, pinpointing the bottleneck in planning rather than perception. We attribute this to a fundamental data imbalance, as safety-critical interactions are rare in real driving logs, so planners are hardly ever trained on them. We close this gap with a novel model-aware framework that generates safety-critical training scenes against a specific planner. Each planner is attacked at the bird's-eye-view (BEV) level through a differentiable surrogate distilled from its own planning head. This keeps the expensive video renderer out of the attack loop. We extract 50 scenes per planner, each pairing an attack with a safe escape trajectory that serves as the fine-tuning label. A fully automated quality-diversity search generates both the attacks and the escapes, so that their variety is not specified by hand. Only these scenes are rendered to fine-tune the planner, which reduces the computational cost significantly. Fine-tuning on these scenes roughly halves the closed-loop collision rate of two planners on NeuroNCAP (SparseDrive 28.0% to 12.2%, DiffusionDrive 37.5% to 19.2%) while largely preserving normal driving. A cross-planner transfer experiment shows that the gain comes from targeting the specific planner. Each planner improves most on the scenes generated against itself, and DiffusionDrive fine-tuned on SparseDrive's scenes retains only 6.7 of its 18.3-point collision-rate reduction.

</details>


### [51] [MATE-BT: A Behavior-Tree-Based Framework for Hierarchically Decentralized Mission Autonomy in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35429)
*Sunbeen Lee,Hoseon Gi,Jik Hur,Inmo Jang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出层次化去中心化的MATE-BT框架，将任务执行、任务分配和任务执行分离，以在机器人故障和新目标出现时保持多机器人任务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队执行预定义任务时需适应运行时变化，并在机器人故障尤其是主动领导者故障后继续任务管理。

Method: MATE-BT采用行为树进行局部规划与执行，由可转移权限的主动车载领导者管理任务进展和团队级任务分配；目标分配由可互换的去中心化方法插件完成，各机器人独立适配本地行为。通过Webots仿真和真实机器人实验验证，并在Jetson Orin Nano和Raspberry Pi 5上进行硬件在环资源测试。

Result: 实验表明在新目标和机器人故障（包括主动领导者故障）下仍保持任务连续性；物理实验中所有计算均在车载运行；仅更新受影响层而无需全局重规划时，30个机器人和120个目标下响应时间约130 ms；四种目标分配插件在任务完成时间和总行驶距离上呈现场景相关权衡。

Conclusion: MATE-BT通过分层解耦、领导者权限转移和局部行为适应，实现了可扩展且具备故障鲁棒性的多机器人任务管理，并支持资源受限平台的车载执行。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams executing predefined missions must adapt to runtime changes and sustain mission management despite robot failures. This paper proposes MATE-BT (Mission, Allocation, and Task Execution with Behavior Trees), a hierarchically decentralized framework that separates mission execution, allocation, and task execution. An active onboard leader manages mission progression and team-level task assignment, with authority transferable to another leader-capable robot after failure. Interchangeable decentralized methods resolve target assignments, while each robot adapts its local behavior through planning and acting with behavior trees. Webots simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate mission continuity despite new targets and robot failures, including active-leader failure. In the physical experiments, all MATE-BT computation runs onboard. Hardware-in-the-loop resource measurements further support onboard execution on Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 5. By updating only the affected layers without full-plan regeneration, MATE-BT maintained a response time of approximately 130 ms with 30 robots and 120 targets. Comparisons of four target-assignment plugins reveal scenario-dependent trade-offs between mission completion time and total travel distance.

</details>


### [52] [EmbodiedLab: Benchmarking Embodied Agents That Act to Know](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35385)
*Hongcheng Gao,Zelin Zheng,Alex Zhou,Jay Zhu,Shijia Ge,Yazhe Wang,Xiping Li,Jianshu Zeng,Di Wu,Xuan Shangguan,Yexing Wan,Lingyu He,Yongli Wang,Xiaohan Yu,Zhiqi Jia,Yue Liu,Bryan Hooi,Yinpeng Dong,Wentao Zhang,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 EmbodiedLab：一个让机器人通过物理实验“行动以获知”的具身科学实验基准，包含30个实验、5个学科、14类实验平台，并系统评测六个前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准只评估在给定目标状态下改变世界，科学发现基准则评估“获知”但用符号动作或数值查询替代真实物理实验；缺少同时要求智能体自主设计并执行物理实验、再从反馈中推断知识的统一评测环境。

Method: 构建基于物理仿真的实验室，每个实例重新采样系统参数，使结果无法预先知道但可精确评分；智能体接收目标和装置，自主选择流程，通过相机读取仪器并操作实验。评测同时检查智能体获得的知识（以回合中记录的读数为依据）和任务要求的物理状态，并验证所有实验可解。

Result: 在六种前沿模型及其编码智能体框架中，使用原生观测与控制时最强智能体成功率不足一半；所有智能体的多数失败发生在与实验装置交互环节；从最强智能体蒸馏出的操作技能对较弱智能体帮助更大，而感知与运动规划的增益相对较小。

Conclusion: EmbodiedLab将具身操作与科学发现统一为“行动以获知”的评测问题，揭示了当前前沿模型在物理实验交互中的显著不足，并提供了可复现的基准与评测代码。

Abstract: Embodied agents are expected to accomplish real-world goals by deciding for themselves how and when to interact with their surroundings. For many such goals, changing the physical state of the world is the means rather than the end: the agent changes the state of a system through interaction, observes the feedback, and explores until it either achieves the goal itself (e.g., discovering physical laws) or learns what the goal requires; it acts to know. Manipulation benchmarks stop at acting to change, with the target state given; scientific discovery benchmarks evaluate knowing but replace physical experimentation with symbolic actions or numerical queries. We introduce EmbodiedLab, a benchmark in which a robot must act to know. Quantitative science experiments make this setting controllable: the parameters of the system are resampled for every instance, so the result cannot be known in advance, yet it can be scored exactly. Across 30 experiments in five disciplines on 14 apparatus platforms, the agent receives an objective and the apparatus, chooses its own procedure, and operates a physics-based simulated laboratory, reading the instruments through its cameras. Evaluation checks what the agent came to know, grounded in readings recorded during the episode, as well as the physical state the task requires; every experiment is verified to be solvable. We evaluate six frontier models in their developers' coding agents with native observation and control, with added perception and motion planning, and with manipulation skills distilled from the strongest agents. With native control, even the strongest agent succeeds less than half the time; for every agent, most failures arise in interacting with the apparatus, and manipulation skills help weaker agents more than perception and planning. The benchmark and its evaluation code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [Resilient Decentralized Radar-LiDAR-Inertial Odometry in Challenging Subterranean Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35363)
*Moumita Mukherjee,Avijit Banerjee,Anton Koval,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化LiDAR-雷达-惯性里程计（LRIO）框架，利用自适应残差阈值（ART）检测传感器退化，并通过鲁棒容错最优信息滤波器（RETOIF）实现故障感知融合，从而在GPS拒止且烟雾退化的地下环境中提升定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止及烟雾、粉尘、雾等视觉退化环境中，精确 localization 仍是自主系统的关键挑战；LiDAR虽具高分辨率空间感知，但受颗粒遮挡影响性能下降，FMCW雷达在恶劣条件下更鲁棒却存在测量稀疏和离群点问题，因此需要融合LiDAR、雷达与惯性传感的互补优势。

Method: 构建去中心化LiDAR-雷达-惯性里程计框架，融合LiDAR-惯性与多路雷达-惯性里程计估计；核心采用自适应残差阈值（ART）机制自主检测时序传感器退化并选择性调整融合过程以减少不可靠测量；进一步在去中心化融合架构中使用鲁棒容错最优信息滤波器（RETOIF），以故障感知方式集成各里程计估计，增强对环境扰动和间歇性传感器故障的鲁棒性。

Result: 使用GPS拒止地下隧道中采集的真实传感器数据，在无烟和烟雾退化条件下评估。无烟实验中，RETOIF框架的定位RMSE为2.1733，传统集中式LRIO为2.9929，RMSE降低27.4%；实验结果进一步表明该框架能在烟雾导致的传感器退化下维持位姿估计。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过LiDAR、雷达和惯性传感的容错融合，提高了挑战性地下环境中位姿估计的鲁棒性，展示了其在自主导航与建图中的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate localization in GPS-denied and visually degraded environments affected by smoke, dust, or fog remains a critical challenge for autonomous systems. While LiDAR provides high-resolution spatial perception, its performance deteriorates under particulate occlusion. In contrast, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar offers robustness under such conditions but suffers from sparse measurements and outliers. This article presents a resilient decentralized LiDAR Radar Inertial Odometry (LRIO) framework that fuses the complementary strengths of LiDAR, radar, and inertial sensing to achieve robust pose estimation in challenging subterranean environments. At the core of the framework is an Adaptive Residual Thresholding (ART) mechanism that autonomously detects temporal sensor degradation and selectively adjusts the fusion process to minimize unreliable measurements. Building on this, a Robust Error-Tolerant Optimal Information Filter (RETOIF) is employed within a decentralized fusion architecture to integrate LiDAR-inertial and multiple radar-inertial odometry estimates in a fault-aware manner, enhancing resilience to environmental disturbances and intermittent sensor failures. The proposed framework is evaluated using real-world sensor data collected in GPS-denied subterranean tunnels under smoke-free and smoke-degraded conditions. In the smoke-free experiment, the proposed RETOIF framework achieves a root mean square localization error (RMSE) of 2.1733, compared with 2.9929 for the conventional centralized LRIO approach, corresponding to a 27.4\% reduction in RMSE. Experimental results further demonstrate the framework's ability to maintain pose estimation under smoke-induced sensor degradation, highlighting its potential for resilient autonomous navigation and mapping in challenging subterranean environments.

</details>


### [54] [VisGeo-PipeTrack: A Visual Geometric Servo Control Framework with MPC-RCAPID Control for UAV-Based Dynamic Pipes Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35342)
*Zhenyang Gao,Jianlin Chen,Yang Yu,Honghao Shan,Jiale Gao,Ziyan Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VisGeo-PipeTrack视觉几何约束伺服控制框架，结合YOLOv5标签检测与MPC-RCAPID控制器，实现无人机对动态管道的精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有管道巡检研究多假设管道静止，而真实工业环境中管道常存在振动等动态运动，对无人机自适应控制与计算效率提出更高要求。

Method: 利用YOLOv5检测管道标签，并基于视觉几何约束解算位置以实现高效精确定位；提出基于模型预测控制的实时校正自适应PID控制器（MPC-RCAPID），采用递归最小二乘进行实时模型估计，并结合滚动时域优化动态调整PID参数。

Result: 在AirSim仿真和Tello无人机平台验证有效性；仿真中MPC-RCAPID相比标准PID、分数阶PID、自适应PID和模糊PID具有最短调节时间3.26 s和最低平均跟踪误差3.52%；Tello实物实验中相比标准PID，在三个测试维度上平均跟踪误差分别降低55.52%、26.72%和26.52%。

Conclusion: VisGeo-PipeTrack能够在动态管道环境中实现高效自主跟踪，验证了视觉几何约束定位与MPC-RCAPID控制的有效性。

Abstract: While unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed for pipeline inspection, prevailing research predominantly assumes static pipes. In real-world industrial environments, pipes often exhibit dynamic movements, such as vibrations. This imposes more stringent requirements on UAVs' adaptive control and computational efficiency than in static scenarios. Therefore, this paper proposes a servo control framework named VisGeo-PipeTrack, which utilizes geometric constraints to enable UAV tracking of dynamic pipes. To achieve efficient UAV positioning, YOLOv5 is employed to detect labels on the pipe. The positions are derived from visual geometric constraints based on these detected labels to enable precise localization. To address tracking control challenges, a model predictive control based real-time corrected adaptive PID (MPC-RCAPID) controller is proposed. This controller employs the Recursive Least Squares method for real-time model estimation and integrates rolling horizon optimization to dynamically adjust PID parameters. Experiments conducted on both the AirSim simulator and the Tello UAV platform validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Simulation comparisons with standard PID, fractional-order PID, adaptive PID, and fuzzy PID demonstrate that MPC-RCAPID achieves the shortest settling time (3.26 s) and the lowest average tracking error (3.52%). In physical experiments on the Tello platform, compared with standard PID control, MPC-RCAPID reduces the average tracking error by 55.52%, 26.72%, and 26.52% across the three test dimensions. These results demonstrate that VisGeo-PipeTrack facilitates efficient autonomous tracking in dynamic pipe environments.

</details>


### [55] [Reward-Guided RRT* for Budgeted Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35289)
*Vinícius Araújo de Oliveira,Gabriel Ferreira Mendes Lourenço,Paulo L. J. Drews-Jr,Armando Alves Neto,Douglas G. Macharet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Reward-Guided RRT*：一种在连续状态空间中按“任务选择-运动规划”局部联合阶段增量构建预算可行任务的采样规划框架，无需预计算任务转移图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间分布任务常需在有限旅行预算下同时选择高回报位置并规划可行运动；现有方法可能依赖任务转移图或采样效率不足。

Method: 采用 reward-guided RRT* 搜索增量构建任务序列，并在每个阶段联合进行任务选择与运动规划；通过 return-feasibility 过程确保每个被选任务可在不违反任务预算的情况下访问。

Result: 在障碍复杂度和任务数增加的实验中，所得解与 VNS-PRM* 相当，但所需采样状态显著更少；进一步扩展到 Dubins 转向，并在 CoppeliaSim 中用车式机器人平台验证。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在连续状态空间中高效生成预算可行的任务方案，并可纳入车辆运动学约束，具有较好的扩展性与实际平台适用性。

Abstract: Robotic missions involving spatially distributed tasks often require jointly selecting rewarding locations and planning feasible motions under limited travel budgets. This paper proposes Reward-Guided RRT*, a new sampling-based framework that constructs a budget-feasible mission through a sequence of locally joint task-selection-and-motion-planning stages, evaluated directly in the continuous state space, without requiring a precomputed task-transition graph. The method incrementally builds a mission using reward-guided RRT* searches, while a return-feasibility procedure ensures that each selected task can be visited without violating the mission budget. Experiments with increasing obstacle complexity and number of tasks show solutions comparable to VNS-PRM* while requiring substantially fewer sampled states. We also extend the framework to Dubins steering and evaluate it in CoppeliaSim with a scaled car-like robotic platform, demonstrating its ability to incorporate vehicle kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [56] [Labference: Benchmarking the Reasoning-Action Connection in Robotic Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35252)
*Shitong Liu,Jingwei Zhang,Sheng Lian,Xinyu Zhuang,Kaiwen Guo,Quan Liu,Chunhai Zhao,Jiaxin He,Fan Yang,Manli Tao,Tingyu Yuan,Jinqiao Wang,Hongwei Shi,Chaomin Guo,Xiao-jing Liu,Xiangyu Zhu,Zhuang Xiong,Feichan Yang,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Labference 基准，用于评估机器人实验中观察到的物理结果如何影响后续操作选择，并分别衡量操作选择、物理执行与任务完成情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现面向科学的具身智能需要机器人将实验目标转化为可靠物理流程，并把语言规划与学习式操作结合；但现有基准多以总体任务完成或推理/执行指标评估，难以厘清“结果依赖的选择”与“物理执行”之间的联系。

Method: Labference 通过受控证据变化测试结果依赖的行为适应，并独立测量操作选择、物理执行和任务完成；同时从共享检查点出发，采用匹配的 Oracle 辅助续接，评估不同支持形式对后续选择和任务完成的影响。

Result: 初步称量评估显示，被测 VLA 策略虽能成功转移物体，但存在目标选择错误；GPT 驱动控制能正确选择目标，却出现执行失败和较高计算成本。

Conclusion: 研究强调需将任务适配的推理与可靠、及时的动作连接起来；Labference 使这一连接可测量、可诊断，有助于面向自主实验的具身智能科学系统改进。

Abstract: Realizing Embodied AI for Science requires robots to translate experimental goals into reliable physical procedures, reducing hazardous and repetitive human work while generating evidence for scientific discovery. Although language-based planning and learned manipulation provide complementary capabilities, their integration requires robots to adapt subsequent operations to actual execution outcomes. For example, an unsuccessful placement changes what must be done next, even when the experimental goal remains unchanged. Existing benchmarks assess planning, manipulation, and laboratory workflows through task completion and reasoning or execution metrics; however, aggregate performance leaves the connection between outcome-dependent choices and physical execution unresolved. To address this evaluation gap, we introduce Labference, a benchmark for evaluating how observed physical outcomes guide subsequent choices and how those choices translate into robotic laboratory actions. Its evaluation protocol is designed to test outcome-dependent behavioral adaptation through controlled evidence variations, while independently measuring operation selection, physical execution, and task completion. Matched Oracle-assisted continuations from shared checkpoints then assess which forms of support improve subsequent choices and task completion. Initial weighing evaluations reveal target-selection errors in the tested VLA policies despite successful object transfers, whereas GPT-driven control achieves correct selection but encounters execution failures and substantial computation costs. Together, these findings highlight the need to connect task-appropriate reasoning with reliable, timely action. By making this connection measurable and diagnosable, Labference supports targeted improvements toward autonomous experimentation for Embodied AI for Science.

</details>


### [57] [Thermal-Headroom Routing for Efficient Robot Perception in Extreme Heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35231)
*Sparsh Roy,Kevin Zhou,Arhaan Shah,Shreyan Garg,Vihaan Joshi-Apte,Maxim Kudryashov,Nanda Cheruvanthala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出热余量路由（THR），用闭式RC热模型预测处理器温度并排除会触发降频的检测器配置，以在高温环境下兼顾机器人感知的时延、能耗与截止期满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应机器人感知需在检测精度、推理时延和能耗之间权衡；高环境温度导致处理器热降频时，静态路由或固定阈值策略难以适应环境条件和热参数变化。

Method: THR是一种轻量模型选择策略：基于闭式RC热模型预测处理器温度，并排除预测会越过降频阈值的检测器配置。实验使用四种YOLO11配置、七条真实感知轨迹、八种环境温度和20个随机种子，与时延感知路由、固定阈值温控、占空比上限及匹配计算预算对照进行比较。

Result: 在45–55°C环境温度和紧推理截止期下，THR在仿真中消除降频，截止期错过率降低3.9–7.5个百分点，每准时帧能耗相对时延信念路由降低约12–13%；在按截止期节拍、非重叠帧执行实验中，错过率降低6.2–9.5个百分点。调优后的温控或占空比上限可在固定工作点匹配THR；Apple M5测量支持单节点升温近似，但未验证硬件降频。

Conclusion: 热预测路由在环境条件或校准热参数偏离静态策略调优条件时最有价值；研究刻画了热预测在资源受限机器人感知中提升效率与及时性的适用条件。

Abstract: Adaptive robot perception systems must balance detector accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption, particularly when high ambient temperatures cause processors to thermally throttle. We propose Thermal-Headroom Routing (THR), a lightweight model-selection policy that forecasts processor temperature using a closed-form RC thermal model and excludes detector configurations predicted to cross a throttling threshold. We evaluate THR with four YOLO11 configurations across seven real-world perception traces, eight ambient temperatures, and 20 randomized seeds, comparing it with latency-aware routing, fixed-threshold thermostats, duty caps, and a matched-compute-budget control. At ambient temperatures of 45-55 degrees C and a tight inference deadline, THR eliminates throttling in simulation, reduces deadline misses by 3.9-7.5 percentage points, and reduces energy per on-time frame by approximately 12-13% relative to a latency-belief router. A separate experiment using deadline-paced, nonoverlapping frame execution yields miss-rate reductions of 6.2-9.5 percentage points. A tuned thermostat or duty cap can match THR at a fixed operating point; predictive routing is most useful when ambient conditions or calibrated thermal parameters differ from those used to tune static policies. Measurements on an Apple M5 support the single-node heating approximation but do not validate hardware throttling. The results characterize the conditions under which thermal forecasting improves the efficiency and timeliness of resource-constrained robot perception.

</details>


### [58] [RetroNav: Scene-Grounded Retrospection and Retry for Self-Evolving Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35172)
*Diantao Tu,Yangdong Liu,Niannian Dai,Mengqi Rong,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RetroNav 是一个无需训练、可自进化的机器人导航框架，将导航、记忆积累与失败反思统一起来，在五项导航基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航方法多聚焦孤立任务，缺乏跨重复导航片段的知识与经验积累；已有终身导航方法主要积累场景知识，却忽视历史导航经验及其与场景证据的关联，难以反思过去错误并改进未来决策。

Method: 提出统一记忆系统，将几何与语义场景知识和历史导航经验关联，并区分客观观察与任务相关解释；每次导航尝试后由独立评估器判断是否需要重试；若需要，回溯代理利用累积场景知识与导航记录重新审视历史决策，生成基于证据的重试简报以指导下一次尝试。

Result: RetroNav 在五个导航基准上达到最先进性能，且方法将开源。

Conclusion: RetroNav 通过连接场景证据与历史导航经验并引入失败反思机制，实现了无需训练的自进化导航，验证了跨任务经验积累与回溯反思对提升导航决策的有效性。

Abstract: Existing robot navigation methods primarily focus on isolated tasks, overlooking the need to accumulate knowledge and experience across repeated navigation episodes. Although recent lifelong navigation methods have explored cross-task knowledge accumulation, they mainly focus on scene knowledge while neglecting historical navigation experience and its connection to scene evidence, limiting the ability to reflect on past mistakes and improve future decisions. To address this limitation, this paper presents RetroNav, a training-free self-evolving navigation framework that integrates navigation, memory accumulation, and failure reflection. Specifically, we develop a unified memory system that connects geometric and semantic scene knowledge with historical navigation experience, distinguishing objective observations from task-dependent interpretations. After each navigation attempt, an independent evaluator determines whether a retry is necessary. If so, a Retrospection Agent revisits historical decisions using accumulated scene knowledge and navigation records, generating an evidence-grounded retry brief to guide the next attempt. RetroNav achieves state-of-the-art performance across five navigation benchmarks. Our method will be open-sourced at this https URL .

</details>


### [59] [How Tuning, Reference Preview, Actuator Modeling, and Fallback Policies Change Quadrotor Controller Comparisons: A Reproducible Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35165)
*Xuan-Quy Dao,Le-Minh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四旋翼控制器对比中，单一跟踪误差排名并不稳健；调参协议、参考预览、回退策略与命令时序都会改变结论。作者在统一非线性被控对象上系统量化这些因素，并提出报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见四旋翼控制器比较只报告单一跟踪误差，但最终排名还受调参努力、参考信息、执行器预测、优化器回退和命令时序影响，缺乏对这些因素的量化与规范报告。

Method: 在含旋翼陀螺耦合、电机滞后和饱和的统一非线性模型上比较级联PID、悬停LQR、边界层滑模控制BL-SMC、带LQR回退的线性MPC（LMPC-F）、启用预览的LMPC-F和几何SO(3)控制器。主研究含556个50 Hz回合，结合12候选匹配调参、400个配对评估回合和48单元MPC析因实验；确认研究含1462个回合，在50个新实现上重复评估，调参预算为12、24和48个候选。

Result: 匹配12候选调参改变了固定基线下的平均RMSE排序，使BL-SMC从最后升至第一；新排序在新实现上复现，48候选预算未改变该排序。参考预览显著降低MPC跟踪误差，并在八字任务中消除回退活动。 abrupt step结果取决于回退策略、预测器与执行器权限的交互。命令时序也改变结果：回放实测命令调用延迟时，桌面计算机上4个标称MPC配置有3个终止，Raspberry Pi 5上4个全部终止；将求解器限制为100次迭代后，所测标称时序配置中所有截止期错过均被消除。

Conclusion: 控制器排名不仅取决于跟踪精度，还取决于调参协议、参考信息、回退行为和实现时序；应报告跟踪精度、调参努力、完成与失败率、回退活动、施加输入变化和命令时序。

Abstract: Quadrotor controller comparisons commonly report a single tracking error, although the resulting ranking also depends on tuning effort, reference information, actuator prediction, optimizer fallback, and command timing. We quantify these effects for cascade PID, hover LQR, boundary-layer sliding-mode control (BL-SMC), linear MPC with LQR fallback (LMPC-F), a preview-enabled LMPC-F, and a geometric $SO(3)$ controller on a common nonlinear plant with rotor gyroscopic coupling, motor lag, and saturation. A primary study (556 episodes at 50~Hz) combines matched twelve-candidate tuning, 400 paired evaluation episodes, and a 48-cell MPC factorial; a confirmation study (1462 episodes) repeats the evaluation on 50 fresh realizations with tuning budgets of 12, 24, and 48 candidates. A matched twelve-candidate tuning protocol changed the mean-RMSE ordering obtained with the fixed baseline settings, moving BL-SMC from last to first; the new ordering recurred on the fresh realizations, and a 48-candidate budget left it unchanged. Reference preview substantially reduced MPC tracking error and, on the figure-eight task, removed fallback activity. On an abrupt step, the outcome depended on the interplay between fallback policy, predictor, and actuator authority. Command timing also changed the outcome: replaying measured command-call delays terminated three of four nominal MPC configurations on a desktop computer and all four on a Raspberry Pi~5, whereas capping the solver at 100 iterations removed every deadline miss in the tested nominal timing profiles. Controller rankings therefore depend not only on tracking accuracy but also on the tuning protocol, reference information, fallback behavior, and implementation timing. We propose a reporting checklist covering tracking accuracy, tuning effort, completion and failure rates, fallback activity, applied-input variation, and command timing.

</details>


### [60] [The Language of Deformation: Semantic Failure Analysis as a Prerequisite for Dexterous Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35103)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,David Hardman,Michele Pierallini,Erfan Shahriari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出语义失效分析是推动可变形物体操作走向成熟的关键步骤，并用概念验证实验支持这一观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性物体操作已较成熟，但可变形物体（线缆、塑料件、布料等）因材料、几何和行为差异导致失效模式更多样且难以检测（如屈曲、塑性变形、压力变化、撕裂），刚性物体基于力平衡和滑移等指标的方法不足以应对。

Method: 论文识别并定义了可变形物体操作中语义失效分析的关键组成部分与挑战，并开展概念验证实验来验证其潜力。

Result: 实验结果表明语义失效分析作为研究方向具有潜力，并说明不同失效事件需要不同纠正策略。

Conclusion: 面向成熟的可变形物体操作，需要引入语义失效分析，以系统识别、解释和应对多样且难检测的失效模式。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation of rigid objects has reached a level of maturity where grasp stability, control, and planning strategies are successfully applied in industry and logistics. In contrast, manipulating deformable objects (cables, plastic pieces, cloth) remains far more challenging, as rigid-object methods do not account for their additional complexity. This paper identifies semantic failure analysis as a key step toward mature deformable-object manipulation and supports this claim with proof-of-concept experiments. Unlike rigid-object manipulation, where goals are well-defined in terms of criteria such as force balance and slippage, deformable-object manipulation involves a broader variety of failure modes arising from differences in materials, geometry, and behaviour. Typical failures include buckling, plastic deformation, pressure variations, and tearing. These diverse and often hard-to-detect events require distinct corrective strategies, making semantic failure analysis necessary. We define its key components and challenges and present experimental results supporting its potential as a research direction.

</details>


### [61] [Zero-HLM: Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation without Real Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35075)
*Dianyi Yang,Mengya Liu,Zhumei Wang,Yu Liu,Xiaochen Zhang,Yixuan Li,Le Ma,Yuhang Yang,Weiqi Huang,Wei Liang,Baoxiong Jia,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zero-HLM 提出真实到仿真再到真实（real-to-sim-to-real）的框架，利用智能手机采集与仿真源演示自动构建可物理执行的人形全身移动操作训练数据，并在 Unitree G1 上实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作（loco-manipulation）的真机示教成本高，难以规模化；仿真虽可扩展，但需要视觉逼真、物理可用且全身动作可执行的场景与参考运动。

Method: 框架包含两部分：高斯引导的智能体式真实到仿真重建，用于对齐视觉与物理场景；以及运动-环境协同自适应，利用 rollout 反馈修正全身参考运动与交互几何。基于此生成 4,800 条通过物理验证的演示，用于三个 Unitree G1 任务的零样本迁移训练。

Result: 仿真训练的 GR00T N1.7 取得 66.7% 平均全任务成功率，高于真实数据训练的 46.7%；在泛化配置上，扩展仿真训练将 pooled 成功率从真实数据基线策略的 23% 提升到 69%，且无需额外真实演示。

Conclusion: 结果表明，高质量 real-to-sim-to-real 数据生成可显著提升人形移动操作策略的零样本与泛化性能，并减少对昂贵真实示教的依赖。

Abstract: Learning generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation remains bottlenecked by costly real-world demonstrations. Simulation provides a scalable alternative, but requires visually faithful, physics-ready scenes and executable whole-body motions. We present Zero-HLM, a real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms smartphone captures and simulated source demonstrations into scalable training data for humanoid loco-manipulation. Gaussian-guided agentic real-to-sim reconstruction aligns visual and physical scenes; motion-environment co-adaptation uses rollout feedback to correct whole-body references and interaction geometry. We generate 4,800 physically validated demonstrations for zero-shot transfer across three Unitree G1 tasks. Simulation-trained GR00T N1.7 achieves 66.7% mean full-task success, versus 46.7% with real-data training. On generalized configurations, expanded simulation training raises pooled success from 23% for base-trained real-data policies to 69%, without additional real demonstrations. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [62] [PushAround: Collaborative Path Clearing via Physics-Informed Hybrid Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35062)
*Zili Tang,Zongyuan Li,Zishao Qiao,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PushAround 物理感知混合搜索，将几何连通性推理与短时仿真耦合，为移动机器人团队生成可执行的可移动障碍清理与通行走廊规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中的大型车辆常被可移动障碍阻塞；现有 NAMO 方法常抽象障碍位移，忽略机器人尺寸、障碍质量、接触几何与力，因而产生物理不可行计划。

Method: PushAround 使用重复的 W-clearance 连通图（WCCG）查询暴露需拓宽的前沿缺口，并按估计下游成本排序；搜索生成直接或递归推动候选，选择障碍、方向、接触与力，并通过物理评估接纳候选，同时结合短时仿真。

Result: 数值实验验证 WCCG 相对标准连通性检查的有效性，并展示其可扩展到更密集杂乱环境和最多四机器人团队，同时对执行侧模拟物理参数失配具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PushAround 能够在复杂杂乱环境中生成物理可执行的清障与通行规划，支持多机器人团队，并具备对物理参数失配的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large vehicles in cluttered environments are often blocked by movable obstacles, motivating mobile robot teams to clear traversable corridors. Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) approaches often abstract obstacle displacements while neglecting robot dimensions, obstacle mass, contact geometry and forces, yielding physically infeasible plans. PushAround is a physics-informed hybrid search that couples geometric connectivity reasoning with short-horizon simulation to construct executable clearing plans. Repeated W-clearance connectivity graph (WCCG) queries expose frontier gaps requiring widening and rank them by estimated downstream costs. The search generates direct or recursive pushing candidates, selecting obstacles, directions, contacts and forces, and admits physics-evaluated candidates. Numerical experiments validate WCCG against standard connectivity checks and demonstrate scaling to denser clutter and teams of up to four robots, with robustness to simulated execution-side physical-parameter mismatch.

</details>


### [63] [3D-Force Estimation for Dual-arm Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects via Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35058)
*Annika Delucchi,Vincenzo Di Paola,Bruno Marco Quadrelli,Matteo Zoppi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于因子图的概率框架，仅利用单一传感信息，在重建可变形线性物体（DLO）形状的同时估计作用其上的三维外力大小、方向与位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO具有欠驱动特性，且通常细长如电缆和导线，应变片与驱动读数等传感方式不实用；可靠接触估计对精确控制和安全操作至关重要，因此需要一种通用且适用于更广任务的外力估计技术。

Method: 采用概率 formulation，将DLO状态与传感器数据通过条件分布关联，并使用因子图确定三维作用力的幅值、方向、位置，同时重建DLO形状；方法仅使用一种传感信息，并在测量噪声和DLO沿预设路径运动的真实测试中验证。

Result: 在引入测量噪声且DLO沿指定路径受外力作用的测试中，方法能够准确估计外部作用力；摘要未给出具体定量指标。

Conclusion: 基于因子图的概率方法可在减少传感需求的情况下实现DLO形状重建与接触力估计，为DLO精确控制和安全操作提供通用可行方案。

Abstract: Estimating forces acting on Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging due to their underactuated nature. However, reliable contact estimation is crucial to precisely control them and enable safe manipulation tasks. Differently from the various approaches presented in the literature, this work adopts a probabilistic formulation where the DLO state and sensor data are related through conditional distributions. In particular, factor graphs are used to determine the magnitude, direction, and location of a 3D force acting on a DLO, while simultaneously reconstructing its shape. Since DLOs are not actuated and are typically thin, like cables and wires, strain gauges and actuation readings are usually impractical. Therefore, to overcome this issue, this work is tested using one type of sensing information only, thus developing a generic technique suited for a wider variety of tasks. Realistic tests are performed by introducing noise into the measurements while the DLO follows a prescribed path in the presence of external forces that are accurately estimated.

</details>


### [64] [VISTA: Active Exploration for Self-Improvement in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35051)
*Zhang Zhang,Shengjun Zhang,Simin Huang,Min Chen,Chong Xia,Xin Fei,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISTA 是一个面向具身智能体 harness 改进的元智能体框架，将事后 rollout 反思转为主动自我探索，并通过 harness 图复用历史假设、避免重复失败方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多将 rollout 视为静态日志用于事后反思，harness 更新缺乏在后续具身交互中的自主测试与迭代优化，难以持续提升开放动态环境中的策略表现。

Method: VISTA 维护记录 harness 谱系的 harness 图，使元智能体可检索先前假设、继承有效机制并规避失败方向；每轮从保留的 harness 版本及执行证据出发，元智能体在环境中主动生成新 rollout、探查执行失败与恢复行为，并依据证据迭代修改 harness，再将精炼后的 harness 重新评估并加入图中。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 基准上，VISTA 逐步提升任务成功率，并比静态反思更 rollout 高效，以更少仿真器 rollout 获得更高成功率。

Conclusion: 主动自我探索与 harness 图机制能有效改进具身智能体 harness，提升任务成功率与 rollout 效率，优于静态事后反思范式。

Abstract: Agentic embodied intelligence plays a foundational role in open-ended, dynamic physical environments. Rather than treating policy learning as an end-to-end mapping from instructions to actions, recent studies reveal that embodied systems depend not only on pre-trained foundation models, but also on harness. Prior approaches largely treat rollouts as static logs for post-hoc reflection, producing harness updates without autonomously testing and refining them through subsequent embodied interaction. To address this challenge, we propose VISTA, a meta-agent framework that transforms post-hoc rollout reflection into active self-exploration for embodied harness improvement. Specifically, VISTA maintains a harness graph that records the lineage of proposed harnesses, which enables the meta-agent to retrieve prior hypotheses, build on effective mechanisms, and avoid repeatedly exploring failed directions. Each iteration begins from a retained harness version and its execution evidence. Rather than directly committing an update from these historical trajectories, the meta-agent actively explores in the environment. It autonomously generates new rollouts, probes execution failures and recovery behaviors, and iteratively modifies the harness based on the resulting evidence. The refined harness is then evaluated afresh and added to the harness graph. We evaluate VISTA on the LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that VISTA progressively improves task success and is more rollout-efficient than static reflection, achieving higher success rates with fewer simulator rollouts.

</details>


### [65] [Context-dependent Runtime Construction of Safe Regions of Attraction from Design-Time Identification for Multi-Candidate Simplex Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35040)
*Georg Jäger,Nils-Jonathan Friedrich,Norman Seyffer,José Cecílio,António Casimiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于收缩稳定性和Taylor模型可达性分析的两阶段RoA认证方法，将HA控制下的闭环系统建模为切换系统，以动态构建安全RoA并安全切换HA候选，从而提升Simplex架构对高性能控制器的保护范围与恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键CPS需要兼顾高性能、适应性与严格安全保证；经典控制保守，优化/学习控制器缺乏形式化验证，现有RTA中安全滤波器会引入抖振，Simplex的许可范围受HA控制器认证吸引域紧度限制。

Method: 采用两阶段RoA认证：先用基于收缩的稳定性分析建立目标集，再用Taylor模型可达性分析认证；将HA控制下的闭环系统视为切换系统，运行时构建上下文相关的安全RoA，并在多个HA候选间安全切换以优化恢复并最大化未认证HP控制器的工作包络；在机器人路径跟踪任务上通过ODE仿真、含未建模动态的高保真Gazebo仿真和物理硬件实验三阶段验证。

Result: Simplex架构在故障注入下保持安全，并成功屏蔽行为克隆训练的HP控制器；决策模块开销可忽略，平均0.028 ms、最坏4.13 ms，低于控制周期的10%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过更紧/动态的RoA认证与安全切换机制缓解Simplex保守性，在保持形式化安全保证的同时扩展高性能控制器的可用范围，并具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Deploying safety-critical cyber-physical systems requires control strategies that reconcile high performance and adaptability with rigorous safety guarantees. Classical control strategies offer stability proofs but are conservative, while advanced optimization- and learning-based controllers achieve high performance but lack formal verification. Runtime Assurance (RTA) architectures bridge this gap by introducing their own (dis-)advantages. Safety filters use online optimization and introduce chattering, while Simplex permissiveness is bounded by the tightness of the certified Region of Attraction (RoA) of its High-Assurance (HA) controller. Targeting this conservatism, we present a two-stage RoA certification procedure leveraging contraction-based stability to establish a target set for Taylor-model-based reachability analysis. We then treat the closed-loop system under HA control as a switched system, enabling: (a) runtime construction of context-dependent safe RoA and (b) safe switching between HA candidates to optimize recovery and maximize the operating envelope of the uncertified High-Performance (HP) controller. We validate this framework on a robotic path-tracking task using a three-stage evaluation: Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) simulation for theoretical verification, high-fidelity Gazebo simulation under unmodeled dynamics, and experiments on physical hardware. Results show that the Simplex architecture maintains safety under fault injection and successfully shields a behavioral-cloning-trained HP controller. Crucially, the decision module introduces negligible overhead (0.028 ms average, 4.13 ms worst-case), consuming under 10 % of the control period.

</details>


### [66] [JevVLA: Jev-Inspired Energy-Based Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35010)
*Wei Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Zheqi Lv,Zheng Dong,Yingjie Niu,Jiaqi Zhu,Jun Xiao,Chao Li,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量能量模型JevVLA，在冻结预训练VLA策略的前提下，用统一的可学习动作偏好函数同时改进离散候选选择与连续动作校正/生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略既可对离散候选做选择，也可生成连续动作块进行运动控制；需要在保留预训练策略能力的同时，用共同机制提升这两类决策，并受Jev结构化概率决策接口启发。

Method: JevVLA冻结基础策略，学习一个以观测、任务上下文和动作为条件的可微标量能量，利用执行反馈、专家纠正或演示偏好训练；该单一动作偏好函数用能量值比较离散候选，用动作梯度校正连续动作或引导其生成，从而共享一个约8万可训练参数的核心决策网络，各基准独立训练权重并固定输入输出编码。

Result: 在留出测试集上，相比各自匹配的基础策略控制，JevVLA在MetaWorld MT50、VIMA、LanguageTable、LIBERO Plus和CLIPort上分别提升任务成功率1.28、2.31、1.60、1.52和1.28个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种轻量、Jev启发的能量模型用于同时适配离散动作选择与连续动作引导的可行性，并计划开源JevVLA实现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can select among discrete candidates for pick-and-place manipulation or generate continuous action chunks for motion control. Improving both forms of decision making through a common mechanism while retaining the capabilities of a pretrained policy is an important adaptation problem. Inspired by Jev's structured, probabilistic decision interface, we propose JevVLA, a lightweight energy-based adaptation framework for VLA policies. JevVLA instantiates this inspiration by modeling the relative quality of action proposals: with the base policy frozen, it learns a differentiable scalar energy conditioned on observations, task context, and actions, using execution feedback, expert corrections, or demonstration-derived preferences. JevVLA uses this single learned action preference function in two ways: its values compare discrete candidates, while its action gradients correct continuous actions or guide their generation. Candidate selection and continuous control thus share one trainable decision core. Each benchmark uses independently trained weights and fixed input/output encodings, while retaining the same core network with approximately 80,000 trainable parameters. On the held-out test sets, JevVLA improves task success over the respective matched base-policy controls by 1.28, 2.31, 1.60, 1.52, and 1.28 percentage points on MetaWorld MT50, VIMA, LanguageTable, LIBERO Plus, and CLIPort, respectively. These results support the feasibility of a lightweight, Jev-inspired energy model for adapting both discrete action selection and continuous action guidance. The JevVLA implementation will be open-sourced at this https URL .

</details>


### [67] [Learning Direct Control from Optimization for Dynamic Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34929)
*Bing Sun,Yue Lin,Canhui Wen,Xin Chen,Yang Liu,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LECO 将动态目标跟踪的轨迹优化转化为离线学习直接控制策略，并结合可微运动学、课程学习与部署时安全回退，实现平滑、鲁棒且实时的移动机器人目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态目标跟踪要求机器人在避障同时保持目标可见，并产生平滑可行控制。在线轨迹优化需反复数值求解，计算负担重；基于路点的神经策略又将几何预测与控制执行分离，难以直接用轨迹级目标指导控制学习。

Method: 提出 LECO 框架：策略预测控制序列，经可微运动学模型映射为轨迹，使轨迹级跟踪与平滑目标直接监督控制学习；采用衰减专家引导课程，先初始化有效跟踪行为，再逐步转向任务目标；部署时使用预测安全锁检查短时域障碍物 clearance，在预测运动不安全或目标预测无效时调用经典规划与控制回退。

Result: 在三种模拟障碍密度下，LECO 相比该指标最佳对比方法将均方根线性 jerk 降低 14.1--31.2%，并获得最高跟踪成功率；单周期计算时间为 4.25--5.63 ms。两个机器人平台实验进一步验证了室内狭窄通道和室外森林环境中的跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从优化中学习直接控制策略能够实现平滑、鲁棒且实时的动态目标跟踪，并具备跨场景部署潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking requires a mobile robot to maintain target visibility while navigating obstacles with smooth, feasible controls. Online trajectory optimization explicitly encodes these objectives but requires repeated numerical solving, whereas waypoint-based neural policies separate geometric prediction from control execution. We introduce LECO, a framework that transfers tracking optimization into offline learning of a direct-control policy. LECO predicts control sequences and maps them to trajectories through a differentiable kinematic model, allowing trajectory-level objectives to directly guide control learning. A decaying expert-guidance curriculum initializes effective tracking behavior and progressively shifts learning toward these task objectives. At deployment, a predictive safety lock checks short-horizon obstacle clearance and invokes a classical planning and control fallback when the predicted motion is unsafe or the target prediction is invalid. Across three simulated obstacle densities, LECO reduces root-mean-square linear jerk by 14.1--31.2\% relative to the best competing method for this metric and achieves the highest tracking success rate, with per-cycle computation times of 4.25--5.63\,ms. Experiments on two robot platforms further demonstrate tracking through indoor narrow passages and outdoor forest environments. These results demonstrate that learning direct control from optimization enables smooth and robust dynamic target tracking.

</details>


### [68] [Quantile-Based Command-Reserve MPC for Current-Command Gimbal Pointing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34913)
*Yaokun Lu,Yifan Li,Enmei Wang,Hao Teng,Pengwei Hu,Jianzhong Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究两轴电流指令万向节在失配机械扰动下的受限视线指向控制，提出分位数扰动预测器与管式模型预测控制相结合的方法，在嵌入式平台上实现低指向误差且评估窗口内不触发指令限幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦、力矩波动、电流响应滞后、模型失配及驱动指令受限会降低两轴万向节指向精度，需在存在可预测加速度残差与剩余不确定性的条件下，同时进行扰动补偿和指令裕量管理。

Method: 提出分位数扰动预测器，从状态、指令和参考历史中学习中位数与外部分位数，以补偿可预测加速度残差并刻画剩余不确定性；提出受管式思想启发的模型预测控制器，利用校准半径和分阶段输入裕量按预测不确定性预留指令余量，且不增加优化变量；针对指定线性误差模型和固定辅助策略建立有限时域管包含结果，并用原生指令边界与最终指令限幅器保证下发指令在给定范围内。

Result: 在两轴嵌入式平台上对四种控制器配置各重复七次试验，所提配置取得二维指向均方根误差3.94±0.10 mrad，且评估窗口内无指令限幅样本；同时单独报告了标称100 Hz实现下的求解器时间和循环超时记录。

Conclusion: 测试工况下的性能支持将残差预测与自适应指令裕量结合使用，用于受限两轴万向节的高精度视线指向控制。

Abstract: This paper studies the constrained line-of-sight pointing control problem of a two-axis current-command gimbal subjected to mismatched mechanical disturbances. Friction, torque ripple, current-response lag, model mismatch, and limited drive commands make accurate pointing more challenging yet interesting. A quantile disturbance predictor is proposed to compensate predictable acceleration residuals and describe their remaining uncertainty, with the aid of learning median and outer quantiles from state, command, and reference histories. A tube-inspired model predictive controller is proposed to reserve command headroom according to prediction uncertainty, with the aid of calibrated radii and stage-dependent input margins. The median improves the nominal prediction, while the radius adapts the command reserve without adding optimization variables. A finite-horizon tube inclusion result is established for a specified linear error model and fixed ancillary policy; its conditions are distinguished from the approximations used in hardware. Native command bounds and a final command limiter keep the sent commands within their prescribed range. On a two-axis embedded platform, four controller configurations are compared in seven repeated trials each. The proposed configuration achieves a two-dimensional pointing root-mean-square error of $3.94 \pm 0.10$ mrad with no command-limit samples in the evaluation windows. Its performance supports the combined use of residual prediction and adaptive command margins in the tested operating condition. Solver times and logged loop overruns are reported separately for the nominal 100 Hz implementation.

</details>


### [69] [MMGNet: Multi-Modal 6-DoF Grasp Synthesis for Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34893)
*Rui Cao,Jia-Hui Pan,Yichuan Li,Yunfan Yu,Chi-Wing Fu,Jiangliu Wang,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态抓取合成网络MMGNet，通过层次化图像到3D特征注入与旋转抓取性建模，提升场景级6-DoF抓取合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实点云常含噪声、不完整且过度平滑，而RGB图像虽提供物体区域、边界和外观线索，但在场景表示与局部抓取推理中的有效利用受限于两个瓶颈。

Method: MMGNet采用层次化图像到3D特征注入，将多尺度图像特征贯穿3D表示形成过程；同时联合预测抓取接近方向与平面内旋转的旋转抓取性，并通过grasp-shaded grouping组织局部图像引导特征。

Result: 消融实验表明两个模块具有互补增益；在GraspNet-1Billion RealSense上平均AP达60.34，超过所有对比方法；在对抗性抓取物体的真实实验中，抓取成功率较AnyGrasp提升8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: MMGNet有效融合图像与点云信息并建模旋转抓取性，在场景级6-DoF抓取合成与真实机器人抓取中均取得优于对比方法的性能。

Abstract: Grasping is a foundational capability in robotic manipulation. Scene-level 6-DoF grasp synthesis relies on point clouds for metric geometry, yet real-world point observations are often noisy, incomplete, and over-smoothed. RGB images provide object-region, boundary, and appearance cues, yet two bottlenecks limit their effective use in scene representation and local grasp reasoning. We present MMGNet, a Multi-Modal Grasp Synthesis Network that addresses these bottlenecks through hierarchical image-to-3D feature injection and rotational graspness modeling. Hierarchical injection integrates multi-scale image features throughout 3D representation formation, while the rotational graspness jointly predicts the grasp approach direction and in-plane rotation before organizing local image-informed features through grasp-shaded grouping. Ablation studies show that these two modules provide complementary gains. MMGNet achieves a mean AP of 60.34 on GraspNet-1Billion with RealSense, surpassing all compared methods. In real-world experiments with adversarial grasp objects, MMGNet improves the grasp success rate over AnyGrasp by 8.46 percentage points. \emph{Code, models, and data will be released upon publication.}

</details>


### [70] [Localization-Uncertainty-Aware UAV Swarm Search for GNSS-Denied Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34873)
*Siyuan Yang,Junning Lyu,Xiaoyu He,Lakmal Seneviratne,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向GNSS拒止海上环境的无人机集群搜索框架，通过Fisher信息矩阵近似多跳协同定位中的级联协方差，并将不确定性纳入控制屏障函数以保障连通性、避碰和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止海上场景中，无人机间测距是协同定位的关键，但定位不确定性会沿多跳参考链传播，进而影响集群连通性、碰撞避免与搜索有效性，因此需要不确定性感知的安全控制方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的控制屏障函数（CBF）框架：建立级联协方差模型刻画测距噪声与邻居不确定性沿定位链的累积；构造不确定性收紧的CBF，将协方差及其变化率纳入连通性保持与避碰裕度，并给出条件不变性与性能优雅退化界；采用分层Voronoi目标策略并结合编队保持实现高效前向覆盖；分布式控制器在回路中接入标准无偏扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行验证。

Result: 框架在多种多边形形状及6至30架小型无人机规模下演示；估计器在回路运行302.5秒完成搜索；20次蒙特卡洛部署均重复完成，所有局部QP可行，至少98.97%的无人机帧满足包含约束；6次MBZIRC运行提供估计器在回路证据；源代码公开。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在GNSS拒止海上环境中实现不确定性感知的协同搜索，在维持连通性与避碰安全的同时支持可重复的高效覆盖，并具备实际集群规模与实验验证支持。

Abstract: This paper studies UAV swarm search in GNSS-denied maritime environments, where inter-UAV ranging drives cooperative localization and uncertainty propagates along the multi-hop reference chain, affecting connectivity, collision avoidance, and search effectiveness. We propose an uncertainty-aware control barrier function (CBF) framework approximating cascading localization covariance through Fisher information matrices. First, the cascading covariance model captures how ranging noise and neighbor uncertainty accumulate along the localization chain. Second, uncertainty-tightened CBFs incorporate the covariance model and its rate into margins for connectivity maintenance and collision avoidance, with conditional invariance and graceful-degradation bounds. Third, a hierarchical Voronoi-target strategy with formation keeping supports efficient forward-looking coverage. The distributed controller is validated with a standard unbiased extended Kalman filter in the loop. The framework is demonstrated on several polygon shapes and swarm sizes from 6 to 30 sUAVs. An estimator-in-the-loop run completes the search in 302.5 s, 20 Monte Carlo deployments confirm repeatable completion with all local QPs feasible and containment in at least 98.97% of UAV-frames, and six MBZIRC runs provide estimator-in-the-loop evidence. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [Physics-Informed and Data-Driven Composite Disturbance Separation and Predictive Control for Airborne Electro-Optical Gimbals with Multiple Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34849)
*Yaokun Lu,Yifan Li,Zhixuan Zhang,Enmei Wang,Hao Teng,Jianzhong Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机载光电云台在载体振动、输入延迟和执行器约束下的复合周期/非周期扰动，提出一种扰动分离的模型预测控制方案，实现视轴稳定与跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载体振动、输入延迟与执行器约束并存时，周期振动和非周期扰动特性不同，传统统一处理易造成误差放大，难以兼顾视轴稳定与约束下的指向精度。

Method: 按扰动物理特性分离处理：自适应周期观测器利用线性回归频率估计预测多谐波振动并嵌入MPC预测模型；线性观测器估计非周期扰动，有界LSTM预测其在输入延迟内的变化，并在优化器外进行补偿；同时采用输入集收紧保证指令可行，并在显式输入序列与预测状态裕度条件下建立区域实用跟踪界。

Result: 搭建无人机搭载两轴云台平台进行室内悬停对比实验，并开展频率变化和参数失配仿真；所提方法偏航和俯仰相对角RMSE分别为0.770度和0.350度，相较线性自抗扰控制分别降低25.1%和45.9%。

Conclusion: 扰动分离的MPC方案能抑制周期与非周期异质扰动、降低非周期估计误差放大的影响，并在约束和延迟条件下提升光电云台指向跟踪精度与可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies the constrained pointing control problem of airborne electro-optical gimbals subjected to composite periodic and aperiodic disturbances. Under carrier vibration, input delay, and actuator constraints, achieving line-of-sight stabilization and tracking is challenging. A disturbance-separated model predictive control (MPC) scheme is proposed to compensate these heterogeneous disturbances according to their physical characteristics. An adaptive periodic observer predicts multi-harmonic vibrations over the MPC horizon using linear-regression frequency estimation. A linear observer estimates the aperiodic disturbance, and a bounded long short-term memory (LSTM) network predicts its change over the input delay. Embedding periodic predictions into the MPC model while applying aperiodic compensation outside the optimizer limits amplification of aperiodic estimation errors and improves pointing accuracy under frequency changes and parameter mismatch. On the stated headroom envelope, input-set tightening provides feasible commands within the prescribed limits, and a regional practical tracking bound is established under explicit input-sequence and predicted-state margin conditions. A UAV-mounted two-axis gimbal platform is assembled for comparative indoor hovering experiments, complemented by simulations under frequency changes and parameter mismatch. In the reported trials, the proposed method achieves yaw and pitch relative-angle root-mean-square errors (RMSEs) of 0.770 degrees and 0.350 degrees, reducing tracking errors by 25.1% and 45.9% compared with linear active disturbance rejection control.

</details>


### [72] [Behavioural Quality Is Not Learning Utility: Objective Mismatch in Human Behaviour Models for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34839)
*Haoyang Jiang,Dana Kulic,Michael Burke,Pamela Carreno-Medrano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出，用人类行为模型训练机器人时，现有行为相似度指标未必能反映模型作为训练伙伴的价值；在高速公路驾驶和物体交接任务中，这些指标与下游强化学习策略性能不一致，且对轨迹表示敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要看生成行为是否接近真实人类数据，却较少评估其对下游机器人策略学习的实用性，因此存在评估目标与训练目标错配的问题。

Method: 在高速公路驾驶和物体交接两个人类-机器人交互任务中，使用常见生成模型评估指标评估多类人类行为模型，并考虑多种人类轨迹表示，比较这些评估结果与使用相应模型训练出的强化学习策略的下游性能。

Result: 现有行为评估指标不能稳定预测下游策略性能，跨领域和轨迹表示时不一致，且评估结果对轨迹表示选择敏感；即使已知真实测试行为分布，训练于不同分布也可能比直接训练于真实分布获得更好的下游表现。

Conclusion: 应面向下游训练效用评估人类行为模型，而不能仅依赖行为相似度；轨迹表示和训练分布选择会显著影响机器人策略学习效果。

Abstract: Human behaviour models are increasingly used to provide simulated human interactions for training robots via reinforcement learning (RL). Existing approaches primarily evaluate these models according to how closely their generated behaviours resemble real human data, while their usefulness as training partners is less commonly assessed. This creates a potential objective mismatch: a model that performs well according to behavioural evaluation metrics may not necessarily provide useful training experiences for downstream robot policy learning. Across two human-robot interaction (HRI) domains, highway driving and object handover, we evaluate different types of human behaviour models using common generative-model evaluation metrics. We consider multiple human trajectory representations and compare the resulting evaluations with the downstream performance of RL policies trained using the corresponding models. We find that existing behavioural evaluation metrics do not consistently predict downstream policy performance across domains and trajectory representations, while their evaluations are sensitive to the choice of trajectory representation. We also show that even when the true testing behaviour distribution is known, training against a different distribution may yield better downstream performance than training directly against the true distribution.

</details>


### [73] [ISB-NMPC: Interception-Oriented Spherical-Bearing Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34789)
*Li Hong,Linkai Liu,Xiangyu Ren,Shuli Lv,Chen Min,Chunjiang Wu,Yujun Zhang,Dingchen Zeng,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向拦截的球面方位非线性模型预测控制（ISB-NMPC），将球面方位预测与四旋翼非线性动力学耦合，联合优化接近目标与保持可见性；30组配对仿真拦截成功率86.7%，平均目标可见时间99.11%，并在户外实现约150 m处捕获30 cm目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搭载捷联相机的四旋翼拦截器成本低，但机体机动与相机指向强耦合，导致快速接近目标与维持目标可见性之间存在冲突。

Method: ISB-NMPC将球面方位预测与非线性四旋翼动力学结合，联合优化目标导向的接近运动与可见性；采用S^2上的姿态补偿扩展卡尔曼滤波估计目标方位运动、补偿感知延迟并预测目标方位，以引导尺度自适应检测。

Result: 在30组配对仿真试验中，ISB-NMPC拦截成功率为86.7%，为所评估控制器中最高，单次试验平均目标可见时间达99.11%；户外飞行在约150 m处捕获30 cm目标，远程飞行拦截器速度达17 m/s，追踪四旋翼拖曳机动目标时约14 m/s。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该方案能在低成本捷联相机配置下兼顾快速拦截与目标可见性，并具备实际户外拦截应用潜力；软件与硬件均已开源。

Abstract: Quadrotor interceptors equipped with strapdown cameras offer a low-cost solution for physically countering unauthorized UAVs. However, this configuration inherently couples vehicle maneuvering and camera pointing, creating a conflict between rapid closing and maintaining target visibility. We present interception-oriented spherical-bearing nonlinear model predictive control (ISB-NMPC), which couples spherical-bearing prediction with nonlinear quadrotor dynamics to jointly optimize target-directed closing motion and visibility. An attitude-compensated extended Kalman filter on S^2 estimates target bearing motion, compensates for perception delay, and predicts target bearings to guide scale-adaptive detection. Across 30 paired simulation trials, ISB-NMPC achieves an interception success rate of 86.7%, the highest among the evaluated controllers, with a mean per-trial target-visible time of 99.11%. Outdoor flights demonstrate acquisition of a 30 cm target at approximately 150 m, with interceptor speed reaching 17 m/s in the long-range flight and approximately 14 m/s while pursuing a quadrotor-towed maneuvering target. The software and hardware are open source at this https URL and this https URL , respectively.

</details>


### [74] [SceneAgent: An Agentic Pipeline from 3D and 4D Captures to Simulation Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34745)
*Luke Hollis,Tianxing Fan,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneAgent 是一个基于仿真器视觉检查与修复的智能体流水线，可将多种视觉/3D捕捉转换为可交互仿真场景，并生成用于策略学习的物体变体与演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉捕捉能记录详细场景外观，但转化为可交互仿真环境仍需物体重建、物理建模并修正中间误差；自动协调这些步骤具有挑战性。

Method: SceneAgent 处理3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）、摄影测量、LiDAR、AI生成3DGS场景及4D动态视频，集成物体分割、物理参数估计、部件分解、铰接建模与可变形仿真，并通过仿真器中的视觉检查与修复来协调流程；同时生成“数字姐妹”物体变体和脚本化演示以支持策略学习。
