Result: 在GraspClutter6D的杂乱料箱场景中，SceneAgent使每个有可评分输出的编码智能体在1 cm处F1提升（无论是否使用其技能）；所有智能体配置均超过SimFoundry，最佳为60.75%对6.9%。在4DCodeBench的三个动态场景中，将Gemini 4 Argon的平均动态IoU从0.86%提升至99.09%。还展示了多样仿真交互及初步的真实机器人策略迁移。

Conclusion: SceneAgent表明，基于仿真器视觉反馈的智能体化重建-修复流水线能显著提升可交互仿真构建质量，并支撑策略学习与初步真实机器人迁移。

Abstract: Visual captures provide detailed scene appearance, but converting them into interactive simulation environments requires object reconstruction, physical modeling, and correction of intermediate errors. We present SceneAgent, an agentic pipeline that coordinates these steps through simulator-based visual inspection and repair. The pipeline processes 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), photogrammetry, LiDAR captures, AI-generated 3DGS scenes, and 4D videos of dynamic scenes, and combines object segmentation, physical parameter estimation, part decomposition, articulation, and deformable simulation. To support policy learning, SceneAgent generates diverse object variants ("digital sisters") and scripted demonstrations. On a cluttered bin scene from GraspClutter6D, SceneAgent raises F1 at 1 cm for every coding agent with scorable outputs both with and without its skills, and its reconstructions are better than SimFoundry: all agent configurations exceed SimFoundry's F1 score, with a best of 60.75% versus 6.9%. On three dynamic scenes from 4DCodeBench, SceneAgent raises the mean dynamic IoU of Gemini 4 Argon from 0.86% to 99.09%. We further demonstrate diverse simulated interactions and preliminary policy transfer to a real robot.

</details>


### [75] [Networked Humanoid Intelligence: Communication, Coordination, Collaboration, and Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34740)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Huy Q. Le,Ji Su Yoon,Nguyen H. Tran,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述将人形机器人视为联网群体系统，围绕通信、协调、协作与社交智能四类信息交换功能，系统梳理网络化人形智能的研究现状、方法维度、硬件数据基准、风险与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人正从实验室单机平台走向共享工作空间、基础设施、任务与知识的联网集群，其运行与改进依赖网络信息交换；但已有研究多将人形机器人作为独立系统考察，缺乏对网络化人形智能的系统认识。

Method: 文章建立网络化人形系统基础，定义四类信息交换功能的问题，并用统一工厂场景串联；按交互架构、交换信息、物理耦合、运行时间尺度、系统规模和具身形态六个维度综述文献；区分物理/仿真人形机器人结果与基于其他机器人平台或无身体智能体的结果，并梳理硬件、数据集、基准及本地风险与联网引入风险。

Result: 综述表明，网络化人形智能需同时考虑平衡、全身运动、接触和物理交互等人形特有限制；其他机器人平台或无身体智能体研究不能充分刻画这些约束。文章还识别了过期信息、不安全知识传播等联网风险，并归纳了控制感知网络、紧凑以身体为中心的消息、物理 grounded 协调、网络与具身感知知识共享、群体级社会一致性和可问责人类监督等开放问题。

Conclusion: 未来研究应从单机人形机器人转向网络化群体智能，在控制、通信、协调、知识共享与社会交互之间建立统一框架，并重视人形具身约束、联网风险与人类监督机制。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are moving from laboratory platforms toward connected fleets that share workspaces, infrastructure, tasks, and knowledge with other robots and humans. As deployments grow, the operation and improvement of each humanoid depend on information exchanged across the network. However, existing research has largely studied humanoids as individual systems. This survey examines networked humanoid intelligence through four functions of information exchange: communication, coordination, collaboration, and social intelligence with human interaction. We first establish the foundations of networked humanoid systems, define the question addressed by each function, and connect them through a common factory scenario. We review the literature along six dimensions: interaction architecture, exchanged information, physical coupling, operating timescale, system scale, and embodiment. We separate results obtained with physical and simulated humanoids from those based on other robotic platforms or disembodied agents, because the latter do not fully capture humanoid-specific constraints such as balance, whole-body motion, contact, and physical interaction. We examine hardware, datasets, and benchmarks, and distinguish risks handled locally from those networking introduces, such as stale information or unsafe knowledge propagation. Finally, we identify open problems in control-aware networking, compact body-centric messages, physically grounded coordination, network- and embodiment-aware knowledge sharing, fleet-level social consistency, and accountable human oversight.

</details>


### [76] [VLA-HWBench: Where the Time Goes When VLA Models Run on Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34717)
*Borui Xu,Hongshi Tan,Wenqi Jiang,Bingsheng He,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文系统评测18个开源VLA模型在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor与AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395上的实时推理表现，采用七阶段拆解，指出动作生成策略比模型规模更决定延迟，并给出边缘VLA软硬件设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型 increasingly 部署到机器人边缘设备，推理延迟直接约束闭环控制；但如何联合设计VLA模型、推理软件与边缘硬件以实现实时推理尚不清晰，且现有工作多只报告任务成功率、动作生成策略覆盖有限、推理阶段划分粗、缺乏同平台优化对比。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor和AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395上，对18个开源VLA模型及已发布/替代后端进行基准测试，并将推理拆分为七个阶段，比较动作生成策略、模型规模、内核融合、4-bit权重、CPU预处理等因素对延迟的影响。

Result: 发现三点：1）动作生成策略比模型规模更重要，在19个Jetson配置中主导14个，延迟在Jetson上相差82倍、在AMD上相差157倍，参数量最多解释15%的延迟方差；少步流匹配生成长动作块可满足控制，逐token解码不行。2）内核融合加4-bit权重收益最大，使π0.5和GR00T N1.7比PyTorch快2.6--2.9倍，但仅支持这两个模型且仅在Jetson上；优化后CPU预处理占GR00T N1.7调用时间从12%升至39%。3）优化后，Jetson上内存带宽仅限制处理少量token的阶段（如去噪步骤），其他阶段主要受软件限制，包括未融合内核和主机CPU串行预处理。

Conclusion: 低延迟边缘VLA应采用少迭代的动作生成；推理软件应将内核融合和低比特权重扩展到更多模型与平台；边缘硬件需要高内存带宽和快速主机CPU。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models increasingly run on edge devices aboard robots, where inference latency constrains closed-loop robot control. However, it remains unclear how to design VLA models, their inference software and edge hardware so that robots can run VLA inference in real time. Most VLA models report only task success, while prior studies cover limited action-generation strategies, divide inference into at most three stages, and lack comparisons of inference optimizations on the same this http URL 18 open VLA models across released and alternative backends on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor and AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 395, with a seven-stage inference breakdown. Our study reveals three insights. First, action-generation strategy matters more than model size. It dominates 14 of 19 Jetson configurations, latency varies by $82\times$ on Jetson and $157\times$ on AMD, and parameter count explains at most 15% of latency variance. Models that generate long action chunks in a few flow-matching steps keep up with control, whereas token-by-token decoding does not. Second, kernel fusion with 4-bit weights helps most, running $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7 2.6--2.9$\times$ faster than PyTorch with \texttt{ this http URL }, but it exists for only these two models and only on the Jetson. Optimization also moves the time to the stages it shortens least, as CPU preprocessing grows from 12\% to 39\% of the GR00T N1.7 call. Third, after optimization, memory bandwidth limits only the stages that process few tokens on the Jetson, such as the denoising steps, whereas software limits all other stages, from unfused kernels to serial preprocessing on the host CPU. Overall, low-latency edge VLAs should use few action-generation iterations, inference software should extend kernel fusion and low-bit weights to more models and platforms, and edge hardware needs high memory bandwidth and a fast host CPU.

</details>


### [77] [Resource-Performance Trade-offs in Intra-Aisle Robotic Picking: A Discrete-Event Simulation Comparison of Shuttle Configurations With and Without an Onboard Tote Lift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34708)
*Mohsen Reda Mahmoud Moursi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究用离散事件仿真比较两种已实现的通道内机器人拣选配置：无车载周转箱升降（两台层专用穿梭车）与有车载升降（一台穿梭车跨相邻层作业）。结果显示车载升降配置在所有任务规模下平均周期时间更长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两种两层通道内机器人拣选配置的周期性能，并量化从两台专用穿梭车减少为一台穿梭车时的性能差异。

Method: 采用离散事件仿真，设置30个配置-任务规模实验（n=1-15个位置，每个10次重复），获得300个重复级周期时间观测；对重复均值使用双侧Welch检验，并用Holm法校正15次比较。

Result: 车载周转箱升降配置在所有测试任务规模下平均周期时间均更长；n=15时均值分别为299.477 s/cycle和333.231 s/cycle，增加33.753 s（11.27%），对应派生物品率为180.314和162.050 items/h（-10.13%）。这些速率按3600n除以场景平均周期时间计算，并非两台层专用穿梭车并发能力的测量；且两种配置的任务位置来自不同的合格层集合，因此结果针对“配置+任务生成”组合，而非相同拣选位置下5秒升降操作的孤立效应。

Conclusion: 研究量化了测试两层区块内周期性能差异及由两台专用穿梭车缩减为一台的影响，但未量化系统级最大吞吐量、投资成本或可靠性。

Abstract: This study compares two implemented intra-aisle robotic-picking configurations through discrete-event simulation. Without an onboard tote lift, two level-captive shuttles serve the two tested levels; with the lift, one shuttle remains on its horizontal level and its onboard mechanism moves the order tote to allow picks from the adjacent upper level. Thirty configuration-task-size experiments (n = 1-15 locations; ten replications each) yield 300 replication-level cycle-time observations. Two-sided Welch tests are applied to the replication means, with Holm adjustment across the 15 comparisons. The onboard-tote-lift configuration has longer mean cycle time at all tested task sizes. At n = 15, the means are 299.477 and 333.231 s/cycle, respectively: a 33.753 s increase (11.27%). The corresponding cycle-time-derived item rates are 180.314 and 162.050 items/h (-10.13%). These rates are calculated as 3600n divided by the scenario-mean cycle time; they are not measurements of the combined concurrent capacity of two level-captive shuttles. Importantly, task locations are sampled from different eligible-level sets in the two configurations, so the result concerns the implemented configuration-and-task-generation packages, not the isolated effect of a five-second tote-lift operation under identical picked locations. The study quantifies a cycle-performance difference alongside a reduction from two dedicated shuttles to one within the tested two-level block; it does not quantify system-level maximum throughput, investment cost, or reliability.

</details>


### [78] [Benchmarking Obstacle-Conditioned Safety in Household Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34674)
*Hyunwoong Bae,Jisu Han,Jiyeon Choi,Dohoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出OSMA-Bench基准与语义敏感度指数SSI，评估VLM/VLA导航策略是否将障碍物语义安全差异转化为连续运动中的间隙、速度与平滑性调整；结果表明现有策略能识别障碍安全相关性，却不能稳定反映到运动参数或动作中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全家庭导航需要机器人根据障碍物类型及距离调节间隙和速度，避免不必要保守；但现有评测未检验语义安全区分是否体现在连续运动控制中，存在语义到运动接地的评估缺口。

Method: 构建OSMA-Bench，即障碍物条件安全运动适配基准，使用仅在障碍物类型上不同、空间匹配的场景集；提出语义敏感度指数SSI，衡量间隙及距离相关速度和平滑性是否符合障碍物谨慎排序；评测VLM与VLA策略，并分析显式安全提示及在障碍识别与导航程序生成之间加入参数推断步骤的效果。

Result: VLM与VLA策略即使有显式安全提示，也不能一致地根据障碍物安全性调整运动；策略可正确区分障碍物的安全相关性，却未将区分反映到运动参数或动作中；对先识别障碍再生成导航程序的VLM策略，加入显式参数推断步骤可改善适配。

Conclusion: 研究揭示语义到运动接地的差距，强调机器人需将语义层面的谨慎判断转化为适当安全的连续运动，而非仅完成障碍语义识别或依赖安全提示。

Abstract: Safe household navigation requires robots to regulate clearance and speed according to what an obstacle is and how close it is, without unnecessary conservatism. Yet existing evaluations do not test whether semantic safety distinctions are reflected in continuous motion. To that end, we introduce OSMA-Bench, an Obstacle-Conditioned Safe Motion Adaptation Benchmark built on spatially matched scenario sets that differ only in obstacle type. We also propose the \emph{Semantic Sensitivity Index} (SSI), which measures whether clearance and distance-dependent speed and smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles. Evaluating VLM-based and vision-language-action (VLA) policies on the benchmark, we find that neither consistently adapts its motion to obstacle safety, even with explicit safety prompts. Our analysis shows that the policies can correctly distinguish obstacles by safety relevance but fail to reflect this distinction in the motion parameters or actions they produce. For VLM-based policies, which recognize the obstacle and then generate a navigation program, adding an explicit parameter-inference step between these two stages improves adaptation. These findings expose a gap in \emph{semantic-to-motion grounding}, highlighting the need to translate semantic caution into appropriately safe motion.

</details>


### [79] [Dream to Assist: Intent-Conditioned World Models for Shared Vehicle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34650)
*Jonathan DeCastro,Thomas Balch,Emily Sumner,Deepak Gopinath,Laporsha Dees,Patricio Reyes Gomez,Elliot Weiss,Megan Applegate-Kenton,Todd Rowell,John Leonard,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Assist 是一个面向紧密耦合共享决策的基于模型强化学习框架，通过世界模型、意图推理与虚构共同博弈训练辅助策略，并在高速竞速和城市驾驶的人机实验中验证其意图对齐与安全性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制中的辅助智能体需同时具备主动性与可理解性：既要预判并支持人类意图，又不能取代人类自主性，还要让人能实时理解并流畅响应。

Method: 扩展循环状态空间世界模型并加入意图推理；通过虚构共同博弈训练辅助策略以支持离散人类目标；构建从世界模型策略到安全人本系统的部署流水线，并用策略下选与奖励重构应对仿真到真实人类的差距，结合迭代式人机接口设计。

Result: 通过三项人类受试者研究和初步实车部署验证，覆盖高速竞速（N=30）与意图结构更丰富的城市驾驶（N=10）；意图条件化在扭矩对齐和碰撞率上带来可测量改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于学习的世界模型辅助智能体可在安全关键、实时人机协作中有效部署；意图条件化有助于提升人机对齐与安全性，并总结了相关设计经验与实践原则。

Abstract: Assistive agents in shared-control settings face a dual challenge: acting proactively to align with the human's intentions while remaining legible enough for the human to comprehend and fluently respond in real time. We present Dream2Assist, a model-based reinforcement learning framework for tightly-coupled shared decision making that addresses both sides of this challenge. Our approach extends a recurrent state-space world model with intent inference, enabling an assistive policy trained via fictitious co-play to anticipate and support discrete human objectives without overriding human agency. Beyond the computational framework, we describe the full deployment pipeline needed to translate a world-model-based policy into a safe, human-centered system in a resource-efficient way. Crucially, we address the sim-to-real-human gap via policy down-selection and reward restructuring within an iterative human-machine interface design. We validate the framework through three human-subjects studies and a preliminary on-vehicle deployment spanning two qualitatively distinct domains: high-speed racing (N=30) and urban driving with richer intent structure (N=10). Our results demonstrate that intent conditioning yields measurable improvements in torque alignment and collision rate. We surface key design insights and practices for deploying learning-based assistive agents in safety-critical, real-time human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [80] [RobotAgentBench: Towards Open-world Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34649)
*Lingjun Mao,Jinglin Cao,Lukun He,Wenpeng Xu,Yuchen Yan,Hengyu Jin,Zijun Zhang,Jinzhou Tang,Songyao Jin,Ziche Liu,Yifei Shao,Junbo Huang,Fang Nan,Ruobing Han,Ziqiao Xi,Ziming Xu,Shuang Liang,Sibo Zhu,Wenyi Wu,Xinyue Wang,Yiming Huang,Xinkai Zou,Gao Zhu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RobotAgentBench（RAB），一个面向真实开放世界家庭环境的机器人智能体综合基准，用于评估任务发现、上下文推理、物理执行与自适应恢复构成的闭环自主能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA/WAM 等模型显著提升了机器人操作能力，但开放世界部署要求超越预定义任务的更高自主性。现有基准多评估孤立能力，难以刻画开放世界机器人自主性的复杂性，因此需要统一评估机器人智能体的综合闭环能力。

Method: 通过智能体辅助、人在回路的 real-to-sim 流程构建 50 个多样化家庭布局、可交互物体和物理接地任务，包含 90 个原子任务、18 个短时程任务、8 个长时程任务和 4 个开放式场景。RAB 提供多层级评估：任务级考察任务发现、上下文推理、操作、失败恢复和鲁棒性五个维度；场景级评估智能体如何整合这些能力以满足高层用户需求。并在统一的规划-执行-验证框架下系统比较独立 VLA/WAM 模型与多种机器人智能体架构。

Result: 大量实验揭示了操作能力与自主性之间的显著差距，证明了智能体架构和递归自我改进的优势，并指出了迈向开放世界机器人自主性仍面临的挑战。

Conclusion: RAB 为开放世界家庭机器人智能体提供了统一、多层级、可比较的评测基准，强调从单一操作能力评估转向闭环自主性评估，并为后续机器人智能体架构设计与开放世界自主性研究提供了实验依据和挑战方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) have significantly improved robotic manipulation, yet deploying robots in the open world demands a higher level of autonomy beyond executing predefined tasks. Robot agents offer a promising paradigm for this transition, integrating task discovery, contextual reasoning, physical execution, and adaptive recovery into a closed interaction loop. However, existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated capabilities, failing to capture the complexity of open-world robot autonomy. We introduce RobotAgentBench (RAB), a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating robot agents in realistic, open-world household environments. Through an agent-assisted, human-in-the-loop real-to-sim pipeline, RAB constructs 50 diverse household layouts with interactive objects and physically grounded tasks, encompassing 90 atomic tasks, 18 short-horizon tasks, 8 long-horizon tasks, and 4 open-ended scenarios. RAB provides a multi-level evaluation of robot agent capabilities: (1) task-level assessments systematically examine five complementary dimensions: task discovery, context reasoning, manipulation, failure recovery, and robustness; (2) scenario-level evaluation assesses how agents integrate these capabilities to autonomously fulfill high-level user needs. Furthermore, RAB enables systematic comparisons of standalone VLA/WAM models and diverse robot agent architectures under a unified planning-execution-verification framework. Extensive experiments reveal substantial gaps between manipulation and autonomy, demonstrate the advantages of agentic architectures and recursive self-improvement, and highlight the remaining challenges toward open-world robot autonomy.

</details>


### [81] [PS-DLO: Property- and Spatially Aware Deformable Linear Object Manipulation Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34147)
*Mengdi Li,Boxian Yao,Xiyu Zhu,Yifan Yang,Yunfan Lou,Xuan Han,Tao Liu,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PS-DLO，一种属性与空间感知的可变形线性物体操作策略，融合图文对比学习的物体属性先验与基于SAM3的未来空间监督，提升多任务DLO操作成功率及视觉泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLO）细长几何导致精确定位困难，且形变依赖物体特定物理属性，形成感知与控制耦合挑战。

Method: 首先用对比图像-文本学习将初始观测映射为DLO属性的类别描述，为动作生成提供显式物理与结构先验；其次用SAM3从未来观测提取掩码引导特征，并蒸馏到策略的动作专家中，以编码任务相关DLO区域及其后续构型；在单臂和双臂平台上分别为每种 embodiment 训练共享多任务策略。

Result: 在九个真实任务上平均成功率为68.9%，比最强基线高20.0个百分点；在蝴蝶结解结的七种视觉偏移条件中，PS-DLO在六种条件下成功率高于基线。

Conclusion: 结合物体属性先验与未来空间监督可有效提升多任务DLO操作性能和对视觉变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deformable linear objects (DLO) pose a coupled perception and control challenge: their slender geometry makes precise localization difficult, while their deformations depend on object-specific physical properties. To address this challenge, we propose PS-DLO, a property- and spatially aware DLO manipulation policy. First, contrastive image-text learning maps the initial observation to categorical descriptions of DLO properties, providing explicit physical and structural priors for action generation. Second, SAM3 extracts mask-guided features from future observations, which are distilled into the policy's action expert to encode task-relevant DLO regions and their subsequent configurations. We evaluate PS-DLO on nine real-world tasks across single-arm and bimanual platforms, training a shared multi-task policy for each embodiment. PS-DLO achieves an average success rate of 68.9%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 20.0 percentage points. In generalization experiments, PS-DLO achieves higher success rates than the baseline in six of seven visual-shift conditions on butterfly-knot untying. These results demonstrate that combining object-property priors with future spatial supervision improves multi-task DLO manipulation and robustness to visual variation.

</details>


### [82] [Semantic-Coupled Perception Framework for Autonomous Laparoscope Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34128)
*Enduo Zhao,Fangzhou Li,Yujie Zhu,Sicheng Lu,Fang Chen,Longfei Ma,Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种闭环自主腹腔镜控制框架，将实时手术阶段识别与相机运动补偿的视觉感知耦合，以提升遮挡、实体重叠和相机运动下的目标状态估计与视野控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中维持合适视野对安全与效率至关重要，但现有自主腹腔镜控制通常将视觉跟踪与手术阶段识别分离，导致在遮挡、实体重叠和相机运动等挑战下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出闭环自主腹腔镜控制框架，耦合实时手术阶段识别与相机运动补偿视觉感知；利用手术实体间的阶段依赖关系改进挑战视觉条件下的目标状态估计，从而支持任务感知的自主视野控制。

Result: 在基准数据集上评估手术阶段识别与目标跟踪，包括遮挡和实体重叠场景；体模实验验证自主视野控制可行性，相较固定视野控制显示更好的目标可见性和任务执行性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将任务级语义引入视觉状态估计可提升自主腹腔镜引导的潜力，为任务感知的自主视野控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgery (MIS), maintaining an appropriate field of view (FoV) is essential for surgical safety and efficiency. However, conventional autonomous laparoscope control methods often treat visual tracking and surgical phase recognition independently, limiting robustness under occlusion, entity overlap, and camera motion. This paper proposes a closed-loop autonomous laparoscope control framework that couples real-time surgical phase recognition with camera-motion-compensated visual perception. By exploiting phase-dependent relationships among surgical entities, the framework improves target state estimation under challenging visual conditions, supporting task-aware autonomous FoV control. Experiments on benchmark datasets evaluate the proposed framework in surgical phase recognition and target tracking, including scenarios involving occlusion and entity overlap. Phantom experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of autonomous FoV control, showing improved target visibility and task execution performance compared with fixed-FoV control. These results highlight the potential of incorporating task-level semantics into visual state estimation for autonomous laparoscope guidance.

</details>


### [83] [HumanoidNDM: Closing the Training-Deployment Gap of Motion Tracking via Humanoid Neural Dynamics Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34126)
*Chao Chen,Zhenrong Wang,Jilong Wang,Yusen Feng,Jiangran Lyu,Li Yi,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidNDM，一种基于真实世界训练的神经动力学模型的人形机器人运动跟踪在线微调框架，用于弥合仿真到部署的动力学差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人运动跟踪在现实部署中会因外部物理扰动或仿真建模不准确产生动力学差距，导致显著的训练-部署差距，因此需要利用真实世界数据有效微调策略。

Method: HumanoidNDM包含三个阶段：数据采集阶段采用带自适应强度机制的低频参考扰动方法，平衡部署可行性与探索效率；模型训练阶段在欧氏空间训练基于历史本体感知的鲁棒神经动力学模型，并引入可微前向运动学，摆脱对昂贵动捕系统的依赖；策略微调阶段通过反向传播模型梯度直接微调策略，实现高数据效率。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上的物理验证表明，HumanoidNDM能够快速适应多种真实世界扰动，是首个面向人形机器人跟踪策略的在线、无动捕微调框架。

Conclusion: HumanoidNDM通过真实世界神经动力学模型驱动的在线微调，有效弥合人形机器人运动跟踪的训练-部署动力学差距，并支持无动捕、高数据效率的现实部署适应。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently achieved remarkable success in motion tracking. However, during real-world deployment, robots inevitably encounter dynamics gaps arising from either extra physical disturbances or inaccurate simulation modeling, resulting in a significant training-deployment gap. Therefore, effectively leveraging real-world data to fine-tune policies represents the crucial last mile for robust deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose HumanoidNDM, an online fine-tuning framework for humanoid motion tracking driven by a real-world-trained neural dynamics model. First, in the data collection stage, we introduce a low-frequency reference perturbation method with an adaptive intensity mechanism, balancing deployment feasibility and exploration efficiency. Second, for the model training stage, we train a robust history-based, proprioceptive neural dynamics model in Euclidean space by incorporating differentiable forward kinematics, eliminating the reliance on costly motion capture systems. Finally, in the policy fine-tuning stage, we directly fine-tune the policy via backpropagated model gradients, achieving high data efficiency. Through physical validation on the Unitree G1 robot, we demonstrate that HumanoidNDM enables rapid real-world adaptation to various perturbations, representing the first online, MoCap-free fine-tuning framework for humanoid tracking policies.

</details>


### [84] [RoboDuo: Attempt, Check and Atomic Recovery through Code for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34108)
*Jie Wang,Bo Zhang,Renzhi Lu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDuo 是一个闭环双系统框架，用代码作为编码智能体与两个 VLA 之间的接口：全任务 VLA 先尝试并检查完成度，失败后由原子技能 VLA 补完剩余步骤，从而在不更新原全任务 VLA 权重的情况下提升长程任务成功率并恢复失败轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型直接将语言指令和当前观测映射为动作，不检查指令是否完成；长程任务中单步失败会被忽略并导致整个任务未完成，而失败轨迹往往已部分完成，剩余步骤又是可在演示中复用的原子技能。

Method: 提出 RoboDuo 闭环双系统框架：编码智能体编写程序，调度全任务 VLA 执行整体任务并判断是否完成；若失败，再调用原子 VLA 完成剩余步骤。智能体不直接输出动作，只决定由哪个 VLA 执行，因此可包裹现有全任务 VLA 且不更新其权重。为训练原子 VLA 和具备完成检查能力的编码智能体，基于同一组 LIBERO 演示构建 LIBERO-Atomic、LIBERO-Code 和 LIBERO-Judge。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 LIBERO-PRO 上，RoboDuo 稳定提升 X-VLA 与 π0.5 的表现，长程任务增益最大；在真实机器人上还能恢复冻结全任务 VLA 的失败 rollout。

Conclusion: RoboDuo 通过代码接口连接编码智能体与全任务/原子 VLA，实现无需微调原模型的长程任务失败检测与原子技能补完，验证了双系统闭环恢复策略的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map a language instruction and the current observation directly to actions, without checking whether the instruction has been completed. For long-horizon tasks, a single failed step therefore goes unnoticed and leaves the whole task unfinished. Such failed episodes, however, often end with the task partially complete. Moreover, the unfinished steps are reusable atomic skills that recur across demonstrations. A failed attempt can thus be recovered by composing with atomic skills. We propose RoboDuo, a closed-loop dual-system framework that uses code as the interface between a coding agent and two VLAs. The agent writes a program that lets a full-task VLA attempt the whole task and checks whether it is complete; on failure, an atomic VLA finishes the remaining steps. Since the agent emits no actions and only decides which VLA for execution, RoboDuo wraps an existing full-task VLA without updating its weights. To train the atomic VLA and the coding agent with the completion-checking ability, we build LIBERO-Atomic, LIBERO-Code, and LIBERO-Judge from a single set of LIBERO demonstrations. RoboDuo consistently improves X-VLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks, and recovers failed rollouts of a frozen full-task VLA on a real robot.

</details>


### [85] [X-World: Transferring Video Priors to Variable-View Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34105)
*Chun Gu,Xiaofei Wei,Yiming Chen,Juntong Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-World 提出将视频扩散先验通过固定长度 token 序列迁移到共享动作专家的机器人控制框架，兼顾多视角输入、异构动作接口与闭环执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型能提供用于机器人控制的交互表征，但迁移时需解决相机输入可变、动作接口异构以及闭环执行等问题。

Method: 该方法用残差跨视角注意力分支将预训练视频骨干扩展到1至3个视角并保留原自注意力路径；用层对齐Q-Former聚合骨干特征；用数据集特定模块将原生状态与动作接口映射到共享动作专家。训练分为视频适配与仅动作预训练（冻结视频骨干），再进行目标域联合后训练；策略通过单次骨干前向提取视频特征，并采用异步动作分块执行。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.5%平均成功率；LIBERO训练检查点在无额外适配的LIBERO-Plus上达到76.5%。七类偏移分析显示在机器人、语言、光照和布局扰动上有提升，但在相机、背景和噪声偏移下弱于OpenVLA-OFT。在六个双臂真实任务上，每任务20次试验取得81.7%平均成功率。消融中移除视频适配或跨视角融合导致最大成功率下降。

Conclusion: X-World能有效将视频先验迁移到多视角、异构动作接口的机器人控制中并取得强性能，但在部分视觉偏移下仍存在鲁棒性短板。

Abstract: Video diffusion models provide a source of interaction representations for robot control, but transferring these representations requires reconciling variable camera inputs with heterogeneous action interfaces and closed-loop execution. We present X-World, a framework that transfers a video prior through a fixed-length token sequence to a shared action expert. A residual cross-view attention branch extends a pretrained video backbone to one, two, or three views while retaining its original self-attention path. A layer-aligned Q-Former aggregates backbone features, and dataset-specific modules map between native state and action interfaces and the shared action expert. Training separates video adaptation from action-only pretraining by freezing the video backbone in the latter stage, followed by joint target-domain post-training. The policy extracts video features with a single backbone pass and uses asynchronous action chunking for execution. On LIBERO, X-World reaches 98.5\% mean success; its LIBERO-trained checkpoint obtains 76.5\% on LIBERO-Plus without further adaptation. The seven-shift breakdown reveals gains in robot, language, lighting, and layout perturbations, alongside weaknesses under camera, background, and noise shifts relative to OpenVLA-OFT. On six dual-arm real-world tasks, it achieves 81.7\% mean success over 20 trials per task. Removing video adaptation or cross-view fusion produces the largest observed success drops among the tested removals.

</details>


### [86] [Doppler-aided Position-Velocity Tracking of Hidden Objects using mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34085)
*Yerin Cheon,Kush Garg,Akshat Dave*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出首个室内非视距毫米波雷达RAD序列数据集，并将隐藏目标跟踪建模为序列位置-速度估计，实现低成本雷达下的高精度运动一致跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内机器人需跟踪不直接可见目标；光学NLoS方法依赖光照、场景几何或弱多跳回波且采集慢，而毫米波雷达可利用墙面反射和FMCW快速测量距离、角度与速度，但低分辨率导致空间模糊，现有方法又常在学习前压缩或丢弃多普勒信息，未充分利用完整RAD观测。

Method: 作者采集首个室内NLoS雷达数据集，将连续RAD测量与同步位置、速度真值配对，覆盖576个序列、720856帧、12条轨迹及不同速度、起点和材料配置；方法上把NLoS跟踪表述为序列位置-速度估计，使用共享3D编码器处理连续帧完整RAD表示，分别用循环分支估计位置和速度，并通过后推理细化融合为运动一致轨迹。

Result: 在留出轨迹上达到8.95 cm位置误差和1.75 cm/s速度误差，并可泛化到包含移动人体目标的未见场景。

Conclusion: 该数据集与跟踪器为使用低成本雷达跟踪隐藏移动目标建立了基准，并证明充分利用RAD与多普勒信息可提升室内NLoS跟踪性能。

Abstract: Safe indoor operation requires autonomous tracking of objects that are not directly visible to the robot. Optical non-line-of-sight (NLoS) methods depend on ambient lighting and scene geometry or on weak multi-bounce returns that need long acquisitions. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers an attractive alternative: walls act as reflectors for mmWave signals, and frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars enable fast, practical measurement of range, angle, and velocity. However, the low resolution of these radars leads to spatially ambiguous observations. The Doppler information, although particularly sensitive to target motion, remains underexplored in indoor NLoS localization: existing methods emphasize spatial representations and either collapse or discard the Doppler dimension before learning. To leverage the complete Range-Azimuth-Doppler (RAD) information, we acquire the first indoor NLoS radar dataset that pairs sequential RAD measurements with synchronized position and velocity ground truth under controlled variations in motion: 576 sequences totaling 720,856 frames across 12 trajectories of a mobile robot moving along varying speeds, starting positions, and material configurations. We then formulate NLoS tracking as sequential position-velocity estimation rather than frame-wise localization: a shared 3-D encoder processes the full RAD representation of consecutive frames, separate recurrent branches estimate position and velocity, and post-inference refinement combines the two into a motion-consistent trajectory. Our method achieves 8.95cm position and 1.75cm/s velocity errors on held-out trajectories and generalizes to unseen scenarios involving a moving human target. Together, our dataset and tracker establish a benchmark for tracking hidden moving targets with a low-cost radar.

</details>


### [87] [DSGN: A Dual-Stream Gated Network for Scalable Simultaneous Surveillance and Containment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34084)
*Heng Zhang,Ziyang Zuo,Guoxiang Zhao,Xiaoqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双流门控网络DSGN，将监视与围堵解耦为专用表征流，并通过上下文感知门控与概率先验协调，以缓解大规模机器人网络中SSC任务切换的不稳定性和可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSC任务要求机器人同时执行监视与围堵：监视倾向空间分散以覆盖区域，围堵需要目标中心聚集以完成包围，二者目标冲突使启发式切换不稳定；随着机器人网络扩大，智能体间及智能体-目标关系增多，问题更显著。端到端DRL虽减少手工切换依赖，但传统单流策略易在共享特征空间中纠缠分散导向与目标中心信号，削弱任务转换。

Method: 提出DSGN架构：将监视和围堵分离为两个专用表征流；采用上下文感知门控机制动态协调两流以完成任务仲裁；引入概率先验正则化门控过程，减少模糊中间决策，支持大规模交互复杂度下更清晰的行为转换。

Result: 评估表明DSGN在恒定密度跨尺度设置下保持较强任务效能与可扩展性；消融和阶段特定评估显示，动态门控机制与概率先验在纯监视和任务切换场景中均能支持有效任务仲裁与可靠模式切换。

Conclusion: DSGN通过双流解耦、上下文感知门控和概率先验，有效缓解规模放大的任务转换困难，为多机器人SSC提供可扩展的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: The simultaneous surveillance and containment (SSC) problem arises in multi-robot applications such as perimeter security and environmental monitoring, where robots must survey a region of interest and coordinate to enclose targets once they are detected. Surveillance favors spatial dispersion for coverage, whereas containment requires target-centric cohesion for enclosure. This conflict makes heuristic switching unstable, and the instability becomes more pronounced as larger robotic networks introduce more inter-agent and agent-target relations. End-to-end DRL reduces reliance on hand-crafted switching, but conventional single-stream policies often mix surveillance and containment cues in one shared feature space that could entangle dispersion-oriented and target-centric signals and weaken task transitions. In this paper, we propose the Dual-Stream Gated Network (DSGN), an architecture designed to address scale-amplified task-transition difficulties by separating surveillance and containment into specialized representation streams and coordinating them through a context-aware gating mechanism. A probabilistic prior further regularizes the gating process, reducing ambiguous intermediate decisions and supporting clearer behavioral transitions under large-scale interaction complexity. Evaluations show that DSGN maintains strong task efficacy and scalability under constant-density cross-scale settings. Additional ablation and phase-specific evaluations show that the proposed dynamic gating mechanism and probabilistic prior support effective task arbitration and reliable mode switching in both pure surveillance and task-switching scenarios. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [Learning Interaction-Aware Path Corrections from Unlabeled Trajectories: A LiDAR-Based Sim-to-Real GNN Framework for Construction Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34060)
*Yilong Chen,Yong K. Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖LiDAR的实时社会导航框架，从无标注示范中学习交互感知的路径修正，用于施工机器人在动态非结构化环境中安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工机器人在工人附近作业时需预测人体运动并适应动态非结构化环境，但现有社会导航方法依赖手工交互规则、标注示范或机器人特定控制策略，限制了可扩展性与迁移性。

Method: 利用匀速外推生成残差学习目标，采用GNN建模机器人、行人与目标上下文关系；部署时用扩展卡尔曼滤波跟踪LiDAR检测构建动态图，并通过规划器无关接口由GNN修正规划器生成的路点。

Result: 在砌砖试验中路径不规则性降低39.2%并提高最小TTC；在Dynablox序列上最小TTC提高38.3%，且路径较基线更短更平滑；消融支持1.0秒预测区间，并验证目标上下文、反射增强和特征归一化的收益。

Conclusion: 该框架以LiDAR-only、规划器无关方式实现实时交互感知路径修正，提升了施工动态环境中社会导航的安全性、平滑性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Construction robots operating near workers require navigation that can anticipate human motion while remaining robust to dynamic and unstructured site conditions. However, existing social navigation methods depend on hand-crafted interaction rules, labeled demonstrations, or robot-specific control policies, limiting their scalability and transferability in construction environments. We propose a real-time, LiDAR-only framework that learns interaction-aware path corrections from unlabeled demonstrations. Constant-velocity extrapolation generates residual learning targets, while a GNN models relationships among the robot, pedestrians, and goal context. During deployment, an extended Kalman filter tracks LiDAR detections to construct dynamic graphs, and the GNN offsets planner-generated waypoints through a planner-agnostic interface. Within the bricklaying trial, the GNN reduced path irregularity by 39.2\% and increased minimum TTC. Across Dynablox sequences, it increased minimum TTC by 38.3\% while producing shorter, smoother paths than the baselines. Ablation studies supported a 1.0-s prediction interval and confirmed the benefits of target context, reflection augmentation, and feature normalization.

</details>


### [89] [Lightweight Change Detection for Adaptive Collective Perception in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34059)
*Thiemen Siemensma,Tiago Travassos,Jiacheng Zhang,Bahar Haghighat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究动态环境下的群体机器人集体感知，提出轻量变化检测与自适应采样机制以提升决策鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体感知研究多集中于静态环境，而动态环境中瓷砖比例和表面模式空间相关性会随时间变化，且资源受限机器人难以采用存储完整样本流的变化检测方法。

Method: 将轻量变化检测机制集成到贝叶斯集体决策框架中，在检测到分布漂移时重置机器人信念；该检测器具有常数内存和常数逐样本计算量。进一步引入空间相关采样检测方法，并在线调整采样间隔以缓解相关性影响。

Result: Webots仿真表明，该方法具有有竞争力的变化检测延迟，在不同通信设置下精度更一致，并在空间相关性变化时提升决策准确率。

Conclusion: 所提方法适用于内存受限机器人，可在动态环境中实现更鲁棒的集体感知与决策。

Abstract: Swarm robotics enables scalable inspection through collective perception, where robots infer environmental properties from distributed observations. A common benchmark considers robots exploring black-white tiled environments and determining the majority tile type. While most work studies static environments, dynamic environments remain underexplored. We study collective perception in dynamic environments where the tile proportion and the spatial correlation of surface patterns may change over time. To enable implementation on resource-constrained robots, we integrate a lightweight change detection mechanism into a Bayesian collective decision-making framework that resets robot beliefs when distribution shifts are detected. The proposed detector requires constant memory and constant per-sample computation, unlike two recent competitive approaches that store the full sample stream, making it suitable for memory-constrained robots. We further introduce a method that detects spatially correlated sampling and adapts sampling intervals online to mitigate correlation effects. Our simulation results in Webots show competitive change detection delays, more consistent precision across communication settings, and improved decision accuracy under changing spatial correlations.

</details>


### [90] [Extended LASMP: Language Aided Subset Sampling Based Motion Planner for Drone Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34054)
*Saswati Bhattacharjee,Anirban Sinha,Mukulika Ghosh,Chinwe Ekenna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种命令对齐采样分布，将导航指令直接融入探索过程，以提高四旋翼三维环境下采样型运动规划的效率；该方法将命令感知子集采样扩展到空中系统，并利用大语言模型把自然语言指令接地为偏航与高度几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂三维环境中，均匀RRT等采样型规划器探索效率低、采样盲目；需要将导航命令或自然语言指令融入采样过程，在保持概率完备性的同时提升四旋翼运动规划效率。

Method: 受Language-Aided Subset Sampling Motion Planner启发，将子集采样推广到命令对齐区域，实现结构化三维探索并保持概率完备性；使用基于提示的大语言模型进行实体抽取和指令验证，将自然语言指令接地为偏航条件与高度感知的几何约束。

Result: 在复杂环境中的大量仿真表明，该方法能够生成无碰撞且一致的轨迹，并相较基于均匀采样的RRT类规划器显著减少采样开销。

Conclusion: 命令对齐子集采样可有效提升四旋翼三维采样运动规划效率，并验证了将命令感知子集采样扩展到空中系统的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a command-aligned sampling distribution that incorporates navigation commands directly into the exploration process to improve the efficiency of sampling-based motion planning for quadcopters in three-dimensional environments. Inspired by the Language-Aided Subset Sampling Motion Planner, this paper extends command-aware subset sampling to aerial systems. Subset sampling is generalized to command-aligned regions, enabling structured 3D exploration while preserving probabilistic completeness. Natural language instructions are grounded into yaw-conditioned and altitude-aware geometric constraints using a prompt-based large language model for entity extraction and instruction validation. Extensive simulations in complex environments demonstrate that the proposed method generates collision-free, consistent trajectories while significantly reducing sampling effort compared to uniform RRT-based planners.

</details>


### [91] [Behavior Spatial Benchmark: Evaluating Spatial Understanding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34051)
*Sirui Chen,Huang Huang,Qineng Wang,Wenlong Huang,Manling Li,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BEHAVIOR-Spatial基准，联合评估VLM空间问答、点目标定位与VLA机器人行为，发现任务成功不能证明空间指令被遵循，改变指令时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务需要将空间语言落地到物理场景，但仅看任务成功率无法判断机器人是否真正遵循空间指令；因此需要厘清VLM空间理解与VLA行为之间的关系。

Method: 基于BEHAVIOR-1K构建BEHAVIOR-Spatial，包含49个活动中的3256个空间问题、14个操作任务中的1240个定位查询，以及覆盖14个操作任务和10个导航任务的动作评估。操作协议评估留出配置，以及在固定训练布局内目标变化时的指令遵循；比较基线、VLM适配、机器人数据预训练和oracle视觉目标线索。

Result: 基线在留出配置上达到41.6%，但在改变指令下仅16.1%；VLM适配和机器人数据预训练提升有限；oracle视觉目标线索将上述两项分别提升至60.3%和38.0%。感知评估显示，VLM问答与定位准确率和VLA操作表现之间存在策略依赖的相关性。

Conclusion: 应同时评估空间指令遵循与任务完成，并进一步研究空间感知性能何时能够转化为机器人行为。

Abstract: Robot tasks require grounding spatial language in physical scenes, but task success alone cannot establish whether spatial instructions are followed. We present BEHAVIOR-Spatial, a benchmark built on BEHAVIOR-1K that measures spatial question answering, point-based target grounding, and robot action to examine how VLM spatial understanding relates to VLA behavior. It comprises 3,256 spatial questions across 49 activities, 1,240 grounding queries across 14 manipulation tasks, and action evaluations covering those 14 tasks and 10 navigation tasks. Our manipulation protocol evaluates held-out configurations and instruction following when the requested target changes within a fixed training layout. The baseline achieves 41.6% on held-out configurations but only 16.1% under changed instructions. VLM adaptation and robot-data pretraining provide limited improvements, whereas oracle visual target cues raise these rates to 60.3% and 38.0%, respectively. Perception evaluations reveal policy-dependent correlations between VLM question-answering and grounding accuracy and VLA manipulation performance. Together, these findings motivate evaluating spatial instruction following alongside task completion and examining when spatial perception performance translates into robot behavior. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [92] [T-ConSG: A LiDAR-Based Real-Time Temporal Construction Scene Graph for Dynamic and Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34041)
*Yilong Chen,Yong K. Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于LiDAR的实时时序施工场景图T-ConSG，用统一图结构联合表示自由空间、静态/动态目标及环境事件，以支持动态施工环境中的机器人空间-时间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工场地中移动智能体、临时障碍物与持久结构变化并存，现有动态/长期SLAM方法虽能检测运动和环境变化，但主要输出度量表示，难以为高层空间与时间推理提供结构化信息。

Method: T-ConSG从LiDAR几何证据增量构建场景图，节点和边表达自由空间区域、静态目标、动态目标及环境事件，并包含空间关系边和事件参与边；框架持续维护自由空间与静态目标信息、跟踪动态目标，并将累积空间证据的变化显式建模为安装和移除事件，从而同时捕捉短期动态与持久环境演化。

Result: 作者通过三个应用研究验证该表示：砌砖测试床中的动态感知导航、ConSLAM数据集上的施工进度监测、以及仿真施工环境中的在线变化检测；结果表明T-ConSG能统一组织空间与时间信息，并支持同时包含短期动态和持久变化的环境中的机器人推理。

Conclusion: T-ConSG为施工场景理解提供了一种统一的实时时空图表示，可桥接度量SLAM输出与高层空间-时间推理，并支持动态施工环境下的导航、进度监测和变化检测等机器人应用。

Abstract: Dynamic and continuously evolving environments present significant challenges for robotic mapping and scene understanding, particularly on construction sites where moving agents, temporary obstacles, and persistent structural changes coexist. While dynamic and long-term SLAM methods can detect motion and environmental changes, their outputs are predominantly metric representations that provide limited structure for higher-level spatial and temporal reasoning. This paper presents a LiDAR-based real-time temporal construction scene graph (T-ConSG) that jointly represents free-space regions, static objects, dynamic objects, and environmental events with spatial-relational and event-participation edges. The proposed framework incrementally constructs the graph from LiDAR-derived geometric evidence, maintaining persistent free-space and static-object information while tracking dynamic objects. Changes in accumulated spatial evidence are further represented as explicit installation and removal events, allowing the graph to capture both transient dynamics and persistent environmental evolution. We demonstrate the proposed representation through three complementary application studies: dynamics-aware navigation in a bricklaying testbed, construction progress monitoring on the ConSLAM dataset, and online change detection in a simulated construction environment. The results demonstrate that the proposed T-ConSG provides a unified representation for organizing spatial and temporal information and supports robotic reasoning across environments containing both short-term dynamics and persistent changes.

</details>


### [93] [Cut a Wall, See a Wall: Identity Substitution in Occluded Point-Cloud Perception for Construction Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34040)
*Tsung-Lung Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 测试时整区域遮挡会使点云分割网络把隐藏构件系统性重标为单一汇类，而非仅漏检；每点加入14个手工几何特征（GPE）可显著恢复mIoU并几乎消除整对象丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑机器人需标注只能部分看到的建筑构件；训练中的遮挡通常是对象局部缺失，但测试中可能出现整区域遮挡，导致构件被错误重标，并影响下游竣工检查。

Method: 分析固定/随机布局合成场景与真实S3DIS扫描中整区域遮挡下的错误模式；在PointNet++和PTv3风格Transformer上，为每点追加14个手工计算的几何特征（GPE），并与遮挡数据增强比较；通过特征置换解释各类别依赖的稳定线索。

Result: 基线在固定布局mIoU降至0.15-0.27、随机布局0.36-0.72；固定布局柱类吸收最多70%测试点，随机布局楼梯预测为真实份额4.5-5.4倍，单测试集最多269个整对象消失。GPE将mIoU恢复至0.54-0.92，并在所有运行中消除64-100%整对象丢失；真实S3DIS整墙丢失从10降到0；竣工柱检查正确视图比例从17-58%升至71-92%。遮挡增强在mIoU上有竞争力，但整对象丢失更多（如25-35 vs 0）。

Conclusion: 整区域遮挡引发的是向单一汇类的系统性重标；GPE通过提供稳定几何线索缓解该问题，提升分割和下游检查，且比遮挡增强更能保住完整对象。

Abstract: Construction robots must label building elements they can only partly see. We show that when occlusion at test time removes whole regions of a scene, rather than parts of an object as in training, point-cloud segmentation networks do not simply miss the hidden elements: they relabel them, and the errors collapse onto a single sink class. With fixed synthetic layouts the column absorbs up to 70% of all test points; with randomized layouts the sink moves to the stair, predicted at 4.5-5.4x its true share across three training seeds. Baseline mIoU falls to 0.15-0.27 (fixed) and 0.36-0.72 (randomized), and up to 269 whole objects vanish in a single test set. Appending 14 hand-computed geometric features per point (GPE) restores mIoU to 0.54-0.92 and removes 64-100% of whole-object losses in every run, for PointNet++ and a PTv3-style transformer in both layout regimes; on real S3DIS scans it removes every whole-wall loss (10 to 0). In an as-built column check computed from the predictions, GPE raises the share of correctly checked views from 17-58% to 71-92%. Feature permutation on fixed, randomized and real scenes explains the pattern: each class relies on whichever cue is stable in its training data, position for height-banded slabs and beams, intrinsic shape for walls and stairs once their position varies. Occlusion augmentation is a strong alternative on mIoU, but in every setting it loses more whole objects than GPE (e.g., 25-35 vs. 0).

</details>


### [94] [Enabling Motion-Induced WiFi-CSI Sensing in Multi-Robot Systems: A Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34034)
*Brendan Dijkstra,Ninad Jadhav,Maurice Stoffijn,Stephanie Gil,Bahar Haghighat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个集成WSR感知流程的开源Gazebo仿真框架，用于生成具有真实物理特性的合成WiFi-CSI数据并支持机器人运动诱导感知、定位及虚实数据对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WiFi-CSI的WSR方法难以部署，主要受限于与标准机器人工具集成不足以及对已弃用WiFi-CSI采集硬件的依赖。

Method: 将WSR感知流程集成到机器人仿真器Gazebo中，生成具有真实信号特性的合成WiFi-CSI测量，利用真实机器人运动支持运动诱导感知与下游定位，并支持处理仿真和真实WiFi-CSI数据以进行直接比较与验证。

Result: 使用TurtleBot3机器人对比仿真与真实实验，验证了框架有效性，并展示了其在方位估计和机器人定位中的应用。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了多机器人系统中WiFi-CSI感知的进入门槛，为将WiFi-CSI感知与多模态感知结合用于定位与协同提供了基础，且代码与实验细节公开。

Abstract: WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) enables fine-grained sensing of spatial relationships between wireless devices. The WiFi Sensor for Robotics (WSR) toolbox demonstrated that WiFi-CSI measurements collected during robot motion, combined with odometry, can be used to synthesize virtual antenna arrays, enabling motion-induced bearing estimation in multi-robot experiments. However, deploying the WSR approach has remained challenging due to its limited integration with standard robotics tools and reliance on deprecated WiFi-CSI acquisition hardware. This work presents an open-source simulation framework that integrates the WSR sensing pipeline within the robotic simulator Gazebo. The framework generates synthetic WiFi-CSI measurements with realistic physical signal properties and leverages realistic robot motion required for motion-induced sensing and downstream localization. It further supports processing both simulated and real WiFi-CSI data for direct comparison and validation. Our aim is to make WSR-style sensing accessible as a modular sensing modality for robotic research. We validate our framework by comparing simulated and real-world experiments using TurtleBot3 robots, and demonstrate its use for bearing estimation and robot localization. This work lowers the barrier to entry for WiFi-CSI-based sensing in multi-robot systems and provides a foundation for integrating WiFi-CSI sensing with multi-modal perception for localization and coordination. All code and experimental details are publicly available at this https URL

</details>


### [95] [ElevateHead: Decide Once, Then Commit: When the Action Head Matters for Multimodal Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34031)
*Tsung-Lung Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态示范下机器人策略动作头如何选择并保持一种可行方式，提出在学到的决策点一次性选择并贯穿回合、且可由用户指定的 ElevateHead，在 Franka Kitchen 上显著优于 BeT，并分析承诺失效场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类示范学习的机器人常面对同一任务的多种有效做法，逐步重决策的策略会在方式间摇摆；作者追问动作头何时重要以及应如何做出并保持选择。

Method: 在受控任务上比较采样单一方式与平均多种方式；提出 ElevateHead：在学习的决策点选择一次、回合内承诺该选择，并将选择作为用户可设置输入；采用诊断式迭代，冻结版本并在未见任务测试，只针对测得的失败修改，且每次运行前登记预测。

Result: 受控任务中两种方式时采样头成功率提高 +0.72，但该趋势未在其他任务复现；前四版至少在一個留出任务上低于 BeT；第五版在留出 Franka Kitchen 四个子任务全完成率 45.2% 对 BeT 29.3%（+0.160，95% CI [+0.129, +0.188]），强制模式时首个完成子任务在 95% 成功回合中匹配；变化起始位置修复后承诺 0.22→0.81、成功 0.74→0.91，变化物体位置仅部分修复（探索变体中承诺 0.03→0.59，未检测到成功率变化）；留出积木堆叠上第四版与 BeT 相当但未超过。

Conclusion: 一次性承诺并由用户控制模式可在多模态任务中提升表现，诊断式设计能定位承诺失效条件；但仍存在场景限制，真实机器人实验计划在后续版本进行。

Abstract: Robots that learn from people often see several valid ways to do the same task: pass an obstacle on the left or on the right, open the microwave or move the kettle first. A policy that re-decides at every step can waver between these ways mid-task. We ask when the action head (the part of a policy that turns the state into actions) matters, and how it should make this choice. On a controlled task, heads that sample one way succeed far more often than a head that averages the ways when the demonstrations contain several ways and the state gives no hint which to take (+0.72 success with two ways); this trend was not reproduced on the other tasks we ran. We then present ElevateHead, a head that decides once at a learned decision point, commits to that choice for the episode, and exposes the choice as an input a user can set. We designed it by diagnosis: each version was frozen and tested on tasks it had not seen, and the next version changed only what a measured failure pointed to; predictions were registered before each run. The first four versions each fell below BeT on at least one held-out task. The fifth completed all four subtasks of the held-out Franka Kitchen task in 45.2% of episodes against 29.3% for BeT, the strongest baseline across our tasks (+0.160, 95% CI [+0.129, +0.188]); when a mode was forced, the first completed subtask matched it in 95% of successful episodes. Diagnosis also located where commitment breaks: varied start positions (repaired on the controlled task: commitment 0.22 to 0.81, success 0.74 to 0.91) and varied object placement (partly repaired: in an exploratory variant, commitment 0.03 to 0.59 on aligning with no detectable change in success). On held-out block stacking, version 4 was comparable to BeT and did not exceed it. A real-robot test with predictions fixed in advance is planned for a follow-up version of this paper.

</details>


### [96] [I Won't Know Until I Try: Interactive Information Gathering for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33959)
*Linfei Yuan,Rachel Holladay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种任务与运动规划（TAMP）框架，将任务导向的交互式信息获取与规划-执行交错结合，以应对长时操作中外观无法确定的物体属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时操作常依赖无法仅凭外观可靠推断的物体属性；物理交互虽能揭示这些属性，却可能改变世界状态并使后续动作失效。

Method: 基于PDDLStream，将交互动作纳入乐观规划；在执行过程中验证交互结果，保留仍可用的动作，并仅对继续执行所需的连接部分进行重规划，实现任务导向的信息获取与恢复式重规划。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上评估了判断物体是否可移动、笔是否有墨水、抽屉能否打开、容器是否为空等任务；实验表明交互驱动的状态更新与恢复重规划可在结果不确定和外部扰动下支持持续执行。

Conclusion: 该框架通过交错的信息获取、规划与执行，提高了长时操作在不确定物体属性和执行扰动下的鲁棒性与连续性。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation often depends on object properties that cannot be reliably inferred from appearance alone. Physical interaction can reveal these properties, but it can also change the world in ways that invalidate subsequent actions. We present a task and motion planning (TAMP) framework that integrates task-directed interactive information-gathering with interleaved planning and execution. Building on PDDLStream, our framework plans optimistically with these interactive actions, verifies their outcomes during execution, and adapts the remaining plan by retaining still-applicable actions and replanning only the connections needed to continue execution. We evaluate the framework in simulation and on a real robot across tasks that require determining whether an object is movable, a pen has ink, a drawer can be opened, or a container is empty. These experiments demonstrate how interaction-driven state updates and recovery replanning support continued execution under uncertain outcomes and external disturbances.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Functional Specialisation for Bimanual Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33947)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在仿真双通信循环控制器的肌肉骨骼系统中，考察双手机器人控制的学习责任如何在模块控制器与两臂间分配，并比较差异化/共享控制器目标与固定/平衡手-任务分配的2×2组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人控制需在运动噪声、能量成本和通信延迟下同时满足不同功能需求；核心问题是学习责任应如何在模块化控制器和两臂之间组织，以兼顾任务表现、控制效率与手角色灵活性。

Method: 采用2×2实验设计，在具有两个相互通信的循环控制器的仿真肌肉骨骼系统中，比较差异化与共享控制器目标，以及固定与平衡的手-任务分配；并以与对侧控制路由对齐的固定分配作为任务对齐的功能特化条件。

Result: 差异化目标结合与对侧控制路由对齐的固定分配时，任务对齐的功能特化最强，规范任务表现和控制效率最高，双臂成功率达81.33%。其相对共享目标的优势随运动噪声增加而扩大，但在更强能量惩罚下缩小。特化增强会降低手角色灵活性：在Differential-Fixed训练下反转手角色使双臂成功率下降80.46个百分点，而Shared-Balanced下仅下降1.51个百分点。

Conclusion: 控制器目标与手-任务经验共同决定功能组织：更强的角色特异性特化在角色稳定时有利于表现和效率，而更分布式的训练在角色变化时更有利于可互换性。

Abstract: Bimanual robotic control requires simultaneous management of distinct functional demands under motor noise, energetic costs, and communication delays. We investigate how learning responsibility should be organised across modular controllers and the two arms in a simulated musculoskeletal system with two communicating recurrent controllers. Using a 2 x 2 design, we compare differential versus shared controller objectives and fixed versus balanced hand-task assignments. Task-aligned functional specialisation was strongest when differential objectives were combined with a fixed assignment aligned with contralateral control routing. This condition achieved the highest canonical-task performance and control efficiency, reaching 81.33% bimanual success. Its advantage over shared objectives increased with motor noise but narrowed under stronger energy penalties. Stronger specialisation also reduced hand-role flexibility: reversing the hand roles decreased bimanual success by 80.46 percentage points under Differential-Fixed training, compared with 1.51 percentage points under Shared-Balanced training. These results show that controller objectives and hand-task experience jointly determine functional organisation. Stronger role-specific specialisation favours performance and efficiency when roles remain stable, whereas more distributed training favours interchangeability when roles change.

</details>


### [98] [Real-Time Human Trajectory Prediction from RGB-D for Mobile Robots Using Bi-Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33916)
*Basheer Al-Tawil,Magnus Jung,Ayoub Al-Hamadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrajMamba 是一种面向 RGB-D 相机的轻量级双向状态空间模型，用于短期人体轨迹预测，通过统一训练与部署处理路径缓解 train-deploy pipeline gap，并在真实机器人上实现实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态、有人环境中导航需要从 RGB-D 相机预测人的短期未来轨迹。核心问题是训练与部署流程不一致：训练常用理想化、平滑的真值，而推理时输入来自姿态检测器和深度传感器的噪声估计，导致模型面对未见过的输入分布。现有 RNN 或 Transformer 方法要么难以捕捉短期时序依赖，要么计算成本较高。

Method: 提出 TrajMamba：使用单条共享处理路径同时用于训练和部署，对齐人体姿态提取、深度反投影和平滑过程；以人体髋部中心速度的短时间窗口为输入，采用堆叠双向 Mamba 块编码，并用 GRU 头解码未来速度。

Result: 在 ETH/UCY 基准上平均 ADE 为 0.29 m、FDE 为 0.51 m；模型仅 262K 参数，可在普通硬件上实时运行，并部署于搭载 NVIDIA Jetson Orin 的 TIAGo 机器人。

Conclusion: TrajMamba 通过统一训练与部署流程，在保持轻量和实时性的同时实现了准确的短期人体轨迹预测，适合真实移动机器人部署。

Abstract: Predicting the short-term future trajectory of a person from RGB-D cameras is a key capability for mobile robots navigating in dynamic and human populated environment. The key challenge to deploying learned predictors on real robots is the train-deploy pipeline gap. Training data are often prepared with an idealized, smoothed ground truth, whereas at inference, the model is fed noisy estimates from pose detectors and depth sensors. The model is therefore evaluated on an input distribution it never encountered during training. Most existing models rely on recurrent neural networks (RNNs) or transformers, which either struggle to capture short-term temporal dependencies or suffer from computational cost. We present TrajMamba, a lightweight, bidirectional state space model for short-term human trajectory prediction using an RGB-D camera. The model uses a single shared processing path for both training and deployment, aligning human pose extraction, depth back projection, and smoothing processes. It encodes a short window of persons's hip center velocities with stacked bidirectional Mamba blocks and decodes future velocities with a GRU head. On the ETH/UCY benchmark, TrajMamba reaches an average ADE of 0.29 m FDE of 0.51 m. With 262K parameters, it runs in real time on modest hardware and is deployed on a TIAGo robot with an onboard NVIDIA Jetson Orin.

</details>


### [99] [Sybil-Resilient Multi-Drone Exploration of Indoor and Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33906)
*Shyaam Mahesh,Aditya B Prakash,Rubal Sagwal,Avinash Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多无人机协同探索建筑时的Sybil攻击：被攻陷无人机伪造多个身份，抢占前沿并发送伪造扫描，导致真实无人机漏探索区域并在地图中生成不存在墙体；作者提出仅依赖机载传感器与地图、无需安全基础设施的五级防御，并在70张地图上仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机团队通过共享地图和声明前沿来避免重复探索，但这也使团队默认信任所有消息来源身份。攻击者可利用一个被攻陷节点创建虚假身份，干扰前沿分配并注入伪造地图数据，从而降低探索覆盖率并污染地图。

Method: 论文设定五类Sybil攻击者，包括封堵楼层间开口的攻击者；提出五级无安全基础设施防御：将收到的地图数据与自身观测审计、检查身份声称位置是否有实体存在、仅信任已观测到实体的身份、隔离未被任何无人机观测到的身份、将每个已观测实体绑定到唯一身份。仿真覆盖7类共70张地图，包括办公室、隧道、三层建筑和洞穴。

Result: 无防御时，一个被攻陷无人机平均可使最多25%的地图未被探索，办公室场景达45%；封堵楼层攻击者使三层建筑探索率降至82.3%，最差地图降至34.6%。隔离机制在所有设置中将探索率恢复到未受攻击团队水平，并在多数设置中将虚假数据恢复到该水平；例外包括两个共谋攻击者、虚假身份紧邻真实无人机、10个洞穴中的3个以及16架无人机团队，其中后三者可通过实体—身份绑定修复。超过2350次任务中，诚实无人机仅被队友撤销两次。

Conclusion: 仅利用无人机自身传感器与地图一致性即可显著抵御多无人机探索中的Sybil攻击，隔离机制能恢复探索性能，实体—身份绑定可处理部分残余场景，且误撤销诚实节点概率极低。

Abstract: When a team of drones explores a building together, each drone tells the others which frontier it is going to and shares the map it has built. This avoids duplicated effort, but it also means that the team trusts every identity from which it receives messages. In a Sybil attack, one compromised drone creates fake identities, and these fake teammates claim frontiers and send fabricated scans, so the real drones leave parts of the building unexplored and end up with walls in their maps that do not exist. We study five such attackers, including one that seals off the openings between floors, and five levels of defence that need no security infrastructure, only the drones' own sensors and maps: an audit of received map data against what a drone sees itself, a check that a body is present where an identity claims to be, trust only for identities whose body has been seen, a quarantine for identities that no drone has observed, and a rule that binds each observed body to one identity. Tests in simulation cover 70 maps of seven kinds, from offices and tunnels to three-storey buildings and caves. Without a defence, one compromised drone leaves on average up to 25% of a map unexplored (45% in offices), and the floor-sealing attacker cuts the explored part of a three-storey building to 82.3%, or 34.6% on the worst map. The quarantine brings exploration back to the level of an unattacked team in every setting, and in most settings it also brings the false data back to that level. The exceptions are two colluding attackers, fakes placed next to real drones, three of the ten caves and a team of 16 drones, and binding bodies to identities fixes the last three. Over 2,350 missions, an honest drone was revoked by a teammate only twice.

</details>


### [100] [RoboLongR: Causally Consistent Rewards Teach Robots to Progressively Master Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33863)
*Zixuan Chen,Lin Cao,Fenggang Zhang,Haotong Zhang,Yangtao Chen,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboLongR：一种离线到在线的长时程机器人操作框架，通过学习依赖历史的奖励，使策略在新任务上按执行历史满足前置条件，并以稀疏终端反馈触发奖励纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作中动作有效性依赖先前交互，但仅凭视觉进展可能掩盖缺失的任务前置条件，导致奖励与真实可执行进展不一致。

Method: 以因果一致性为核心目标，使奖励进展与执行历史支持的前置条件对齐；采用阶段打乱监督增强执行顺序敏感性，循环记忆保留早期交互证据，两轮训练利用预测进展细化轨迹片段；所得奖励引导阶段条件策略从行为克隆逐步精炼到在线强化学习，并用稀疏终端反馈将策略经验连接到奖励学习，对重复高分失败触发纠正。

Result: 在88个RLBench任务和Open-X数据上预训练后，于未参与奖励预训练的18个长时程任务上平均成功率达31.8%，比最强评估基线高9.2个百分点；在4个真实机器人任务上成功率达57.5%，比对比方法高32.5个百分点；奖励级控制实验检验对合成顺序扰动的敏感性，消融实验分析各组件对策略改进的贡献。

Conclusion: RoboLongR通过历史依赖奖励与因果一致性约束，有效缓解视觉进展掩盖前置条件缺失的问题，并在长时程仿真与真实机器人任务上显著优于基线。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires actions whose validity depends on earlier interactions, yet visual progress can conceal missing task prerequisites. We propose RoboLongR, an offline-to-online framework that learns history-dependent rewards to guide policy adaptation to new tasks. Its central objective is causal consistency: aligning rewarded progress with prerequisites supported by execution history. Stage-shuffle supervision encourages execution-order sensitivity, recurrent memory preserves evidence of earlier interactions, and two-round training refines trajectory segments using predicted progress. The resulting rewards guide stage-conditioned policy refinement from behavior cloning to online reinforcement learning. Sparse terminal feedback connects policy experience to reward learning by triggering correction for repeated high-scoring failures. Pretrained on demonstrations from 88 RLBench tasks and Open-X data, RoboLongR achieves 31.8% average success on 18 long-horizon tasks excluded from reward pretraining, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.2 percentage points. Across four real-robot tasks, it achieves 57.5% success, a 32.5-percentage-point improvement over the comparison method. Reward-level controls probe sensitivity to synthetic order perturbations, while ablations examine the contributions of individual components to policy improvement.

</details>


### [101] [AdaDexTrack: Dynamic Modulation for Adaptive and Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33852)
*Jianibieke Adalibieke,Qianwei Han,Xueyi Liu,Yuzhe Qin,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaDexTrack 将语言条件灵巧手-物体操作重构为“带调制器的闭环跟踪”，通过参考、物体潜表示和目标位置三类在线反馈修正，缓解参考噪声、重定向具身偏差与误差累积，提升泛化与实机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单条语言指令难以直接生成长期接触丰富的手-物体交互：合成参考有噪声，人到机器人重定向存在具身偏差，固定参考跟踪会使小误差滚雪球。

Method: 提出 modulator-in-the-loop 框架：以蒸馏得到的通用跟踪器作为技能载体；紧耦合调制器进行三项反馈修正——参考调制（持续调整跟踪目标）、物体潜调制（在线适配物体表示以调用合适技能）、位置目标调制（状态相关的小幅执行修正）。跟踪器通过大规模专家到通用跟踪器蒸馏学习；调制器在同一任务目标下用强化学习训练，保证耦合。

Result: 大规模评估中，AdaDexTrack 在未见轨迹和未见物体集合上的平均跟踪误差与成功率均持续优于先前 SOTA；并在真实硬件上实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，加入调制器相较仅跟踪器版本有显著提升。

Conclusion: AdaDexTrack 将语言条件灵巧操作重新表述为调制跟踪，用闭环调制替代开环固定参考跟踪，调整参考、物体潜表示和位置目标，从而在噪声文本参考下实现抗漂移执行。

Abstract: Language is a natural way to command robots, but converting a single instruction into a long-horizon, contact-rich hand-object interaction remains challenging: synthesized references are noisy, human-to-robot retargeting introduces embodiment bias, and fixed-reference tracking lets small errors snowball. We address this with AdaDexTrack, a modulator-in-the-loop framework for language-conditioned manipulation tracking. A distilled generalist tracker serves as the skill carrier, while a tightly aligned modulator performs three feedback corrections: reference modulation (continual adjustment of what to track), object-latent modulation (online adaptation of the object representation to recruit suitable skills), and positional-target modulation (small state-dependent refinements for execution). The tracker is learned via large-scale specialist to generalist distillation on a corpus of language-conditioned hand-object trajectories; the modulator is trained with RL under the same task objective, ensuring tight coupling. Across large-scale evaluations, AdaDexTrack consistently outperforms prior SOTA on unseen-trajectory and unseen-object sets in both average tracking error and success rate, demonstrating robustness and generalization. We further show zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on real hardware, where adding the modulator yields substantial gains over a tracker-only variant. AdaDexTrack reframes language-conditioned dexterous manipulation as modulated tracking, replacing the open-loop, fixed-reference tracking with in-loop modulation that adjusts the reference, object latent, and positional target, yielding drift-resistant execution from noisy text references.

</details>


### [102] [RoboWorM: Evaluating Movement Realism, Consistency and Acceleration Affects in Embodied Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33807)
*Doanh Le Thien,Viet-Thanh Nguyen,Pham Tri Quang,Nhi Pham,Christopher Wewer,Minh-Loi Nguyen,Hoang H. Tran,Tho Le,Tung M. Luu,Dung D. Le,Vu N. Duong,Jonas Fischer,Ngan Le,An Thai Le,Daniel Sonntag,Gim Hee Lee,James Zou,Mathias Niepert,Jan Peters,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向机器人操作视频世界模型（VWMs）的部署感知基准 RoboWorM，并给出无需 VLM 的物理一致性指标 KIVA 与 RIGIS，系统评估结构/时空保真度及质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多将 VWMs 仅作为开放世界视频生成器评估，依赖缺乏严格 3D 空间与运动学推理的 VLM；真实部署还需要低延迟，并关注加速是否破坏物理约束。

Method: RoboWorM 利用机器人运动学树和固定相机配置等硬件先验，构建覆盖单臂、双臂、人形形态，Markovian 与长时非 Markovian 任务，单/多视角设置的 1,605 个 episode；提出确定性、VLM-free 的 KIVA（视角内运动学稳定性）和 RIGIS（多视角几何一致性）指标，并大规模审计 3 个领先世界模型上的 9 种加速技术（超 29,000 条 rollout）。

Result: KIVA 相对最强前沿 VLM 将幻觉检测与人工标注对齐提升 25.8%；RIGIS 与人类偏好的 Spearman 相关性提升 130%。加速审计显示部分方法保持表层视觉质量却悄然损害部署关键物理约束；利用所提指标可实现最高 2.3 倍推理加速且质量损失最小。

Conclusion: RoboWorM 提供了部署感知、可系统检验结构与时空保真度的 VWM 基准，并表明需用机器人内在不变量评估物理一致性；其指标可在加速部署中兼顾效率与物理可靠性。

Abstract: Video world models (VWMs) are rapidly emerging as scalable neural simulators for robotic manipulation. However, most benchmarks only evaluate them as open-world video generators, using Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that lack strict 3D spatial and kinematic reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboWorM, a comprehensive, deployment-aware benchmark that leverages known hardware priors such as robot kinematic trees and fixed camera configurations, to systematically examine structural and spatiotemporal fidelity. RoboWorM curates 1,605 episodes across single-arm, bimanual, and humanoid morphologies, spanning both Markovian and long-horizon non-Markovian tasks under single- and multi-view setups. To explicitly measure physical consistency, we propose two deterministic, VLM-free metrics: KIVA for intra-view kinematic stability, and RIGIS for multi-view geometric consistency. By exploiting intrinsic robotic invariants, KIVA improves hallucination detection alignment with human annotations by 25.8% relative to the strongest frontier VLMs, while RIGIS achieves a 130% higher Spearman correlation with human preferences. Furthermore, since real-world deployment requires low inference latency, we utilize RoboWorM to conduct the first large-scale audit of the quality-efficiency trade-off. Evaluating 9 state-of-the-art acceleration techniques across 3 leading world models (over 29,000 generated rollouts), we demonstrate how these acceleration methods can retain superficial visual quality while silently compromising deployment-critical physical constraints. Using our metrics, we achieve 2.3$\times$ maximum inference speed-up with minimal quality loss.

</details>


### [103] [One Scan is Enough: Demonstration-Free Adaptation for Language-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33794)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxing Long,Du Wang,Junyang Wang,Hongwei Fan,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需人类演示的场景自适应框架：用一次 iPhone 扫描重建可训练环境并自动生成导航指令数据，再以闭式 Pareto 效用实现策略适配，在真实机器人上显著提升语言引导导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导导航策略（VLA）部署到新建筑时，会因布局、外观和语言分布漂移而逐场景退化；现场遥操作采集新轨迹成本高、难以扩展，亟需无需人类演示的可扩展场景适配方法。

Method: Scan2Instruction 将一次简短的 iPhone 扫描编译为完整训练环境，自动合成带 grounded instructions 的可执行导航 episode；PRPT-KS 直接从重建场景计算闭式 Pareto utility 来适配策略，无需学习 critic，也无需人工标签。

Result: 在五个真实环境中，RealNav 达到 85% 真实机器人成功率，而使用人类演示后训练的 RealNav 基线为 49%；在同一建筑未扫描区域仍达到 82%，同一基线为 41%。

Conclusion: 该工作将语言引导导航的免演示场景适配从愿景变为可行现实，显著优于依赖人类演示的后训练基线，并具备对未扫描区域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deploying a language-guided navigation policy in a new building usually means watching it fail. Public-benchmark VLAs degrade scene by scene as layout, appearance, and language drift, and the standard fix of teleoperating fresh trajectories on site does not scale. We replace human demonstration with a single brief iPhone scan. Scan2Instruction compiles that scan into a complete training environment, automatically synthesizing executable navigation episodes with grounded instructions. PRPT-KS then adapts the policy through a closed-form Pareto utility read directly off the reconstructed scene, with no learned critic and no human labels. Across five real environments, the two designs together drive our RealNav to an 85% real-robot success rate, compared with 49% for a RealNav baseline post-trained on human demonstrations, and still achieve 82% in unscanned regions of the same buildings, compared with 41% for the same baseline. Our work turns demonstration-free scene adaptation for language-guided navigation from an aspiration into a working reality.

</details>


### [104] [RoboReason: Boosting Robotics-Relevant VLM Reasoning Through Scalable Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33778)
*Sagnik Anupam,Utkarsh Lal,Aneesh Edara,Botong Zhang,Yao Tang,Jiatao Gu,Dinesh Jayaraman,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出利用真实机器人立体相机和腕部相机图像上的时空排序任务来评估视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉推理能力，并针对现有模型表现不足提出可扩展的自主训练数据生成流程，通过监督微调和强化学习显著提升VLM在排序任务及下游机器人和视觉推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM已展现出在机器人操作流程中的潜力，但在真实机器人下游任务上评估其能力困难且昂贵，难以规模化；因此需要与机器人应用直接相关、可扩展的视觉推理评测任务。

Method: 设计三类排序任务：根据任务语言描述对打乱帧排序、按相机距离进行深度排序、按像素坐标从左到右排序；任务复杂度可通过改变待排序集合大小调节。实验评估多个最先进VLM，并提出可扩展的自主训练数据生成流程，使用监督微调和强化学习在生成数据上训练。

Result: 多个最先进VLM在排序任务上表现不佳；基于生成数据的监督微调和强化学习均能显著提升VLM在时空排序任务以及下游机器人和视觉推理任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 真实机器人相机图像上的时空排序任务是评估VLM机器人相关视觉推理能力的合适基准；所提出的自主数据生成与训练方法可有效改善VLM推理性能，并迁移到下游任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance when integrated into robot manipulation pipelines, but real-world evaluation of VLM capabilities on downstream robotics tasks is both difficult and expensive to scale. We propose that spatial and temporal ordering tasks using images from stereo and wrist cameras attached to real-world robots are particularly well-suited for evaluating visual reasoning capabilities, as they assess skills directly applicable to robots. We evaluate VLM reasoning abilities on three questions: ordering shuffled frames conditioned on a language description of the task, ordering objects depthwise based on distance from the camera, and ordering objects from left-to-right in pixel coordinates. The complexity of these tasks can also be modulated by changing the size of the set to sort. Our experiments show that several state-of-the-art vision-language models struggle on ordering tasks. To fix this, we propose a scalable autonomous training data generation pipeline, and show that both supervised finetuning and reinforcement-learning on the generated data can significantly improve VLM reasoning performance both on spatiotemporal ordering tasks as well as downstream robotics and visual reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [105] [POD-RoPE: Rotary Position Encoding over Physical Observation Distributions for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33745)
*Xiaodi Huang,Zilin Wang,Jinpeng Liu,Pengchuan Xiao,Zhenlei Shao,Zhang Zhang,Shuxiang Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出POD-RoPE，将token的观测足迹建模为相机中心、视角方向和采样时间上的分布，用期望旋转编码相对几何关系，并在注意力中同时指导Q/K匹配与V/O聚合，实现几何感知且高效的具身注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身感知需要同时捕捉物理几何以及观测的空间和时间范围；现有位置编码难以统一表达这些信息，且几何感知注意力常带来序列增长或高计算开销。

Method: POD-RoPE将每个token的观测足迹表示为相机中心、观察方向和采样时间的分布；在独立采样假设下，用期望旋转编码分布间相对关系，分布退化为单点时恢复pointwise RoPE；同一旋转统计用于Q/K匹配和依赖相干性的V/O变换；采用逐token预聚合，避免增加序列长度或处理观测节点对。

Result: 在4种机器人策略架构、仿真与真实操作、重建/深度估计/新视角合成任务上评估；集成到Fast-WAM后，RoboTwin 2.0全部50个任务平均成功率从91.83%提升至92.88%，Sharpa North两个真实机器人任务从32.5%提升至52.5%；重建消融支持分布编码和V/O变换贡献；H20模型块基准中，前向+反向中位加速分别为P-RoPE的1.91倍和RayRoPE的5.34倍，相对Standard RoPE中位延迟开销17.4%。

Conclusion: 物理观测分布可作为有效且计算高效的几何感知注意力基础，兼顾几何表达、策略性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Embodied perception requires positional representations that capture both physical geometry and the spatial and temporal extent of observations. We introduce POD-RoPE (Physical Observation Distribution RoPE), which represents token observation footprints as distributions over camera centers, viewing directions, and sampling times. Under independent sampling, expected rotations encode relative relationships between distributions; when each distribution collapses to a single address, the construction recovers pointwise RoPE. The same rotation statistics support Q/K matching and coherence-dependent V/O transformations, allowing observation geometry to guide both information selection and content aggregation. Per-token pre-aggregation avoids increasing sequence length or processing pairs of observation nodes. We evaluate POD-RoPE across four robot policy architectures, simulation and real-world manipulation, and complementary reconstruction, depth-estimation, and novel-view-synthesis tasks. Integrating POD-RoPE into Fast-WAM improves mean success from 91.83% to 92.88% across all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, and from 32.5% to 52.5% on two real-robot tasks on Sharpa North. Controlled reconstruction ablations support the contributions of distribution encoding and V/O transformations. In H20 model-block benchmarks, POD-RoPE achieves median forward-plus-backward speedups of $1.91\times$ over P-RoPE and $5.34\times$ over RayRoPE, with a median latency overhead of 17.4% over Standard RoPE. These results support physical observation distributions as an effective and computationally efficient basis for geometry-aware attention.

</details>


### [106] [Floor Friction Assessment from Robot Tire-Surface Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33735)
*Kulbhushan Komarraju,Nathaniel Pearson,Yu Gu,Jason Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用轮式滑移转向机器人在五种不同牵引特性地面上的轮速里程计与惯性估计速度不匹配，提取时域响应延迟和频域谱特征，实现地面类型识别并达到97.1%的平衡分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式移动机器人在GNSS拒止室内环境中依赖轮速里程计，地板牵引力会影响打滑、航位推算和导航性能；作者希望利用机器人自身测量表征地面条件并改善定位。

Method: 让滑移转向机器人按可诱发滑移的指令速度曲线在五种地面上重复加速和换向；比较惯性传感估计的机体速度与轮速里程计速度，计算积分相对速度误差、相位导出的轮-体响应延迟和速度反向响应延迟；对正负加速度下的速度不匹配做Welch功率谱密度分析，提取带内积分谱功率、谱质心和谱衰减率，并用线性判别分析和多类分类评估。

Result: 响应延迟呈现一致的地面相关趋势：肥皂地面延迟最长，地毯最短；谱特征在测试地面间实现清晰分离，在记录级交叉验证下取得97.1%的平衡分类准确率。

Conclusion: 轮-体速度不匹配的幅值和频域特征均包含有用的轮胎-地面相互作用信息，可用于地面识别，并支持机器人状态估计框架中考虑滑移的轮速里程计加权。

Abstract: Floor traction influences wheel-slip behavior, dead-reckoning accuracy, and the navigation performance of wheeled mobile robots that rely on wheel odometry. As these robots become increasingly common in GNSS-denied indoor environments, including automated manufacturing and retail facilities, their onboard measurements could also be used to characterize floor conditions and improve localization. In this study, a skid-steer robot was driven over five floor surfaces with distinct traction characteristics using commanded velocity profiles designed to induce wheel slip through repeated acceleration and direction reversal. Differences between the body velocity estimated from inertial sensing and the velocity obtained from wheel odometry were evaluated using the integrated relative-velocity error, phase-derived wheel-to-body response delay, and velocity-reversal response delay. Welch power spectral density analysis was additionally applied to characterize the frequency distribution of the velocity mismatch during positive and negative acceleration. Band-integrated spectral power, spectral centroid, and spectral decay rate were extracted from each recording and evaluated using linear discriminant analysis and multiclass classification. The response-delay measurements produced consistent surface-dependent trends, with the longest delays observed on soap and the shortest delays observed on carpet. The spectral features also provided clear separation among the tested surfaces, achieving a balanced classification accuracy of 97.1% under recording-level cross-validation. These results demonstrate that both the magnitude and frequency domain characteristics of wheel-to-body velocity mismatch contain useful information about tire-surface interactions. Such information could support surface recognition and the development of slip-aware wheel-odometry weighting within robot state-estimation frameworks.

</details>


### [107] [RoboJev: An Open Vision-Language Judge for Robot Success Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33718)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboJev-27B 是一个开放权重的视觉语言模型，用于根据任务指令和机械臂操作视频帧判断机器人是否成功完成任务，并在同型号机器人上达到很高准确率，同时具备一定跨实验室/跨机器人泛化能力，且发布推理代码与透明评测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器人操作视频没有标注任务是否成功，因此需要一种能够自动检测操作成功与否的模型，以支持视频理解、评测和后续学习。

Method: RoboJev-27B 基于 JEV-27B-VL，仅在语言模型上使用 rank-32 LoRA 适配器训练，视觉编码器保持冻结；训练数据为 52,598 条 episode 记录。模型输入任务指令、10 帧操作尝试帧以及可用的辅助相机最终帧，采用仅对研究数据生效的 source-specific KL 惩罚，并通过校准的两 token 读出在单次前向中输出成功/失败概率。

Result: 在 SO-101 同型号机器人的 268 个留出 episode 上，RoboJev-27B 达到 95.2% balanced accuracy 和 0.993 AUROC；在未见实验室/机器人的 459 个 episode 上 AUROC 为 0.890；在清晰标注的社区采集 135 个 episode 上准确率为 93.3%。基座模型在相同输入的未见实验室/机器人子集上 AUROC 为 0.900。单张 H100 串行推理中位数为每 episode 0.26 秒，按每 GPU 小时 2.69 美元计算约为每 1,000 次成功预测 0.19 美元，不含媒体处理和 serving 成本。作者还发布推理代码及包含 20,628 条预测、29 个模型/协议条目的透明评测，并与仅输入主相机 4 帧的 API 前沿 VLM 比较，但指出该比较并非严格同条件。

Conclusion: RoboJev-27B 是一个有效的开放权重机器人操作成功检测模型，在同型号机器人上表现很强，并能泛化到未见实验室/机器人和社区数据；其发布代码与评测数据有助于可复现基准测试，但与前排 API VLM 的比较需考虑输入帧数不同这一限制。

Abstract: Many robot manipulation videos do not indicate whether the task was successfully completed. RoboJev-27B is an open-weight vision-language model that takes a task instruction, 10 frames of a manipulation attempt, and (if available) final frames from auxiliary cameras and predicts whether the robot accomplished the task. RoboJev-27B is a variant of JEV-27B-VL, trained with a rank-32 LoRA adapter on the language model only (keeping the vision encoder frozen) using 52,598 episode records. It also uses a source-specific KL penalty that applies only to research data, anchoring predictions closer to the original model on those sources. It uses a calibrated two-token readout that outputs success/failure probabilities in a single forward pass. On 268 held-out episodes of the same robot model (SO-101), RoboJev-27B attains 95.2% balanced accuracy and 0.993 AUROC. It also generalises to episodes collected by unseen labs/robots (AUROC = 0.890 over 459 episodes) as well as to clearly labelled community-collected episodes (accuracy = 93.3% over 135 episodes), where the base model attains an AUROC = 0.900 on the unseen-lab/robot subset with the same inputs. It takes 0.26 seconds per episode (median, serial) to infer on a single H100, which is about \$0.19 per 1,000 success predictions at \$2.69 per GPU-hour (not including media processing and serving costs). RoboJev-27B is released together with its inference code, along with a transparent evaluation: 20,628 predictions organized into 29 model/protocol entries are made available to facilitate browsing and to enable reproducible benchmarking of success detection. We also compare against strong API-based frontier VLMs, which take in fewer input frames (4 images from the primary camera only); note that this comparison is not strictly like-for-like because RoboJev-27B is given more frames at inference.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics Through Hilbert-Space Encoding of Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33715)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种保结构量子表示，将刚体变换与串联机械臂运动学映射为参数化量子电路，并据此重构末端位姿与几何雅可比，进而提出混合闭环逆运动学方法 QCLIK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算融入机器人学需要能保持机器人运动几何结构与构型依赖的数学表示；同时需建立经典运动学模型与参数化量子电路之间的对应关系。

Method: 基于 Bloch 球将空间方向编码为单量子比特态，用量子旋转复现参考系变换的有序复合；平移项通过量子态聚合处理并经典保留度量尺度，从 Pauli 期望值重构末端位置与姿态。该表示保留原始关节变量并兼容不同运动学约定与关节类型。进一步利用参数移位规则从量子可观测量导数重构机械臂几何雅可比，并提出利用量子导出的位姿与雅可比信息的混合闭环逆运动学方法 Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics（QCLIK）。

Result: 该表示可保持运动学几何结构并重构末端位姿；在理想读出下，基于参数移位规则的量子可观测量导数与经典几何雅可比解析等价；QCLIK 展示了该表示可用于机器人级算法。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人运动学与运动规划中的量子及混合计算方法提供了具有几何可解释性的基础。

Abstract: The integration of quantum computing into robotics requires mathematical representations that preserve the geometric structure and configuration dependence of robot motion.
