This paper introduces a structure-preserving quantum representation of rigid-body transformations and serial-manipulator kinematics, establishing a correspondence between classical kinematic models and parameterized quantum circuits. Building on the Bloch-sphere representation, spatial directions are encoded into single-qubit states, while quantum rotations reproduce the ordered composition of reference-frame transformations. Translational contributions are combined through quantum-state aggregation, with their metric scales retained classically, enabling the reconstruction of end-effector position and orientation from Pauli expectation values. The formulation preserves the original joint variables and accommodates different kinematic conventions and joint types. Beyond forward kinematics, we establish that the manipulator geometric Jacobian can be reconstructed from quantum-observable derivatives using the parameter-shift rule, with analytical equivalence to its classical counterpart under ideal readout. To demonstrate the applicability of this representation to robot-level algorithms, we introduce Quantum Closed Loop Inverse Kinematics, a hybrid closed-loop method that exploits quantum-derived pose and Jacobian information. The proposed framework provides a geometrically interpretable foundation for developing and investigating quantum and hybrid computational methods for robotic kinematics and motion.

</details>


### [109] [Open-World Law-to-Action: Vision-Language Priors and Touch for Parts with Hidden Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33705)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Open-World Law-to-Action（L2A），让机器人在未见家庭中利用 VLM 先验与在线触觉交互识别可动部件的运动规律，并决定是否再看一眼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人面对从未见过的门、抽屉、盖子和水龙头时，图像无法揭示其物理规律；而“把抽屉轻轻开到一半”等指令依赖部件行程、阻力和锁定机制，因此需要在开放世界中在线辨识物体运动规律。

Method: VLM 为每个可动部件提出运动方式、运动定律项及可能的锁定因素，这些提议作为带下限的先验，使触觉总能推翻先验。机器人用带闭式证据的贝叶斯线性回归在线辨识单模式或双模式运动规律，并保留“未见部件或运动锁定目标”的假设。作者证明仅靠触觉无法排除该假设，推导额外观察何时值得其代价，并说明部件单向运动时恒定负载与摩擦不可区分。

Result: 仿真中在开发场景上构建、在留出场景上单次运行。新厨房中机器人从 VLM 提议出发完成任务 80%，所有部件已知时为 98%。所提物理先验使需要新项的物体成功率从 353/400 提升到 378–383/400；第二次观察使混合场景成功率从 284/366 提升到 329/366。移动机器人不从仿真器获取信息时在新场景完成 70/100 个请求，而由仿真器告知每个盒子对应部件时为 62（两次留出测试间做了一次修改）。

Conclusion: L2A 表明将 VLM 语义先验、可被触觉推翻的在线贝叶斯物理辨识和主动观察结合，可提升未见环境中机器人操作可动部件的成功率；但当前验证限于仿真，且触觉辨识存在可证明的不可区分性与锁定假设限制。

Abstract: A robot in a home it has never seen must open doors, drawers, lids and taps whose physics a picture does not show. Instructions such as "open the drawer halfway, gently" depend on that physics: how far a part travels, how it resists, and what locks it. We present Open-World Law-to-Action (L2A). A vision-language model (VLM) proposes, for each movable part, its motions, the terms of its law of motion, and what may lock it. Each proposal becomes a prior with a floor, so that touch can always overturn it. The robot identifies each part's law online by Bayesian linear regression with closed-form evidence, for parts that move in one way or in two, and keeps a hypothesis that an unseen part or motion locks the target. We prove that touch alone cannot rule this hypothesis out, derive when another look is worth its cost, and show that a constant load and friction cannot be told apart while a part moves one way. All experiments are in simulation, developed on some scenes and run once on held-out ones. In new kitchens the robot completes 80% of tasks from the VLM's proposals, against 98% when every part is known. Proposed physics raises success on objects that need new terms from 353 to 378-383 of 400 episodes, and a second look raises success in mixed scenes from 284 to 329 of 366 tasks. A mobile robot that takes nothing from the simulator completes 70 of 100 requests in new scenes, against 62 when the simulator says which part each box is (after one change made between two held-out tests).

</details>


### [110] [EXE: Egocentric Cross-Embodiment Pretraining via Factorized Latent Actions for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33685)
*Qingyuan Cai,Bo Zhang,Yongzhen Huang,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EXE——一种以第一人称人类操作视频进行跨具身预训练的框架，通过分解潜在动作表示分离共享操作先验与具身特定运动信号，从而缓解VLA模型机器人数据稀缺和具身差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于机器人数据稀缺；第一人称人类操作视频丰富、多样且语义强，但人手与机器人在运动学上存在具身差距，直接预训练会引入人类特定运动模式并干扰机器人策略学习。

Method: EXE从第一人称人类视频中学习因子化潜在动作表示，将可迁移的共享操作技能（如reach、grasp、place）与具身特定信号解耦；机器人策略利用共享动作空间获取可泛化操作语义，避免受人手运动学偏置影响，并支持不同运动学结构的具身迁移。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa GR1和真实世界操作任务上，EXE相较无预训练基线取得一致性能提升，且同一预训练可同时受益于平行夹爪和灵巧手。

Conclusion: 第一人称视频预训练可产生捕获可迁移操作技能的共享动作表示；通过显式解耦人类特定运动模式，EXE能有效实现跨具身知识迁移并提升机器人操作策略性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are bottlenecked by the scarcity of robot data. Egocentric human manipulation videos offer an abundant, diverse, and semantically-rich alternative that inherently captures manipulation intent. However, the embodiment gap makes it difficult to extract action-relevant representations from these videos. From the perspective of action execution, human hands and robots share analogous manipulation primitives---reaching, grasping, placing---yet exhibit distinct kinematics tied to their embodiments. This might motivate our key approach: egocentric video pretraining can yield shared action representations capturing transferable manipulation skills, while human-specific motion patterns should be disentangled to avoid interfering with robot policy learning. To this end, we propose EXE, an Egocentric Cross(X)-Embodiment pretraining framework that learns a factorized latent action representation from egocentric human videos, separating shared manipulation priors from embodiment-specific signals. By leveraging the shared action space, the robot policy acquires generalizable manipulation semantics from diverse human demonstrations without being biased by human-specific kinematics. This shared knowledge transfers across embodiments with different kinematic structures, enabling the same pretraining to benefit both parallel grippers and dexterous hands. EXE yields consistent gains over baseline without pretraining on RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa GR1, and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Energy-Based Fault Detection and Isolation Framework for Flat Port-Hamiltonian Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33679)
*Johnny Garcia,Nicolas Salguero,Naveed Iqbal,Brahim Brahmi,Ramy Rashad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于能量的SHAPE故障诊断框架，利用端口功率残差和CWT-CNN从完整轨迹中区分正常、执行器失效、异常摩擦与振动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受调节运动中的故障诊断需要有效信号，因为反馈会削弱跟踪偏差；同时希望避免依赖专用动态观测器的递归更新和状态动力学计算。

Method: SHAPE结合端口哈密顿变量与微分平坦性，从规划运动生成标称功率参考，再用指令力乘以测量流量减去参考得到机械端口功率残差；随后使用连续小波变换尺度图和共享编码器CNN进行分类。评估在模型预测控制下、存在固定模型-对象失配的六自由度Kinova机械臂仿真中进行，设置指定关节单故障。

Result: 在5个训练种子和3000次留出测试中，SHAPE-CWT平均准确率为84.69%，扭矩残差为81.67%，广义动量观测器为97.17%；对应样本标准差分别为0.70、0.70和0.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 代数功率残差可实现有用的故障类型判别，但严重度-激励剖面和离线速度扰动揭示了其诊断能力的局限。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis during regulated motion requires informative signals even when feedback attenuates tracking deviations. This paper develops an energy-based diagnostic framework that constructs mechanical port-power residuals without a dedicated dynamic observer. Spectral Hamiltonian Algebraic Power Evaluation (SHAPE) combines port-Hamiltonian variables and differential flatness to generate nominal power references from planned motion for fully actuated mechanical systems with collocated actuation. Subtracting these references from commanded effort multiplied by measured flow yields residuals whose final evaluation requires neither recursive observer updates nor measured-state dynamics calculations. Continuous wavelet transform (CWT) scalograms and a shared-encoder convolutional neural network are used to distinguish nominal operation, actuator loss of effectiveness, anomalous friction, and vibration from complete trajectories. Evaluation uses a simulated six degree of freedom Kinova manipulator under model predictive control and fixed plant-model mismatch, with single faults at a specified joint. Across five training seeds and 3000 held-out test runs, SHAPE-CWT achieves a mean accuracy of 84.69 percent, versus 81.67 percent for torque residuals and 97.17 percent for the generalized momentum observer under common CWT processing. The respective sample standard deviations are 0.70, 0.70, and 0.37 percentage points. These results demonstrate useful fault-type discrimination from algebraic power residuals, while severity-excitation profiles and offline velocity perturbations reveal limits to their diagnostic capability.

</details>


### [112] [HARP: Handoff-Aware Recovery Planning for Active Target Acquisition in Multi-UAV Handoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33640)
*Heegyeong Kim,Alice James,Avishkar Seth,Endrowednes Kuantama,Richard Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARP是一种面向多无人机连续空中跟踪的交接感知恢复规划器，可在接收机缺乏目标先验观测时主动获取可用视角，并避免干扰发送机现有覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空中跟踪需要多无人机长时间维持目标覆盖；交接时接收无人机可能缺乏目标先验观测，必须在不破坏发送机持续覆盖的前提下主动获取可用视角。

Method: HARP将发送机的有界预测配准到接收机局部地图，区分视场失败与物理遮挡，并生成针对失败类型的偏航、平移和视差视角；候选运动需检查接收机间隙、发送机基准可观测性、受保护的发送机—目标可见性、时间有效性和截止时间合规性；若无法接纳完整轨迹，则执行采样的可接纳非零前缀并基于更新证据重新规划。

Result: 仿真中HARP在全部49次标准试验和16次压力试验中的15次成功获取目标；外场试验验证了其在视场受限和环境遮挡下的恢复几何。

Conclusion: HARP能够在满足发送机覆盖保护约束的同时实现稳健的交接感知目标恢复，并得到仿真与外场试验验证。

Abstract: Continuous aerial tracking often requires multiple drones to maintain target coverage over long periods. During handoff, the receiving UAV may lack prior target observations and must actively acquire a usable view without disrupting the sender's ongoing coverage. This work introduces HARP, a handoff-aware recovery planner that registers bounded sender predictions in the receiver's local map, distinguishes field-of-view failure from physical occlusion, and generates failure-specific yaw, translation, and parallax viewpoints. Candidate motions are checked for receiver clearance, sender-fiducial observability, protected sender--target visibility, temporal validity, and deadline compliance. When a complete trajectory cannot be admitted, HARP may execute a sampled admissible nonzero prefix and replan from updated evidence. In simulation, HARP acquired the target in all 49 standard trials and in 15 of 16 stress trials. Field trials validated HARP's recovery geometry under field-of-view limitation and environmental occlusions.

</details>


### [113] [HA-DiPO: Diffusion Policy Optimization for Heterogeneous Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33584)
*Xuebin Li,Jinpeng Xiao,Zong Chen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Bruno Siciliano,Jiebao Sun,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HA-DiPO：一种基于CTDE的分层多智能体强化学习框架，用于异构腿式机器人协同搬运非固定共享载荷，高层扩散协调策略与低层本体运动控制解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构腿式机器人运动能力不同，且共享载荷未固定，协同搬运需同时处理团队协调与各本体运动控制，并在执行时仅依赖局部观测实现去中心化协作。

Method: HA-DiPO采用分层多智能体强化学习与CTDE：高层由集中式评论家利用团队价值估计联合训练各机器人独立的协调策略，并用扩散策略建模更灵活、可能多模态的动作分布；低层使用各本体特定的鲁棒运动策略跟踪协调指令。协调策略通过奖励驱动的仿真交互从零学习，无需示范；执行时各机器人仅用自身与载荷的局部观测生成协调指令，不依赖伙伴状态或机器人间通信。

Result: 仿真对比表明，HA-DiPO相比评估基线取得更高的最终平均回报和速度跟踪成功率，并具有更低的速度误差；真实实验进一步展示了四足-轮腿式机器人以及人-机器人协同搬运。

Conclusion: HA-DiPO通过解耦团队协调与本体运动控制，有效支持异构腿式机器人在非固定共享载荷下的去中心化协同搬运。

Abstract: Cooperative transport with heterogeneous legged robots requires coordinating different motion capabilities while supporting an unfixed shared payload. We present HA-DiPO (Heterogeneous-Agent Diffusion Policy Optimization), a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework under centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE). The framework decouples team-level coordination from embodiment-specific motion control: at the high level, a centralized critic uses team-value estimates to jointly train independent coordination policies, aligning robot-specific action responses with a shared transport objective; at the low level, embodiment-specific robust motion policies track the coordination commands. The high-level diffusion policies offer more flexible action distributions than single-Gaussian parameterizations, allowing potentially multimodal coordination responses to be represented. The coordination policies learn from scratch through reward-driven simulator interaction without demonstrations. At execution, each robot generates coordination commands using only local robot and payload observations, without partner-state inputs or inter-robot messages. Simulation comparisons show that HA-DiPO achieves higher final mean returns and velocity-tracking success rates, as well as lower velocity errors, than the evaluated baselines. Real-world experiments further demonstrate quadrupedal-wheeled-legged and human-robot cooperative transport.

</details>


### [114] [Memory-Aware Keyframe Management for Dense Maps on Memory-Constrained Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33574)
*Huichang Yun,Jaeyeul Kim,Seungho Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向同设备语言模型与稠密3D地图共存的内存受限建图方法：直接限制常驻地图内存，并以语义空间单元（房间/走廊）进行关键帧准入与释放，从而避免OOM且保持定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型和稠密3D地图共享设备内存，超出限制会导致OOM；现有地图系统按关键帧数量限制工作集，但单关键帧内存占用差异大，数量无法有效控制实际内存；基于位姿半径、固定栅格或图邻居的准入单元也会搬入不需要的地图部分。

Method: 直接对常驻地图内存设上限；利用离线计算并随地图携带的每关键帧常驻大小，在读取任何数据前预知每次准入成本；以建筑中的房间和走廊等语义空间单元为单位准入和释放关键帧，使机器人保留当前所在区域及即将进入区域，释放已通过区域；每次准入先测量常驻总量并腾出空间，再读取数据，保证预算不被超出。

Result: 在两块NVIDIA Jetson板卡上，所有运行均未超预算，语言模型吞吐量最多下降0.8%；无该机制时操作系统会杀死模型或地图。在该预算下，语义区域准入比按匹配区域数划分的几何分区多定位6-9个百分点的查询帧，约为RTAB-Map检索的两倍，且ATE更低。

Conclusion: 直接限制内存可使地图与语言模型共存，而按语义区域移动有界地图使其保持可用性。

Abstract: Running a language model and a dense 3D map on one device means the two share one memory, and exceeding its limit is an out-of-memory failure. Map systems that keep a bounded working set bound it by a count of keyframes, but the memory one keyframe occupies varies widely with what it carries, so a count does not control what the working set occupies. The unit of admission is as poorly matched, since a radius around the pose, a fixed cell, or the graph neighbors of the last matched keyframe all move parts of the map that are not needed. We therefore propose two decisions. First, the memory the resident map uses is bounded directly, and the cost of every admission is known before any data is read, from per-keyframe resident sizes computed offline and carried with the map. Second, keyframes are admitted and released at a semantic spatial unit, the rooms and corridors of the building, so that the robot holds the zone it occupies together with the zone it is about to enter while the zones it has passed are released. Every admission measures the resident total, makes room, and only then reads, so the budget is never overshot. On two NVIDIA Jetson boards the budget holds in every run and the model gives up at most 0.8 percent of its throughput, whereas without it the operating system kills the model or the map. Under that budget, and over the environments evaluated, admission by semantic zone localizes 6-9 points more of the query frames than a geometric partition of matched zone count and about twice as much as the retrieval of RTAB-Map, at a lower ATE. Bounding memory lets the map and the model coexist, and moving the map in semantic zones makes the bounded map usable.

</details>


### [115] [WorldFailBench: Do World Models Understand Policy Failures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33565)
*Zheng Zhang,Xiaoyang Tang,Shuaiqi Cheng,Jiakai Huang,Zhongbo Wu,Ziyi Zhang,Yuan Si,Zeyu Liu,Weibo Gao,Shunyu Liu,Shan You,Fei Wang,Xiaogang Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldFailBench，用于评估世界模型能否忠实模拟机器人策略的失败原因、失败阶段与成功到失败的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果的任务成功/失败评估无法判断世界模型是否真实再现策略如何失败；失败模拟失真会误导策略诊断，并高估其部署可靠性。

Method: 构建包含三项任务的基准：失败原因测试模型是否复现可观察错误；失败阶段评估失败是否集中在完整任务执行的相同阶段；失败边界检验模型能否在受控场景变化下复现策略由成功转为失败的条件。覆盖 LIBERO、RoboTwin 仿真环境及 G1 人形机器人真实操作任务。

Result: 在七种世界模型上的实验显示，它们对策略失败的理解存在显著差距。

Conclusion: WorldFailBench 揭示了世界模型作为策略评估器的失败保真度不足，为开发更可靠的策略评估世界模型提供指导。

Abstract: Robot policy evaluation informs deployment decisions and policy improvement, but physical testing and simulator preparation require substantial effort. World models offer a complementary route through closed-loop interaction in generated environments. Recent benchmarks have demonstrated their potential as policy evaluators through outcome-based evaluation (i.e., assessing task success or failure). However, such evaluation does not establish whether world models faithfully simulate how policies fail. Misrepresenting failures can lead to incorrect diagnoses of policy weaknesses and make policies appear ready for deployment even when small scene changes cause them to fail. We therefore ask: do world models faithfully simulate how and at which stages policies fail, and under what conditions they transition from success to failure? To answer this question, we introduce WorldFailBench with three evaluation tasks. Failure reason tests whether models reproduce the observable errors policies make. Failure stage assesses whether failures concentrate at the same stages of full-task execution. Failure boundary examines whether models reproduce the conditions under which policies transition from success to failure under controlled scene changes in simulation. The benchmark covers manipulation tasks in the LIBERO and RoboTwin simulation environments and real-world manipulation tasks performed by the G1 humanoid robot. Experiments across seven world models reveal substantial gaps in their understanding of policy failures, providing guidance for developing more reliable world models for policy evaluation.

</details>


### [116] [MOON: Learning When to Forget and How to Move On in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33546)
*Jingmeng Zhou,Seson Sun,Binyao Xu,Lingming Zhang,Hanshi Wang,Fudong Ge,Weiming Hu,Jin Gao,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言导航（VLN）策略因视觉历史导致的行为惯性问题，提出MOON：在冻结VLN策略上加入偏差判断器与恢复策略，通过“何时遗忘历史”实现偏差恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN策略依赖视觉历史，错误动作会使智能体偏离指令路线；保留的历史还会阻碍必要转向，形成行为惯性。干预实验表明，清除历史的收益取决于时机：偏差开始时清除可显著提升成功，在线路上清除反而有害，偏差后期仅移除偏离路线帧帮助有限。

Method: MOON为冻结VLN策略增加两个轻量模块，参数量小于原模型1%。偏差判断器预测智能体是否偏离路线，并在需要时触发警报以清除历史；恢复策略从警报状态出发，用GRPO训练，学习偏离后如何继续行动，将仅靠采样才能找到的成功转化为单次贪心 rollout。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，MOON在所比较方法中取得最高R2R-CE成功率，并将冻结基座成功率分别提升5.2和6.0个百分点；随机触发消融表明增益依赖于清除历史的时机。无需重训练，MOON还提升了HM3D-OVON上的零样本物体目标导航。

Conclusion: 偏差恢复的关键不仅在于“如何行动”，更在于“何时遗忘历史”；适时清除导致行为惯性的视觉历史，再配合学习到的恢复策略，可有效提升VLN及零样本导航性能。

Abstract: Recent vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policies act on their visual history, and an incorrect action can take them off the instructed route. Paired interventions on four VLN policies show that clearing the history doubles pooled success at the onset of a deviation but lowers it on route, while later in a deviation, removing only the off-route frames helps much less. After a deviation, the retained history keeps the policy from turning where a turn is needed, which we call behavioral inertia. We propose MOON, a framework that equips a frozen VLN policy with two lightweight modules, adding less than 1% of its parameters. A deviation judge decides when to forget, raising an alarm that clears the history when it predicts that the agent is off route. A recovery policy, trained with GRPO from alarm states, learns how to move on, turning more of the successes found only by sampling into a single greedy rollout. Experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE show that MOON reaches the highest R2R-CE success rate among the compared methods and raises the success rate of its frozen base by 5.2 and 6.0 points, and an ablation with a random trigger indicates that this gain depends on when the history is cleared. Without retraining, MOON also improves zero-shot object-goal navigation on HM3D-OVON. Together, these results suggest that deviation recovery depends on knowing when to forget, not only how to act. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [MimicAll: Structured Embodiment Conditioning for Cross-Humanoid Whole-Body Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33508)
*Jia Li,Lingyun Xu,Yitang Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicAll 是一个跨异构人形机器人的强化学习全身运动跟踪框架，通过结构化具身描述符与异构仿真实现共享策略，并在多体型基准上验证运动泛化与跨家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动跟踪器可复现多样行为，但跨不同结构、形态和动力学的身体共享策略需显式处理具身差异。

Method: 提出 MimicAll：将结构化具身描述符编码为全局条件与关节对齐条件，与观测历史及身体匹配的运动参考结合，预测关节目标偏移；采用物理一致的具身随机化、身体校准重定向和异构仿真，覆盖16个人形家族、4096个变体；构建包含5个分布层、464个身体的基准，评估运动泛化、同家族具身偏移与未见家族迁移，并进行编码与条件消融。

Result: 消融显示具身信息可提升运动完成度，简单拼接有效而自注意力取得最低全局位置误差；相同训练转移下，共享训练在100STYLE随机G1身体上30秒成功率达99.4%，单身体专家为42.9%；Flat-MLP编码加拼接并在排除障碍类别的运动子集继续训练后，对采样训练身体和同家族分布外变体分别达98.3%与93.5%；在排除地面和起身序列的8运动LAFAN1子集、464个身体上，错配身体条件使自注意力30秒成功率从61.7%降至47.0%。

Conclusion: 具身信息在闭环跟踪中具有明确收益，共享策略可有效支持异构人形机器人的全身运动跟踪与跨体型泛化。

Abstract: Humanoid motion trackers reproduce diverse behaviors, but sharing a policy across bodies requires accounting for differences in structure, morphology and dynamics. We present MimicAll, a reinforcement-learning framework for shared whole-body motion tracking across heterogeneous humanoids. A structured embodiment descriptor is encoded into global and joint-aligned conditions, which are combined with observation history and body-matched motion references to predict joint-target offsets. Physics-consistent embodiment randomization, body-calibrated retargeting and heterogeneous simulation support training across 4,096 variants from 16 humanoid families. We introduce a benchmark of 464 bodies across five distribution strata to evaluate motion generalization, within-family embodiment shifts and unseen-family transfer. Controlled ablations show that embodiment information improves motion completion with simple concatenation, while self-attention yields the lowest global position errors. With equal training transitions, shared training achieves 99.4% 30-second success on randomized G1 bodies in 100STYLE, versus 42.9% for a single-body specialist. With Flat-MLP encoding and concatenation, continued training on a motion subset excluding obstacle categories achieves 98.3% and 93.5% 30-second success on 100STYLE for sampled training bodies and out-of-distribution variants within seen families, respectively. On an eight-motion LAFAN1 subset excluding ground and get-up sequences across 464 bodies, mismatched body conditions reduce self-attention's 30-second success from 61.7% to 47.0%, demonstrating a closed-loop benefit from embodiment information.

</details>


### [118] [ReSight: Task-Driven Self-Improving Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33487)
*Lirong Che,Yang Chen,Yimeng Su,Daqi Liu,Zhenyu Huang,Jiong Wang,Yuzhe Yang,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Jian Su,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSight 是一个面向具身智能体的任务驱动自改进记忆系统，通过分离观测证据与可扩展派生字段，并利用 Explore-Distill-Mount（EDM）从执行和探索记录中识别能力缺口、沉淀可复用工具与技能，从而持续扩展历史观测的处理与访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人虽可利用历史观测回答熟悉环境中的时空查询，但相关证据存在并不意味着现有工具支持任务所需操作；因此需要一种能随任务需求扩展记忆表示和访问能力的系统。

Method: ReSight 保留关键帧及其时空关联，将观测证据与可扩展派生字段分离，使已存观测可按新需求重处理；执行智能体在有限在线预算内调用注册工具，编码智能体使用可编程工作区和更大探索预算检查证据、编写程序并按需获取模型。EDM 从执行与探索记录中识别能力缺口（无需任务参考答案），并将探索所得方案转化为可复用的记忆构建器、查询工具和技能。

Result: 在三个公开基准上验证了基础记忆访问与能力扩展效果。固定执行模型和在线预算时，EDM 将 OC-NaVQA 平均位置误差从 33.10 m 降至 24.72 m，将 FindingDory 高层成功率从 38.3% 提升至 63.8%；冻结工具和技能后，在未参与探索与蒸馏的 FindingDory 片段上成功率仍提升 28.3 个百分点；真实机器人巡逻记忆实验表明这些能力可复用于后续查询和新记忆，且平均在线工具调用更少。

Conclusion: 任务驱动的自改进记忆系统能够在不依赖参考答案的情况下扩展具身智能体的记忆表示与工具能力，并实现跨查询、跨记忆的有效复用和更高效的在线访问。

Abstract: Robots can use historical observations to answer spatiotemporal queries in familiar environments, but the presence of relevant evidence does not imply that existing tools support the operations a task requires. We introduce ReSight, a task-driven self-improving memory system for embodied agents that expands the capabilities for processing and accessing historical observations as task demands arise. ReSight retains keyframes and their spatiotemporal associations, separating observation evidence from extensible derived fields so that stored observations can be reprocessed for new needs. An execution agent and a coding agent share this memory through different interfaces: the former calls registered tools within a bounded online budget, while the latter uses a programmable workspace and a larger exploration budget to inspect evidence, write programs, and acquire models as needed. To realize capability expansion in ReSight, we develop Explore-Distill-Mount (EDM), which identifies capability gaps from execution and exploration records without task reference answers and converts exploration-derived solutions into reusable memory builders, query tools, and skills, extending memory representations and access capabilities as needed. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate effective base memory access and capability expansion. With the execution model and online budget held fixed within each benchmark, EDM reduces mean position error on OC-NaVQA from 33.10 m to 24.72 m and improves high-level success on FindingDory from 38.3% to 63.8%. With the tools and skill frozen, success still improves by 28.3 percentage points on FindingDory episodes excluded from exploration and distillation. Experiments on real robot patrol memories further demonstrate reuse of these capabilities for subsequent queries and new memories, with fewer online tool calls on average.

</details>


### [119] [Learning Dexterous Manipulation: Hands, Simulators, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33486)
*Luai Abuelsamen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统盘点113种学习型灵巧手物体姿态重定向方法，统计其手型、仿真器、训练范式和评估报告，指出领域已高度集中于“GPU仿真特权状态RL蒸馏为纯视觉学生”的路线，并识别遥操作示范/VLA/扩散策略等快速增长分支及评估与硬件覆盖缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手能够在不放下物体的情况下改变物体位姿，但相关学习方法分散，所用硬件、仿真环境和结果报告缺乏统一认识，亟需系统梳理领域趋势、可复现性问题与未报告的关键物理量。

Method: 综述113篇学习型方法，统计任务族、手型、仿真器、训练流程和结果报告方式；并将论文与已发布代码及厂商规格对照，核查可购买或可按公开设计搭建的手是否被方法论文采用；在第VII节提出针对未报告量的报告协议。

Result: 近一半语料采用单一GPU仿真器中的特权状态强化学习并蒸馏为纯视觉学生以迁移硬件；另一条增长更快的分支基于遥操作示范，包括视觉-语言-动作模型、扩散策略及以数据采集装置为贡献的系统。发表量自2022年以来增长三倍；抓取仍为最大任务族，双臂协调增长最快；实验几乎集中于四种手；八种可购买或可搭建的手未出现在方法论文中；抓取深度即使被报告，也未按产生运动的物理过程测量。

Conclusion: 该领域训练范式高度集中，硬件覆盖和评估报告不足；需要标准化报告未报告物理量，并扩展硬件与物理测量覆盖，以提升可比性和可复现性。

Abstract: A dexterous hand can change an object's pose without setting it down. This survey covers 113 learning-based methods that attempt it, the hands they run on, the simulators they train in, and how they report results. The field has largely converged on one recipe: reinforcement learning on privileged state in a GPU simulator, distilled into a vision-only student for transfer to hardware. Almost half the corpus trains that way, nearly all of it in one simulator, whose own successors are barely used. A second and faster-growing branch learns from teleoperated demonstration instead, through vision-language-action models, diffusion policies, and systems whose contribution is the collection rig itself. Published work has tripled since 2022. Grasping remains the largest task family and bimanual coordination the fastest-rising, and almost every experiment runs on one of four hands. What the survey adds comes from reading released code and vendor specifications beside the papers. Eight hands that can be bought or built from published designs appear in no method paper here. Where the depth a hand sinks into the object it holds is reported at all, it is not measured in the physics that produced the motion. Section VII proposes a protocol for the unreported quantities; the body gives the counts behind every claim above.

</details>


### [120] [MDPO: Multi-constraint Dexterous Policy Optimization with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33480)
*Ao Zhou,Zongyou Yu,Ke Wu,Tao Xu,Junqiao Zhao,Wenchao Ding,Alois Knoll,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MDPO，将灵巧操作建模为同时包含累积成本约束与状态级成本约束的多约束策略优化问题，并通过多约束投影实现安全策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需高维控制并满足关节限位、手指构型安全、机械臂碰撞和物体交互稳定等多类安全要求；现有约束强化学习方法多关注累积成本约束，难以同时处理多种异构约束。

Method: 将灵巧操作表述为多约束策略优化问题，联合考虑累积成本约束和状态级成本约束；在策略优化中引入多约束投影，使用约束特定的策略投影，将最大化任务性能的中间策略映射到由累积成本约束和状态级成本约束定义的可行域。

Result: 在ReDMan的关节安全控制、手指安全和抓取任务上，MDPO相比基线在约束满足方面更优，同时保持有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: MDPO能够有效处理灵巧操作中的多类安全约束，在安全性与任务性能之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires high-dimensional control policies to accomplish complex contact-rich tasks while simultaneously satisfying multiple safety requirements, such as joint limits, unsafe finger configurations, arm collisions, and unstable object interactions. Constrained reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for incorporating these requirements into dexterous manipulation policy learning. However, existing constrained RL methods for dexterous manipulation primarily focus on cumulative cost constraints and struggle to simultaneously handle multiple heterogeneous constraints during dexterous policy learning. In this work, we propose Multi-constraint Dexterous Policy Optimization (MDPO) for safe dexterous manipulation. We first formulate dexterous manipulation as a multi-constraint policy optimization problem involving both cumulative cost constraints and state-wise cost constraints. Based on this formulation, we integrate multi-constraint projection into the dexterous manipulation policy optimization process. Specifically, we employ constraint-specific policy projections to map the intermediate policy that maximizes task performance onto the feasible regions defined by the cumulative cost constraint and the state-wise cost constraint. Extensive experiments on ReDMan, including safe joint control, finger safety, and grasping tasks, demonstrate that MDPO achieves superior constraint satisfaction while maintaining competitive task performance compared with the baselines. Our code is available at this link.

</details>


### [121] [A Differentiable Chassis Safety Layer for Intelligent Vehicle Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33476)
*Lu Xiong,Xuge Hu,Yufei Song,Weidong Liu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可微底盘安全层，将任务导向控制与显式物理约束模块化结合，通过序列二次规划与隐式微分实现时序梯度耦合，并用两阶段学习协调标称控制与底盘安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型车辆运动控制需协调多任务目标与状态相关底盘安全约束；传统级联架构中安全滤波独立修改标称指令，可能损害任务性能。

Method: 将执行器限制、单胎力约束和车辆操控包络纳入序列二次规划；每个投影指令传播预测车辆动力学并更新后续约束，借助隐式微分实现时序梯度耦合；采用专家模仿、风险引导监督和条件干预引导的两阶段学习策略。

Result: 在未见场景、紧急机动和消融实验的闭环评估中，该方法提升了安全性与性能的协调，并在保持操控稳定性的同时挖掘车辆动态潜力。

Conclusion: 可微底盘安全层能够有效耦合学习型任务控制与显式物理安全约束，实现安全-性能协同的车辆运动控制。

Abstract: Learning-based vehicle motion control requires coordination between diverse task objectives and state-dependent chassis safety constraints. Conventional cascaded architectures may compromise task performance when safety filters modify nominal commands independently of policy learning. This article proposes a differentiable chassis safety layer that modularly integrates task-oriented control with explicit physical constraints. Actuator limits, individual-tire force constraints, and vehicle handling envelopes are incorporated into sequential quadratic programs. Each projected command propagates the predicted vehicle dynamics and updates subsequent constraints, enabling temporal gradient coupling through implicit differentiation. A two-stage learning strategy combines expert imitation with risk-guided supervision and conditional intervention guidance to coordinate nominal control decisions with chassis safety constraints. Closed-loop evaluations under unseen scenarios, emergency maneuvers, and ablation studies demonstrate that the proposed method improves safety-performance coordination and exploits vehicle dynamic potential while maintaining handling stability.

</details>


### [122] [SCET-CBF: Speed-Calibrated Soft Guidance for One-Step Dynamic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33471)
*Xiaoyu Han,Dongkun Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCET-CBF：一种面向单步动态导航的速度校准软约束制导律，通过短时横向逃逸机动确定允许接近速度，并在QP中联合权衡制动对接近速度和横向运动的双重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF虽能避撞，但在密集动态相遇中易导致无可行指令；几何修正和软约束可保留运动选择，但仍需考虑车辆转向响应随速度变化，尤其是转向受限自行车模型中制动会同时降低接近速度和到达障碍前所需横向运动能力。

Method: SCET-CBF先利用短横向逃逸机动给出允许接近速度，并用其速度导数使二次规划在选择加速度和转向时权衡制动的双重效应；软惩罚在由分段和制动约束允许的输入集内协调竞争性障碍偏好。作者推导制导律，并证明每个被接受运动段上的保护性及单障碍情形下的条件制动不变性。

Result: 在2500个60秒截止的配对任务中，SCET完成72.8%，高于CBF/速度障碍控制的49.8%和增强1秒CBF-MPC的69.8%；在100个障碍场景中，SCET与增强MPC分别完成52.4%和33.6%。配对研究识别了速度反馈和提前激活的贡献，并进一步评估了横向加速度限制及有界运动/执行误差下的工作范围。

Conclusion: SCET-CBF通过速度校准的软制导和制动-转向联合权衡，在密集动态环境中显著提升可行性与任务完成率，并具备针对接受运动段和单障碍制动情形的理论保护保证。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) support collision avoidance, yet dense dynamic encounters can leave a vehicle with no feasible command. Geometric refinements and soft constraints help preserve motion choices. Using these choices also requires accounting for the vehicle's changing turning response. For a steering-limited bicycle, braking can lower closing speed while slowing the lateral motion needed before an obstacle arrives. We propose SCET-CBF, a speed-calibrated soft guidance law for one-step dynamic navigation. A short lateral escape maneuver gives an allowable approach speed. Its speed derivative allows a quadratic program to weigh both effects of braking when choosing acceleration and steering. Soft penalties accommodate competing obstacle preferences within the input set permitted by segment and braking constraints. We derive the guidance and establish protection over each accepted motion segment, together with conditional braking invariance for a single obstacle. On 2500 paired tasks with a 60 s deadline, SCET achieves 72.8% completion, compared with 49.8% for CBF/velocity-obstacle control and 69.8% for an enhanced 1 s CBF-MPC. At 100 obstacles, SCET and the enhanced MPC complete 52.4% and 33.6% of tasks. Paired studies identify the contributions of speed feedback and early activation. Further evaluations examine the operating range under lateral acceleration limits and bounded motion and execution errors.

</details>


### [123] [MOTIO: Agentic Skill Engineering for Reality-Grounded Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33446)
*SpatialtemporalAI*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOTIO 将人类动作视频、真实感 3D 资产、运动参考和可复用机器人技能转化为可执行的人形移动操作程序，无需任务专用遥操作或机器人演示采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人移动操作技能的获取通常依赖任务专用遥操作与机器人演示，难以扩展；作者希望利用普通人类动作视频、真实感 3D 资产和物理反馈，由智能体自动构建并验证技能。

Method: MOTIO 通过上下文中的任务与接触推理，让智能体构建分阶段的机器人原生交互，并利用物理执行、多帧反馈和人工验证迭代改进；Agentic 3D 提供度量级产品与环境资产，现有全身控制、逆运动学和灵巧手接口负责执行程序；生成的技能还可监督 BC/DAgger 蒸馏到共享参考策略。

Result: 在 33 次带时间戳的首次批准中，记录到的智能体单轮时间中位数为 97.0 分钟，从请求到批准为 2.80 小时；仿真侧布局与物体变化支持长时程瓶子处理技能，在扫描办公室中实现 G1 连续 101.75 秒物理执行；物理细化将拧螺丝的螺纹残差降低 76.0%；蒸馏后的共享参考策略在同一 checkpoint 下完成 18 个仿真任务，推理时无需专家干预。

Conclusion: 结果表明，智能体化技能工程可从人类视频与真实感 3D 资产生成、物理验证并蒸馏人形移动操作技能，显著降低对任务专用遥操作和机器人演示的依赖。

Abstract: We present MOTIO, an agentic skill-engineering system that turns ordinary human-action videos, reality-grounded 3D assets, motion references, and reusable robot skills into executable humanoid loco-manipulation programs. Through in-context task and contact reasoning, an agent constructs staged robot-native interactions and improves them using physics execution, multi-frame feedback, and human validation---without task-specific teleoperation or robot demonstration collection. Agentic 3D supplies metric product and environment assets, while existing whole-body, inverse-kinematics, and dexterous-hand interfaces realize the programs. Across 33 timestamped first approvals, the median recorded agent-turn time is 97.0 minutes and request-to-approval duration is 2.80 hours. Simulation-side layout and object variation prepare a long-horizon bottle-disposal skill for a continuous 101.75-second physical G1 execution in a scanned office; physical refinement reduces screw-tightening thread residual by 76.0%. Agent-generated skills additionally supervise BC/DAgger distillation into one shared reference policy, completing 18 simulated tasks under the same checkpoint without inference-time expert intervention. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [124] [TinyFlow: Breaking the Warping Bottleneck for Edge Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33338)
*Xiao'ao Song,Chahat Deep Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyFlow 是一种面向边缘加速器的无 warp 光流网络，通过固定窗口相关替代 flow-dependent warp，使整个网络可编译为单一静态 INT8 图，并在精度和部署效率上优于现有轻量光流网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能光流网络通常依赖迭代细化中的 warp 操作，需要基于当前光流进行重采样和数据相关内存访问，难以部署到资源受限的边缘加速器。

Method: 提出 1.26M 参数的 TinyFlow，保留由粗到细的迭代细化，但用固定窗口、常量偏移的相关运算替代 warp；结合量化感知微调、warp-teacher 蒸馏和 model souping，将 Float32 到 INT8 的精度损失降至 MPI-Sintel 上低于 0.2 EPE，并使全网可编译为单一静态 INT8 图。

Result: TinyFlow 在 Sintel Clean、Sintel Final 和 FlyingChairs 上，无论 Float32 还是 INT8，均优于 EdgeFlowNet 和 NanoFlowNet，且参数量比 EdgeFlowNet 少 54%；INT8 模型在 Sintel Clean/Final 上达到 6.29/7.13 EPE。在 TartanAir 和 UZH-FPV 的纯光流单目视觉里程计中，同一 INT8 模型在 11 条序列中的 7 条取得最低轨迹误差。

Conclusion: TinyFlow 通过消除 warp 依赖并实现静态 INT8 部署，在保持迭代细化能力的同时显著提升边缘部署友好性，并在光流估计和视觉里程计任务上取得有竞争力的精度与效率。

Abstract: High-performance optical flow networks are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge accelerators because their iterative refinement warps features by the current flow, which relies on flow-dependent resampling and data-dependent memory access. We present TinyFlow, a 1.26M-parameter warp-free optical flow network that preserves iterative coarse-to-fine refinement by replacing the warp with a fixed-window correlation over constant offsets, so the whole network compiles as a single static INT8 graph. Quantization-aware fine-tuning, warp-teacher distillation, and model souping reduce the Float32-to-INT8 penalty to below 0.2 end-point error (EPE) on MPI-Sintel. TinyFlow outperforms EdgeFlowNet and NanoFlowNet on Sintel Clean, Sintel Final, and FlyingChairs in both Float32 and INT8, while using 54% fewer parameters than EdgeFlowNet, and our INT8 model achieves 6.29/7.13 EPE on Sintel Clean/Final. In flow-only monocular visual odometry on TartanAir and UZH-FPV, the same INT8 model yields the lowest trajectory error on seven of eleven sequences.

</details>


### [125] [Active Reachability Learning for Humanoid Recoverability Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33317)
*Zeyuan Feng,Baljeet Singh,Sarthak Ranjeet Kaingade,Somil Bansal,Bryan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将人形机器人运行时跌倒监测建模为学习回退策略的随机有限时域可恢复包络，并提出值学习与主动探索交替的统一框架，以提升监测覆盖率和准确性并抑制OOD乐观幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署人形机器人任务策略需要可靠的运行时监测器，以及时预测不可恢复跌倒并触发回退。现有方法假设训练数据自然覆盖可恢复包络，但接触丰富系统的数据获取困难，导致仅恢复真实包络的一部分，并对分布外输入产生乐观幻觉。

Method: 提出统一框架交替进行值学习和主动探索：将数据探索重构为reach-avoid问题，由不确定性引导的探索器系统发现未见状态并避开高置信不可恢复区域；结合Temporal Difference与Monte Carlo目标，实现分布内值预测和可靠不确定性估计；将剩余高不确定性区域显式视为不可恢复。

Result: 在随机基准和29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人上评估，方法显著提升监测器覆盖率和准确性，并成功零样本迁移到真实硬件。

Conclusion: 通过主动探索与不确定性感知的值学习学习可恢复包络，可有效缓解OOD乐观幻觉，为人形机器人提供更可靠的运行时跌倒监测与回退触发能力。

Abstract: Deploying humanoid task policies requires reliable runtime monitors that anticipate unrecoverable falls in time to trigger a fallback. We formulate this task as learning the stochastic, finite-horizon recoverability envelope of a given fallback policy. Synthesizing this monitor is challenging: existing methods assume training data naturally covers the recoverability envelope, yet acquiring such data remains a major bottleneck for contact-rich systems. Consequently, they recover only a fraction of the true envelope, leaving them prone to optimistic hallucinations for out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. We resolve this via a unified framework that iterates between value learning and active exploration. Data exploration is reframed as a reach-avoid problem, where an uncertainty-guided explorer systematically uncovers unseen states while avoiding confidently unrecoverable regions. This loop is further supported by a combined Temporal Difference and Monte Carlo objective for accurate in-distribution value prediction and reliable uncertainty estimation. By explicitly treating the remaining high-uncertainty regions as unrecoverable, the monitor mitigates optimistic OOD hallucinations. Evaluated on stochastic benchmarks and a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid, our approach drastically improves monitor coverage and accuracy and successfully transfers zero-shot to real-world hardware.

</details>


### [126] [Tiny Recursive Policy: Recursive Latent Refinement for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33288)
*Hakan Aktas,Cengiz Oztireli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Tiny Recursive Policy（TRP），一种参数高效的行为克隆策略，将持久时序状态与递归潜在精炼结合，用于机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数行为克隆策略在每个预测步仅从固定观测窗口重建内部表示，执行过程中不维护持久潜在上下文，难以处理长时程和依赖上下文的任务。

Method: TRP在每个控制步使用共享计算模块反复更新高层与低层潜在状态，再解码动作序列，并将得到的潜在状态传播到后续预测；从而在步内进行递归精炼并跨步保持持久状态。

Result: 在LIBERO-100、MetaWorld ML45和LIBERO-Mem上，TRP在多任务与少样本设置中具有竞争力，在长时程和上下文依赖任务上显著提升，且参数量远少于大型生成式基线。执行时扰动实验表明，对持久携带的依赖随时序上下文需求增加；训练时对携带状态加扰动可显著提升对携带扰动和视觉损坏的鲁棒性。消融显示持久状态与递归精炼存在强交互：携带带来的收益强烈依赖步内计算量，性能不能仅由参数量解释。

Conclusion: TRP表明持久时序状态与递归潜在精炼的结合能以较少参数提升行为克隆策略在长时程、上下文依赖及扰动场景下的表现，二者协同作用而非单纯参数规模驱动性能。

Abstract: Behaviour cloning has become a powerful approach for robotic manipulation, but many modern policies reconstruct their internal representation from a fixed observation window at each prediction and do not maintain persistent latent context throughout execution. We introduce Tiny Recursive Policy (TRP), a parameter-efficient behaviour-cloning policy that combines persistent temporal state with recursive latent refinement. At each control step, TRP repeatedly updates high- and low-level latent states using a shared computation module before decoding an action sequence, and propagates the resulting state to subsequent predictions. We evaluate TRP across LIBERO-100, MetaWorld ML45, and the LIBERO-Mem benchmarks. TRP performs competitively across multi-task and few-shot settings and shows substantial gains on long-horizon and context-dependent tasks, while using significantly fewer parameters than large generative baselines. Execution-time perturbations show that reliance on the persistent carry increases with temporal-context demand, while training with carry perturbations substantially improves robustness to both carry and visual corruptions. Ablations reveal a strong interaction between persistent state and recursive refinement: the benefit of carry depends strongly on the amount of within-step computation, and the resulting performance cannot be explained by parameter count alone.

</details>


### [127] [Neuromorphic Decision Dynamics for Deadlock-free Safe Navigation in Congested Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33271)
*Himani Sinhmar,Vaibhav Srivastava,Naomi Ehrich Leonard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化、事件驱动的神经形态决策动力学（NDD）控制器，用于拥挤环境中的安全导航，兼具避碰与协调通行，并给出安全性、活性、鲁棒性和可扩展性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥挤空间导航不仅需要避碰，还需要协调通行；现有方法常依赖通信、优先级、邻居目标/未来动作模型或全局规模估计，难以同时保证安全、无死锁/活锁和可扩展性。

Method: NDD将智能体内部决策与注意状态同物理状态及局部观测到的相对运动耦合；每个智能体沿既定路径连续调节速度，无需通信、预分配优先级或邻居模型。智能体自组织为局部交互组并发解决冲突；当冲突进入安全临界时，决策-动作反馈打破集体犹豫，形成协调的通行顺序。论文推导了传感范围、反馈增益、响应时间尺度和执行器限制等显式条件，以保障有限时间避碰和通行顺序形成，且无死锁或活锁。

Result: 理论分析表明，对有限、异构且规模任意变化的智能体群体，在给定条件下可实现碰撞避免和有限时间通行顺序形成；计算成本随每个智能体感知到的局部交互数量缩放。大规模仿真、与现有方法比较及硬件实验验证了NDD在复杂拥挤场景中的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: NDD将安全且活性的冲突消解转化为局部智能体交互中可控、可局部调节的涌现属性，为无通信、去中心化拥挤环境导航提供了具有理论保证和实验验证的方案。

Abstract: Safe navigation in congested environments requires both collision avoidance and coordinated passage. We introduce neuromorphic decision dynamics (NDD), a decentralized, event-based controller, that provides navigation in congested spaces with guarantees on safety, liveness, robustness, and scalability. NDD makes safe-and-live deconfliction a controllable, locally tunable emergent property of local agent interactions, without requiring communication, preassigned priorities, models of neighbors' objectives or future actions, or estimates of group size or global network properties. By design, NDD couples the dynamics of internal decision and attention states with changing physical states and locally observed relative motion. Each agent continuously modulates its speed along its prescribed path, responding as soon and as much as conflict resolution requires. Agents self-organize into disjoint, locally engaged groups that resolve conflicts concurrently. When any group's conflict becomes safety-critical, decision-action feedback destabilizes collective indecision, and coordinated conflict-resolution emerges that stabilizes a safe traversal order within the group. Order persists as conflicts clear, but groups may split or merge as agents autonomously adapt to new conflicts and those with resolved conflicts resume their nominal motion. We derive explicit conditions on sensing range, feedback gains, response time scales, and actuation limits that guarantee collision avoidance and traversal-order formation in finite time with no deadlock or livelock for finite, heterogeneous agent populations of arbitrary, changing size. Computational cost scales with the number of local interactions sensed by each agent. Large-scale simulations, comparisons with existing approaches, and hardware experiments demonstrate NDD in complex congested scenarios and validate its scalability.

</details>


### [128] [ORBIT: A Modular Multi-finger Robotic Hand Platform with Rotational Finger Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33241)
*Benito Ribadeneira,Siqi Shang,Lillian Chin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化多指手平台ORBIT，通过旋转指节与皮带驱动实现手指布局快速重构，用于系统比较不同手部形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手研究广泛，但缺乏可系统、直接比较不同手部形态的通用平台，难以开展公平的苹果对苹果评估。

Method: ORBIT采用绕圆柱掌的旋转指节机构，独立手指由皮带驱动沿掌周运动，可在不拆解手部的情况下于1秒内重构手指布局；以三指实例开展抓取试验，比较拟人、等距和Barrett式布局对11种不同尺寸、形状和纹理物体的抓取表现。

Result: ORBIT手指平均角速度为1.57 rad/s，指根抗外力为50 N；其非拟人轨道自由度还支持立方体面重定向、拧盖和亚厘米物体精密捏取等固定指节设计难以完成的任务。

Conclusion: ORBIT可作为面向特定操作任务、直接比较多指手形态的可行平台，为手部形态的系统化研究提供基础。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of multi-fingered robotic hands for dexterous manipulation, there is no systematic platform to compare different hand morphologies against one another. We present ORBIT (Orbital Reconfigurable Base-actuated Integrated Theta-motion Hand), a modular platform designed to enable principled exploration of multi-fingered hand morphology. The core contribution of ORBIT is a rotating knuckle mechanism, where independent fingers travel around a cylindrical palm via a belt drive. This enables ORBIT to perform quick recon- figuration (< 1 s) of finger layout without hand disassembly. ORBITs a robust system, with finger average angular velocity of 1.57 rad / s1 and a root finger force resistance of 50 N. Using a three fingers instantiation of ORBIT, we demonstrate an initial pilot grasping study, comparing anthropomorphic, isometric, and Barrett-style finger layouts across eleven objects of varied size, shape, and texture. We further demonstrate how ORBITs nonanthropomorphic orbital DOF enables the achievement of tasks that would be difficult to perform with fixed-knuckle designs, such as cube face reorientation, lid threading, and precision pinching of sub-centimeter objects. These results establish ORBIT as a viable platform for direct apples to apples comparison of multi-fingered hand morphologies for specific manipulation tasks

</details>


### [129] [Causal-based approaches to explain and learn from self-extension - A review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33227)
*Rebeca Marfil,Pablo Bustos,Antonio Bandera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文评述面向自主机器人的自感知、自演化架构，主张机器人需具备深层推理与解释生成能力，并将经人类验证的解释纳入知识库以改进行为与建立信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习赋予机器人强感知能力，自动规划使其能自主完成复杂任务，但机器人常缺乏对感知与行动原因的内部理解，导致性能、可靠性和可信度受限；黑箱模型无法提供行为背后的原因。

Method: 文章分析近期自感知、自演化机器人研究进展，讨论构建基于深层推理的底层架构，使机器人能够生成关于动作、计划失败或感知异常的解释，并在与人类共享和验证后把解释整合进知识库。

Result: 仅靠深度学习无法弥合理解与解释缺口；具备解释能力并将解释反馈到知识库的机器人，可利用这种理解改善未来行为，并增强功能能力与人类信任。

Conclusion: 自主机器人需要从黑箱响应转向可解释的深层推理架构；自感知、自演化机器人通过向人类提供必要解释，有望提升可靠性、可信度和任务能力。

Abstract: The last decades have seen a revolution in autonomous robotics. Deep learning approaches and their hardware implementations have made it possible to endow robots with extraordinary perceptual capabilities. In addition, they can also benefit from advances in Automated Planning, allowing them to autonomously solve complex tasks. However, on many occasions, the robot still acts without internalising and understanding the reasons behind a perception or an action, beyond an immediate response to a current state of the context. This gap results in limitations that affect its performance, reliability and trustworthiness. Deep learning alone cannot bridge this gap, because the reasons behind behaviour, when it emanates from a model in which the world is a black-box, are not accessible. What is really needed is an underlying architecture based on deeper reasoning. Among other issues, this architecture should enable the robot to generate explanations, allowing people to know why the robot is performing, or has performed, a certain action, or the reasons that may have caused a certain plan failure or perceptual anomaly. Furthermore, when these explanations arise from a cognitive process and are shared, and thus validated, with people, the robot should be able to incorporate these explanations into its knowledge base, and thus use this understanding to improve future behaviour. Our article looks at recent advances in the development of self-aware, self-evolving robots. These robots are designed to provide the necessary explanations to their human counterparts, thereby enhancing their functional capabilities, in the quest to gain their trust.

</details>


### [130] [Interpretable Collision Avoidance and Feasibility Guarantees through an Uncertainty Aware Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33186)
*Doris Xu,Jin Hyun Park,Guadalupe Alvarado Fernandez,Adhyan Prasad,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化感知-预测-规划流水线，通过跨模块传播结构化不确定性并构造凸安全集，在动态不确定场景中同时保证碰撞避免与实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器难以同时保证碰撞避免和实时可行性：机会约束方法在不确定性累积时易不可行，确定性规划器可行但忽略不确定性，端到端学习方法缺乏可解释保证。

Method: 将不确定性作为结构化、可解释量在感知、预测、规划模块间传播；在上游解析碰撞概率并构造凸安全集，使规划器每个周期都能给出可行解，从而将安全保证与求解器可行性解耦。

Result: 在四向无信号交叉口蒙特卡洛试验中实现0%碰撞和0%求解不可行，单周期12.7ms；确定性基线和机会约束基线碰撞率分别为36%和10%。消融验证各不确定性来源必要性，Clearpath Jackal UGV部署验证实时性。

Conclusion: 该流水线以可解释的模块化方式在不确定性下同时实现碰撞避免与实时可行规划，并通过仿真和实车部署验证有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) operating in dynamic, challenging conditions such as intersections and roundabouts must guarantee collision avoidance under uncertainty while maintaining real-time feasibility. Most existing planners struggle to simultaneously guarantee both requirements. Chance-constrained formulations embed safety constraints directly in the optimization but become infeasible as uncertainty compounds; deterministic planners provide feasible solutions but fail to address the uncertainty that causes collisions; end-to-end learned policies provide neither guarantee in an interpretable form. We present a modular perception-prediction-planning pipeline that closes this gap by propagating uncertainty as structured, interpretable quantities across module boundaries. Rather than embedding probabilistic risk directly in the optimization, our pipeline resolves collision probabilities upstream and constructs convex safe sets within which the planner provides a guaranteed feasible solution at every cycle -- decoupling the safety guarantee from solver feasibility. Across Monte Carlo trials in a four-way unsignalized intersection, our pipeline achieves 0% collisions and 0% solver infeasibility at 12.7ms per cycle, compared to 36% and 10% collision rates for deterministic and chance-constrained baselines respectively. The ablation study confirms the necessity of each uncertainty source and the hardware deployment on Clearpath Jackal UGVs validates the pipeline's real-time viability on physical platforms.

</details>


### [131] [Bracketing Shows the Way: Adaptive Multi-Exposure Policy as Input for Robust Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33174)
*Olivier Gamache,Anthony Lavertu,David-Alexandre Duclos,Jean-Michel Fortin,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对快速光照变化导致VSLAM图像饱和、自动曝光未及时适应而丢失视觉对应的问题，提出B-Adapt与BVSLAM两级缓解方案，并在隧道出口场景验证其显著降低状态估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速光照跃迁会使图像在AE策略适应前饱和，破坏维持跟踪所需的视觉对应关系，从而干扰VSLAM。

Method: B-Adapt采集自适应曝光包围以覆盖场景动态范围并促进光度重叠；BVSLAM不依赖HDR融合，而是将多曝光序列直接作为因子图状态估计器中的约束。作者在常规环境和模拟车速32 m/s的重复隧道出口过渡中与最先进方法对比评估。

Result: 在隧道场景中，将B-Adapt与BVSLAM结合后，相比AE图像将状态估计误差降低78%；结果表明曝光包围能有效提升视觉位姿估计对快速光照扰动的鲁棒性，同时在常规条件下保持较低误差。

Conclusion: 采用自适应曝光包围并结合多曝光约束的状态估计，可在不进行HDR合并的情况下提升VSLAM对快速光照变化的鲁棒性，并维持常规环境下的低误差表现。

Abstract: Rapid illumination transitions can disturb Visual SLAM (VSLAM) by saturating images before the Automatic Exposure (AE) policy adapts, removing the visual correspondences needed to maintain tracking. Our proposed methods mitigate this disturbance at two levels: B-Adapt acquires adaptive exposure brackets to cover the scene dynamic range while promoting photometric overlap, and BVSLAM directly uses the multi-exposure sequence as constraints in a factor graph state estimator without relying on High Dynamic Range (HDR) merging. We evaluate both methods against state-of-the-art approaches in nominal environments and during a repeated tunnel-exit transition at an emulated vehicle speed of 32 m s-1. In the tunnel, combining our B-Adapt with BVSLAM reduces the state estimation error by 78 % compared with AE images. These results suggest that bracketing efficiently improves visual pose estimation robustness to rapid illumination disturbances while retaining low error under nominal conditions. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Securing Edge & Physical AI: Post-Quantum IoT Blockchain for Mission-Critical Cyber-Physical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33862)
*Kürşat Çakal,Suat Özdemir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“旋转保密性”架构目标，并给出PARS及可选的PARS-ESL，用于分布式CPS与Edge AI中的后量子密钥纪元管理与认证状态协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机使现有公钥认证和密钥建立机制面临Shor算法威胁，Grover算法也降低对称/哈希机制安全裕度；PQC虽可迁移，但更大的公钥、密文和签名带来计算、内存、通信、存储及分片开销，可能制约动态多智能体系统。

Method: 定义rotational secrecy以通过认证的密码纪元切换限制密钥时间暴露；提出PARS参考机制，在授权Physical AI代理间协调策略触发的密钥建立、轮换与对账；进一步设计可选许可型PARS-ESL，由授权验证者或边缘网关维护，仅记录密钥纪元、组成员、撤销、算法套件策略、挑战验证和恢复检查点等认证状态，不存储高频业务交易。

Result: 结果表明，在给定的信任、失陷、连接性和资源假设下，账本辅助的后量子密钥纪元管理对部分Edge AI与CPS安全控制工作负载是可行的。

Conclusion: PARS/PARS-ESL通过将高频低时延业务流量留在账本共识路径之外并由当前对称纪元密钥保护，使资源受限代理可作为轻量客户端参与，同时实现后量子时代的密钥生命周期与安全状态协调；其可行性具有场景和假设条件。

Abstract: Distributed Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) and Edge AI networks increasingly depend on public-key authentication and key-establishment mechanisms that are vulnerable to cryptographically relevant Quantum Computers (QC). Shor's algorithm threatens widely deployed asymmetric primitives, while Grover's algorithm reduces the effective security margins of symmetric and hash-based mechanisms. Although Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) provides a migration path, its larger public keys, ciphertexts, and signatures introduce additional computation, memory, communication, storage, and packet-fragmentation costs that can constrain dynamic multi-agent systems. In this article, we introduce rotational secrecy, an architectural objective that limits temporal key exposure through authenticated transitions between cryptographic epochs. We present Private Authentication for Rotational Secrecy (PARS), a reference mechanism that coordinates policy-triggered key establishment, rotation, and reconciliation among authorized Physical AI agents. For deployments that need distributed agreement on security state, we further develop the PARS-Enabled Edge Security Ledger (PARS-ESL), an optional permissioned architecture maintained by authorized validators or capable edge gateways. PARS-ESL does not store high-volume operational transactions. Instead, it records authenticated state for key epochs, group membership, revocation, algorithm-suite policies, challenge validation, and recovery checkpoints. As a result, resource-constrained agents can take part as lightweight clients, and low-latency operational traffic stays outside the ledger consensus path, protected by the currently active symmetric epoch keys. Our results show that ledger-assisted post-quantum key-epoch management is feasible for selected Edge AI and CPS security-control workloads under the stated trust, compromise, connectivity, and resource assumptions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Using Routed Ensembles and Trajectory Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35733)
*Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多目标强化学习中目标冲突导致偏好相关策略学习样本效率低的问题，提出冲突系数、路由集成策略学习与核代理轨迹归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习需平衡如安全对齐中的有用性与诚实性、机器人中的速度与能耗等竞争目标；最优策略会随目标权重组合剧烈变化，且变化程度取决于目标冲突程度，导致逐偏好学习策略样本效率低。

Method: 作者引入目标冲突系数并推导在线设置下接近最优的 regret 界，以量化目标错配/冲突对学习效率的影响；提出路由集成策略学习，维护少量基策略并将轨迹动态路由到潜在奖励最高的策略；同时将轨迹选择视为缓解目标冲突的途径，设计核代理轨迹归因算法，估计一组轨迹的反馈信号如何影响其他轨迹上的策略学习，核可捕获轨迹间协同与拮抗等非线性交互。

Result: 在 Meta-World 和 MO-Gymnasium 在线环境中，路由集成较基线平均性能提升 4.3%；核代理归因与留一分析的相关性较现有基线提升 50%；用于轨迹选择时，基于核的归因较基线归因方法奖励提升 27%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式量化目标冲突并结合路由集成与核代理轨迹归因，可有效缓解多目标冲突带来的学习低效，并提升在线多目标策略学习与轨迹选择性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning is a fundamental problem for modern AI, with applications spanning post-training, reasoning models, and robotic control. A central challenge is to learn policies that balance competing objectives, such as helpfulness versus honesty in safety and alignment, or speed versus energy in robotics. The optimal policy can vary sharply with objective-weighting combinations, and the extent of this variation depends crucially on the degree of conflict among objectives, making per-preference policy learning sample-inefficient. In this paper, we introduce objective-conflict coefficients and derive near-optimal regret bounds for online settings using this measure, thereby quantifying the impact of misalignment on learning efficiency. Then, we design routed ensemble policy learning, an algorithm that maintains a small set of basis policies and dynamically routes trajectories to the policy with the highest potential reward. Finally, we consider trajectory selection as another approach to mitigate objective conflict, and design a kernel surrogate trajectory attribution algorithm that estimates how feedback signals from a set of trajectories influence policy learning on other trajectories. The kernel can capture nonlinear interactions such as synergy and antagonism between trajectories. We evaluate our approach across diverse environments and benchmarks: routed ensembles improve average performance by 4.3% over baselines for online environments from Meta-World and MO-Gymnasium, and kernel surrogate attribution improves correlation with leave-one-out analysis over existing baselines by 50%. When applied to trajectory selection, kernel-based attribution yields a 27% improvement in reward over baseline attribution methods.

</details>


### [134] [Adapt for Planning, Not just Prediction: Optimistic Test-Time World Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35636)
*Akshay Mete,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OpTTA：在测试时用“预测损失 + 规划器目标到达代价”联合更新潜世界模型，使适应偏向对达成目标乐观的模型，从而提升分布偏移下的规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常在离线预训练后冻结，测试时遇到分布偏移会退化；而规划型 TTA 是闭环的，仅用预测损失只拟合自身动作结果，可能对能达目标的动作仍然判断错误，低预测误差不一定带来更好规划。因此需要明确 TTA 应使用什么目标。

Method: 受自适应控制中 reward-biased maximum likelihood 启发，OpTTA 的目标包含两项：观测转移上的预测损失，以及规划器在其候选 rollout 上的目标到达代价；两项均对模型参数最小化，不增加环境交互。规划项使适应偏向对达到目标乐观的模型。

Result: 在操作、到达和导航任务的视觉与环境偏移下，仅适应 predictor 时，OpTTA 相对冻结模型的最终规划成功率平均提升 13.5 个百分点，预测-only 的 AdaJEPA 为 6.9；同时适应 encoder 时分别为 13.5 和 7.5。实验覆盖三种不同 encoder 与训练目标的 JEPA 世界模型，并在 temporal straightening 中使用 CEM 与梯度规划器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向规划的世界模型测试时适应不仅需要预测损失，还应加入乐观的、任务感知的规划项，以更有效提升分布偏移下的规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically pre-trained offline and kept frozen at test time, so planning with them can degrade under distribution shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this degradation by updating the model during deployment. Unlike pre-training on fixed data, TTA for planning is closed-loop: the planner uses model rollouts to select actions, and the resulting transitions drive the next update. This raises the question: which objective should guide TTA for planning? Under a prediction-only objective, the model is fit only to the outcomes of its own actions, so it can remain wrong about the actions that would reach the goal, and low prediction error need not translate into better planning. Inspired by reward-biased maximum likelihood from adaptive control, we introduce Optimistic Test-Time Adaptation (OpTTA). Its objective has two terms, the prediction loss on observed transitions and the planner's goal-reaching cost on its candidate rollouts, both minimized over the model parameters. The planning term biases adaptation toward models that are optimistic about reaching the goal, without any additional environment interaction. Across visual and environment shifts on manipulation, reaching and navigation tasks, OpTTA raises final planning success over the frozen model by 13.5 percentage points on average, compared with 6.9 for the prediction-only objective (AdaJEPA), when only the predictor is adapted. When the encoder is adapted as well, the gains are 13.5 and 7.5 points. These experiments span three JEPA world models with different encoders and training objectives and, for temporal straightening, both CEM and gradient-based planners. Our results suggest that test-time adaptation of world models for planning benefits from an optimistic, task-aware term in addition to the prediction loss.

</details>


### [135] [High-resolution tactile observation makes hidden racket-control mechanisms learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35563)
*Haoyang Tian,Zhengwei Li,Houcheng Li,Xu Yang,Peigeng Gu,Xinyue Wang,Tairan Zhu,Shuo Jiang,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用高密度触觉-惯性手套和力学 grounded 两层框架，从羽毛球拍控制中推断手-物操控的驱动与稳定机制，并将机制转化为个性化指导，显著提升学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家能掌握精细的手-物操控技能，但对快速触觉交互的有意识感知有限，难以说清其控制机制；羽毛球拍控制需要快速精细的手部力协调，是揭示这些机制的理想测试场景。

Method: 使用864节点触觉-惯性手套、100 Hz同步采集，记录70余名参与者在五个技能水平上的14000余次击球；受感觉运动控制启发，提出基于力学的两层框架，从触觉历史推断物体驱动与握持稳定控制，并解析其时空力实现；进一步将机制转化为针对力控制和力-动作协调的个性化指导，并在随机队列中验证。

Result: 更高熟练度与选择性而非广泛性控制相关：技能特异性更强、预期性调节、局部远端调制、较低几何力矩抵消；时空协调呈非单调的“获得-精炼”模式。机制指导学习者获得0.72个专家评定技能等级提升，而仅水平反馈组为0.05。

Conclusion: 手-物操控的控制机制可被推断并转化为可操作的个性化指导，机制引导反馈能显著提升技能学习效果。

Abstract: Experts can master fine hand-object manipulation skills, yet limited conscious perception of rapid tactile interactions hinders their ability to articulate the underlying control mechanisms. Badminton racket control requires rapid, fine hand-force coordination, providing a demanding testbed for uncovering these mechanisms. An 864-node tactile-inertial glove with synchronized 100-Hz acquisition recorded over 14,000 strokes from more than 70 participants across five skills. Inspired by sensorimotor control, our mechanics-grounded two-layer framework inferred object-driving and grip-stabilizing control from tactile history, while resolving their spatiotemporal force realization. We found that higher proficiency was associated with selective rather than extensive control: greater skill specificity, anticipatory regulation, focal distal modulation and lower geometric torque cancellation, while spatiotemporal coordination showed a non-monotonic acquire-refine pattern. We translated these mechanisms into personalized guidance targeting force control and force-motion coordination. In a randomized cohort, mechanism-guided learners gained 0.72 expert-rated skill levels versus 0.05 with level-only feedback, making control mechanisms actionable.

</details>


### [136] [Video World Models Need Predictable Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34663)
*Mattia Scardecchia,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在视频世界模型的潜空间中同时兼顾未来预测可预测性与像素级观测重建精度，通过时间目标与过去条件解码器提升长时程和分布外泛化，并显著降低训练与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视频世界模型对策略学习与评估有潜力，但现有方法在长时程推演、分布外泛化和推理效率上仍受限；重建训练的自编码器潜空间未针对动力学建模优化，而面向潜空间规划的世界模型又牺牲了像素空间观测预测精度。

Method: 论文将表示学习的时间目标与过去条件解码器架构结合，使潜空间几何更适合未来预测，同时保持对未来观测的准确预测，并将该设计融入自编码器训练。

Result: 在相同训练预算下，方法实现最高5倍推理成本降低、超过7倍训练加速，并在分布外和长时程设置下将像素与感知误差降低15%–24%；使用视觉语言模型从像素预测判断任务成功时，其评估结果与真实策略评估更一致。

Conclusion: 时间可预测性应作为视频世界模型潜空间的重要设计准则；论文给出了将时间预测目标纳入自编码器训练的具体指导，并表明可同时获得高效动力学建模与准确观测预测。

Abstract: Video world models trained on large-scale robot data promise a safe and scalable approach for learning and evaluating policies that act in the physical world. However, they still struggle with long-horizon rollouts and out-of-distribution generalization, and they remain computationally demanding at inference time. Typically, video world models make predictions in the latent space of a reconstruction-trained autoencoder, which however is not directly optimized for dynamics modeling. On the other hand, world models designed for latent planning usually optimize latents to be predictable, but they forgo accurate prediction of observations in pixel space. In this paper, we show that it is possible to achieve the best of both worlds: a latent geometry optimized to make future prediction as simple as possible without sacrificing accurate prediction of future observations. More precisely, we find that temporal objectives for representation learning, paired with a past-conditioned decoder architecture, lead to consistent improvements in generative video world models at the same training budget. The gains include up to 5x cheaper inference, over 7x faster training, and 15-24% lower pixel and perceptual errors out of distribution and over long horizons. Our world models also agree more closely with real-world policy evaluation when using a vision-language model to judge task success from their pixel-space predictions. Overall, our study indicates that temporal predictability is an important design criterion for the latent space of video world models, and provides concrete guidance on how to incorporate it into the training of autoencoders.

</details>


### [137] [Execution Logs Sway VLM Judges of Robot Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34151)
*Weiyan Zhao,Sunny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型在评估机器人视频时，画面旁的一行文本就能显著改变其判定；其中，伪造的“有进展”日志尤其容易让模型把失败误判为进展，但一句警告可大幅削弱这种影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型被用于机器人视频评分，其评判可靠性至关重要；作者关注画面旁文本是否会影响模型判定，即是否存在可被利用的文本侧信道偏差。

Method: 在机器人正在失去进展的片段中，加入一行声称“机器人在取得进展”的日志，并与中性日志对比；在Gemini、Qwen和GPT三类评审模型上测试判定翻转率。进一步测试加入“日志可能有误”的一行警告是否缓解影响，并比较伪造“任务完成”声明与无关日志行的效果。

Result: 伪造“有进展”日志使正确判定翻转为“有进展”的比例，比中性日志高10至35个百分点；一行警告可消除大部分影响。伪造“任务完成”声明可翻转Qwen约一半的正确判定，而无关日志行仅约十分之一；对Gemini未检测到显著影响。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型作为机器人视频评审时易受画面旁文本误导，且脆弱性因模型而异；简单警告提示能有效缓解此类文本诱导偏差，但模型鲁棒性仍需系统评估。

Abstract: Vision-language models now grade robot videos, and text placed beside the frames can change their verdicts. On clips where the robot is losing progress, we add one log line saying the robot is making progress. Across Gemini, Qwen and GPT judges, this flips correct verdicts to progress 10 to 35 percentage points more often than a neutral line does. A one-line warning that the log may be wrong removes most of this effect. Selected fake "task complete" claims flip half of Qwen's correct verdicts, against a tenth for unrelated log lines. They have no detectable effect on Gemini.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [EAST: Embodied AI Safety Testing via Systematic Fault Injection in Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34358)
*Yuhao Tian,Tao Li,Ling Zhou,Dave Towey,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Rubing Huang,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 EAST 数字孪生安全测试框架，将文献中的安全证据转化为跨感知、指令、环境、动作和时序的故障注入测试，并在 RoboTwin 任务上验证其能暴露被任务成功掩盖的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务成功不足以衡量具身 AI 安全性，因为机器人可能在完成目标的同时违反安全要求；现有评估偏重性能和鲁棒性，缺乏从已记录安全危害到可执行、可验证测试的系统化路径。

Method: EAST 结合专家审核的安全故障分类体系和八个可执行算子，并施加适用性与有效性约束；采用三阶段流程生成测试规范、在仿真执行中验证故障实现，并基于仿真器状态证据独立评估安全挑战、违规、任务结果和行为特征，同时保留执行轨迹与可回放记录以支持归因和可复现性。

Result: 在六个 RoboTwin 操作任务及 ACT、RDT 两种策略架构上，与替代测试策略在匹配预算下进行五轮比较；EAST 覆盖全部 74 个可接受的任务-策略-算子组合并为 63 个获得有效证据，安全挑战暴露率接近 Random-Legal 和 Rule-Based；此外，82 次有效 EAST 执行在发生安全违规的同时完成了任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅以任务完成度评估会掩盖安全风险；需要面向有效性、系统化且可复现的安全测试，超越任务完成指标。

Abstract: Task success is not a sufficient measure of safety for embodied artificial intelligence (AI), because robots may complete objectives while violating safety requirements. Existing evaluations often emphasize performance and robustness, but lack a systematic route from documented safety hazards to executable, verifiable tests. We present EAST (Embodied AI Safety Testing), a digital-twin framework that converts literature-grounded safety evidence into fault-injection tests across perception, instructions, environments, actions, and timing. EAST combines an expert-reviewed safety-fault taxonomy and eight executable operators with applicability and validity constraints. Its three-stage workflow generates test specifications, verifies fault realization during simulated execution, and independently assesses safety challenges, violations, task outcomes, and behavioral signatures using simulator-state evidence. The framework preserves execution traces and replayable records, supporting attribution and reproducibility across experiments. We evaluate EAST on six RoboTwin manipulation tasks and two policy architectures, ACT and RDT, against alternative testing strategies under matched budgets. Across five comparative campaigns, EAST targets all 74 admissible task-policy-operator combinations and obtains valid evidence for 63, while achieving safety-challenge exposure rates close to Random-Legal and Rule-Based. Moreover, 82 valid EAST executions complete their tasks despite safety violations, revealing risks concealed by success-oriented evaluation. These findings support systematic, validity-aware, reproducible safety testing beyond task-completion metrics.

</details>
